// src/main/main.cpp
#include "app/EditorExtension.h"
#include "core/Application.h"

int main() {
  engine::Application app(1920, 1080, "Sim");

  app.AddExtension(std::make_unique<EditorExtension>()); // ← редактор
  // app.AddExtension(std::make_unique<PerfOverlayExtension>()); // ← потом
  // добавишь любые

  app.Run();
  app.Shutdown();
  return 0;
}



===== src/configs/WindConfig.h =====
#pragma once
#include <glm/glm.hpp>
struct WindConfig {
    glm::vec3 direction = glm::vec3(1.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f); // Направление ветра
    float strength = 10.0f;                              // Сила ветра (0-1)
    float gustiness = 0.3f;                             // Порывистость
    float change_speed = 0.1f;                          // Скорость изменения
    
    // Метод для получения текущего ветра с шумом
    glm::vec3 getCurrentWind(float time) const {
        glm::vec3 base_wind = glm::normalize(direction) * strength;
        
        float noise_x = sin(time * change_speed * 2.0f) * gustiness;
        float noise_z = cos(time * change_speed * 1.7f) * gustiness;
        
        return base_wind + glm::vec3(noise_x, 0.0f, noise_z);
    }
};


===== src/configs/FireParams.h =====
#pragma once
#include <glm/glm.hpp>
#include <string>
struct FireParams {
    // ТЕКСТУРЫ ДЛЯ КАЖДОГО ЭФФЕКТА
    std::string fireTexturePath = "res/textures/fire_texture3.png";
    std::string smokeTexturePath = "res/textures/smoke_texture.png";
    std::string sparksTexturePath = "res/textures/sparks_texture.png";
    std::string ashTexturePath = "res/textures/ash_texture.png";
    std::string heatTexturePath = "res/textures/heat_texture.png";
    
    struct EffectParams {
        bool enabled = true;
        float particleSize = 2.0f;
        float sizeVariation = 0.5f;
        float animationSpeed = 1.0f;
        float lifetime = 3.0f;
        float spawnRate = 100.0f;
        float riseSpeed = 2.0f;
        float turbulence = 0.5f;
        float windInfluence = 1.0f;
        float spread = 1.0f;

        // ЦВЕТА
        glm::vec3 baseColor = {1.0f, 0.3f, 0.1f};
        glm::vec3 endColor = {1.0f, 0.8f, 0.2f};

        float redIntensity = 1.0f;
        float yellowReduction = 0.3f;
        float alphaMultiplier = 1.0f;
        float textureInfluence = 0.7f;
        float colorVariation = 0.0f;
    };
    
    EffectParams fire;
    EffectParams smoke;
    EffectParams sparks;
    EffectParams ash;
    EffectParams heat;
};


===== src/graphics/ShaderStorageBuffer.h =====
#pragma once
#include <cstdint>
#include <glad/glad.h>
#include <memory>
namespace engine {

class ShaderStorageBuffer {
public:
  static std::shared_ptr<ShaderStorageBuffer>
  Create(uint32_t size, const void *data = nullptr,
         uint32_t usage = GL_DYNAMIC_DRAW);
  ~ShaderStorageBuffer();
  ShaderStorageBuffer() = default;

  void Bind() const;
  void Bind(uint32_t binding) const;
  void Unbind() const;

  void SetData(const void *data, uint32_t size, uint32_t offset = 0);
  void GetData(void *data, uint32_t size, uint32_t offset = 0);

  void *Map(uint32_t access = GL_READ_WRITE);
  void Unmap();

  uint32_t GetID() const { return m_rendererID; }
  uint32_t GetSize() const { return m_size; }

private:
  uint32_t m_rendererID = 0;
  uint32_t m_size = 0;
};

} // namespace engine


===== src/graphics/VertexBuffer.h =====
#pragma once
#include <cstdint>
#include <glad/glad.h>
#include <memory>
#include <string>
#include <vector>
namespace engine {
enum class ShaderDataType {
  Float,
  Float2,
  Float3,
  Float4,
  Int,
  Int2,
  Int3,
  Int4,
  Mat3,
  Mat4,
  Bool
};
static uint32_t ShaderDataTypeSize(ShaderDataType type) {
  switch (type) {
  case ShaderDataType::Float:
    return 4;
  case ShaderDataType::Float2:
    return 4 * 2;
  case ShaderDataType::Float3:
    return 4 * 3;
  case ShaderDataType::Float4:
    return 4 * 4;
  case ShaderDataType::Mat3:
    return 4 * 3 * 3;
  case ShaderDataType::Mat4:
    return 4 * 4 * 4;
  case ShaderDataType::Int:
    return 4;
  case ShaderDataType::Int2:
    return 4 * 2;
  case ShaderDataType::Int3:
    return 4 * 3;
  case ShaderDataType::Int4:
    return 4 * 4;
  case ShaderDataType::Bool:
    return 1;
  }
  return 0;
}
struct BufferElement {
  ShaderDataType type;
  std::string name;
  uint32_t size;
  uint32_t offset;
  bool normalized;
  BufferElement(ShaderDataType type, const std::string &name,
                bool normalized = false)
      : name(name), type(type), size(ShaderDataTypeSize(type)), offset(0),
        normalized(normalized) {}
  uint32_t GetComponentCount() const {
    switch (type) {
    case ShaderDataType::Float:
      return 1;
    case ShaderDataType::Float2:
      return 2;
    case ShaderDataType::Float3:
      return 3;
    case ShaderDataType::Float4:
      return 4;
    case ShaderDataType::Int:
      return 1;
    case ShaderDataType::Int2:
      return 2;
    case ShaderDataType::Int3:
      return 3;
    case ShaderDataType::Int4:
      return 4;
    case ShaderDataType::Mat3:
      return 3;
    case ShaderDataType::Mat4:
      return 4;
    case ShaderDataType::Bool:
      return 1;
    }
    return 0;
  }
};

class BufferLayout {
private:
  std::vector<BufferElement> m_elements;
  uint32_t m_Stride = 0;

public:
  BufferLayout() = default;
  BufferLayout(const std::initializer_list<BufferElement> &elements)
      : m_elements(elements) {
    CalculateOffsetsAndStride();
  }
  const std::vector<BufferElement> &GetElements() const { return m_elements; }
  uint32_t GetStride() const { return m_Stride; }
  // Итераторы для for-each
  std::vector<BufferElement>::iterator begin() { return m_elements.begin(); }
  std::vector<BufferElement>::iterator end() { return m_elements.end(); }
  std::vector<BufferElement>::const_iterator begin() const {
    return m_elements.begin();
  }
  std::vector<BufferElement>::const_iterator end() const {
    return m_elements.end();
  }

private:
  void CalculateOffsetsAndStride() {
    size_t offset = 0;
    m_Stride = 0;
    for (auto &element : m_elements) {
      element.offset = offset;
      offset += element.size;
      m_Stride += element.size;
    }
  }
};

class VertexBuffer {
private:
  GLuint m_id;
public:
  virtual ~VertexBuffer() = default;

  virtual void Bind() const = 0;
  virtual void Unbind() const = 0;
  virtual void SetData(const void *data, uint32_t size) = 0;
  virtual void SetLayout(const BufferLayout &layout) = 0;
  virtual size_t GetSize() const = 0;
  virtual const BufferLayout &GetLayout() const = 0;
  static std::shared_ptr<VertexBuffer> Create(int32_t size);
  static std::shared_ptr<VertexBuffer> Create(float *vertices, int32_t size);
};

} // namespace engine


===== src/graphics/Renderer.cc =====
#include "Renderer.h"
#include "graphics/Texture.h"
#include "graphics/VertexArrayObject.h"
#include <exception>
#include <glad/glad.h>
#include <glm/glm.hpp>
#include <glm/gtc/matrix_transform.hpp>
#include <iostream>
#include <memory>
static float vertices[] = {
    // front face
    -0.5f, -0.5f, 0.5f, 0.5f, -0.5f, 0.5f, 0.5f, 0.5f, 0.5f, -0.5f, 0.5f, 0.5f,

    // back face
    -0.5f, -0.5f, -0.5f, 0.5f, -0.5f, -0.5f, 0.5f, 0.5f, -0.5f, -0.5f, 0.5f,
    -0.5f};
static unsigned int indices[] = {
    // Front face
    0, 1, 2, 2, 3, 0,

    // Back face
    4, 5, 6, 6, 7, 4,

    // Left face
    0, 3, 7, 7, 4, 0,

    // Right face
    1, 5, 6, 6, 2, 1,

    // Top face
    3, 2, 6, 6, 7, 3,

    // Bottom face
    0, 1, 5, 5, 4, 0};
namespace engine {
void Renderer::Init() {
  m_shader = Shader::Create("res/shaders/shader.sh");
  m_modelShader = Shader::Create("res/shaders/model_shader.sh");
  m_fireGeometryShader = Shader::Create("res/shaders/fire_geom.sh");
  m_instanceModelShader =
      Shader::Create("res/shaders/instance_model_shader.sh");
  m_terrainShader = Shader::Create("res/shaders/terrain.sh");

  m_vao = VertexArrayObject::Create();

  m_vbo = VertexBuffer::Create(vertices, sizeof(vertices));
  m_vbo->SetLayout({{ShaderDataType::Float3, "a_position"}});
  m_vao->AddVertexBuffer(m_vbo);

  m_ebo = IndexBuffer::Create(indices, sizeof(indices) / sizeof(unsigned int));
  m_vao->SetIndexBuffer(m_ebo);

  m_agentVAO = VertexArrayObject::Create();
  m_agentVBO = VertexBuffer::Create(50000 * sizeof(AgentRenderData));
  m_agentVBO->SetLayout({{ShaderDataType::Float3, "a_position"},
                         {ShaderDataType::Float, "a_intensity"},
                         {ShaderDataType::Float3, "a_bounds_min"},
                         {ShaderDataType::Float3, "a_bounds_max"}});
  m_agentVAO->AddVertexBuffer(m_agentVBO);

  m_universalParticleShader = Shader::Create("res/shaders/fire_geom.sh");
  
  m_particleVAO = VertexArrayObject::Create();
  m_particleVBO = VertexBuffer::Create(100000 * sizeof(UniversalParticleData));
  m_particleVBO->SetLayout({
      {ShaderDataType::Float3, "a_position"},
      {ShaderDataType::Float, "a_intensity"},
      {ShaderDataType::Float3, "a_bounds_min"},
      {ShaderDataType::Float3, "a_bounds_max"},
      {ShaderDataType::Float, "a_effect_type"}
  });
  m_particleVAO->AddVertexBuffer(m_particleVBO);
  SetFireParams({});
}

void Renderer::Shutdown() {
  m_vao.reset();
  m_vbo.reset(); 
  m_ebo.reset();
  m_agentVAO.reset();
  m_agentVBO.reset();
  m_instanceVAO.reset();
  m_particleVAO.reset();
  m_particleVBO.reset();
  
  m_shader.reset();
  m_modelShader.reset();
  m_fireGeometryShader.reset();
  m_instanceModelShader.reset();
  m_terrainShader.reset();
  m_universalParticleShader.reset();
  
  m_fireTexture.reset();
  m_smokeTexture.reset();
  m_sparksTexture.reset();
  m_ashTexture.reset();
  m_heatTexture.reset();
}

void Renderer::DrawCube(const glm::vec3 &position, const glm::vec3 &color,
                        const glm::vec3 &size) {
  glm::mat4 model = glm::mat4(1.0f);
  model = glm::translate(model, position);
  model = glm::scale(model, size);
  m_shader->Bind();

  m_shader->SetMat4("u_model", model);
  m_shader->SetVec3("u_color", color);
  m_shader->SetMat4("u_view", m_viewMat);
  m_shader->SetMat4("u_projection", m_projectionMat);
  m_vao->Bind();
  glDrawElements(GL_TRIANGLES, 36, GL_UNSIGNED_INT, 0);
  m_vao->Unbind();
  m_shader->Unbind();
}

void Renderer::DrawModel(Model &model, const glm::vec3 &position,
                         const glm::vec3 &scale, const glm::vec3 &camPos) {
  glm::mat4 modelmat = glm::mat4(1.0f);
  modelmat = glm::translate(modelmat, position);
  modelmat = glm::scale(modelmat, scale);
  m_modelShader->Bind();
  m_modelShader->SetMat4("u_model", modelmat);
  m_modelShader->SetMat4("u_view", m_viewMat);
  m_modelShader->SetMat4("u_projection", m_projectionMat);
  m_modelShader->SetVec3("viewPos", camPos);
  m_modelShader->SetVec3("light.position", glm::vec3(500.0f, 200.0f, 100.0f));
  m_modelShader->SetVec3("light.ambient", glm::vec3(0.2f));
  m_modelShader->SetVec3("light.diffuse", glm::vec3(0.8f));
  m_modelShader->SetVec3("light.specular", glm::vec3(1.0f));
  model.Draw(*m_modelShader);
}
void Renderer::DrawModelInstanced(Model &model,
                                  const std::vector<glm::mat4> &instances,
                                  const glm::vec3 &camPos,  std::shared_ptr<Material> material) {
  if (instances.empty())
    return;

  m_instanceModelShader->Bind();
  m_instanceModelShader->SetMat4("u_view", m_viewMat);
  m_instanceModelShader->SetMat4("u_projection", m_projectionMat);
  m_instanceModelShader->SetVec3("viewPos", camPos);
  m_instanceModelShader->SetVec3("light.position",
                                 glm::vec3(500.0f, 200.0f, 100.0f));
  m_instanceModelShader->SetVec3("light.ambient", glm::vec3(0.2f));
  m_instanceModelShader->SetVec3("light.diffuse", glm::vec3(0.8f));
  m_instanceModelShader->SetVec3("light.specular", glm::vec3(1.0f));
  if (material) {
    material->Bind(*m_instanceModelShader);
  } else {
    m_instanceModelShader->SetBool("material.use_diffuse_map", false);
    m_instanceModelShader->SetBool("material.use_specular_map", false);
    m_instanceModelShader->SetVec3("material.diffuse", glm::vec3(0.8f));
    m_instanceModelShader->SetVec3("material.specular", glm::vec3(0.5f));
    m_instanceModelShader->SetFloat("material.shininess", 32.0f);
  }

  model.DrawInstanced(*m_instanceModelShader, instances);
  glBindBuffer(GL_ARRAY_BUFFER, 0);
  glBindVertexArray(0);
  m_instanceModelShader->Unbind();

}

void Renderer::DrawModelWithMaterial(
    Model &model, const glm::vec3 &position, const glm::vec3 &scale,
    const glm::vec3 &camPos, const std::shared_ptr<Material> &material) {

  // 1. Вычисляем матрицу модели
  glm::mat4 modelmat = glm::mat4(1.0f);
  modelmat = glm::translate(modelmat, position);
  modelmat = glm::scale(modelmat, scale);

  m_modelShader->Bind();

  m_modelShader->SetMat4("u_model", modelmat);
  m_modelShader->SetMat4("u_view", m_viewMat);
  m_modelShader->SetMat4("u_projection", m_projectionMat);
  m_modelShader->SetVec3("viewPos", camPos);

  // 3. Настройка освещения (как у тебя было)
  m_modelShader->SetVec3("light.position", glm::vec3(500.0f, 200.0f, 100.0f));
  m_modelShader->SetVec3("light.ambient", glm::vec3(0.2f));
  m_modelShader->SetVec3("light.diffuse", glm::vec3(0.8f));
  m_modelShader->SetVec3("light.specular", glm::vec3(1.0f));

  if (material) {
    material->Bind(*m_modelShader);
  } else {
    m_modelShader->SetVec3("material.diffuse", glm::vec3(0.8f));
    m_modelShader->SetVec3("material.specular", glm::vec3(0.5f));
    m_modelShader->SetFloat("material.shininess", 32.0f);
  }

  model.DrawGeometryOnly(*m_modelShader);

}

void Renderer::BeginScene(const glm::mat4 &view, const glm::mat4 &projection) {
  glClearColor(0.1, 0.1, 0.1, 1.0f);

  glClear(GL_COLOR_BUFFER_BIT | GL_DEPTH_BUFFER_BIT);
  glEnable(GL_DEPTH_TEST);
  glEnable(GL_CULL_FACE);
  glCullFace(GL_BACK);
  glFrontFace(GL_CCW);
  m_viewMat = view;
  m_projectionMat = projection;
}
void Renderer::UploadAgentsData(const std::vector<Agent> &agents) {
  m_particleData.clear();
    
    for (const auto &agent : agents) {
        if (!agent.isBurning()) continue;
        
        float intensity = agent.getOverallIntensity();
        
        // ОГОНЬ (всегда)
        if (m_fireParams.fire.enabled) {
            m_particleData.push_back({
                glm::vec3(agent.position.x, agent.position.y, agent.position.z),
                intensity,
                glm::vec3(agent.fire_collider.bounds_min.x, agent.fire_collider.bounds_min.y, agent.fire_collider.bounds_min.z),
                glm::vec3(agent.fire_collider.bounds_max.x, agent.fire_collider.bounds_max.y, agent.fire_collider.bounds_max.z),
                0.0f // fire
            });
        }
            if (m_fireParams.smoke.enabled && intensity > 0.3f) {
            m_particleData.push_back({
                glm::vec3(agent.position.x, agent.position.y, agent.position.z),
                intensity * 0.7f,
                glm::vec3(agent.fire_collider.bounds_min.x, agent.fire_collider.bounds_min.y, agent.fire_collider.bounds_min.z),
                glm::vec3(agent.fire_collider.bounds_max.x, agent.fire_collider.bounds_max.y, agent.fire_collider.bounds_max.z),
                1.0f // smoke
            });
        }
        
        // ИСКРЫ (при высокой интенсивности)
        if (m_fireParams.sparks.enabled && intensity > 0.7f) {
            m_particleData.push_back({
                glm::vec3(agent.position.x, agent.position.y, agent.position.z),
                intensity * 0.5f,
                glm::vec3(agent.fire_collider.bounds_min.x, agent.fire_collider.bounds_min.y, agent.fire_collider.bounds_min.z),
                glm::vec3(agent.fire_collider.bounds_max.x, agent.fire_collider.bounds_max.y, agent.fire_collider.bounds_max.z),
                2.0f // sparks
            });
        }
          if (m_fireParams.ash.enabled) {
            m_particleData.push_back({
                glm::vec3(agent.position.x, agent.position.y, agent.position.z),
                intensity * 0.3f,
                glm::vec3(agent.fire_collider.bounds_min.x, agent.fire_collider.bounds_min.y, agent.fire_collider.bounds_min.z),
                glm::vec3(agent.fire_collider.bounds_max.x, agent.fire_collider.bounds_max.y, agent.fire_collider.bounds_max.z),
                3.0f // ash
            });
        }
        
        // ТЕПЛО (при любой интенсивности)
        if (m_fireParams.heat.enabled) {
            m_particleData.push_back({
                glm::vec3(agent.position.x, agent.position.y, agent.position.z),
                intensity * 0.2f,
                glm::vec3(agent.fire_collider.bounds_min.x, agent.fire_collider.bounds_min.y, agent.fire_collider.bounds_min.z),
                glm::vec3(agent.fire_collider.bounds_max.x, agent.fire_collider.bounds_max.y, agent.fire_collider.bounds_max.z),
                4.0f // heat
            });
        }
    }
    
    // ОДИН раз загружаем все данные
    if (!m_particleData.empty()) {
        m_particleVBO->Bind();
        m_particleVBO->SetData(m_particleData.data(), m_particleData.size() * sizeof(UniversalParticleData));
    }
}

void Renderer::EndScene() {
  glBindVertexArray(0);
  glUseProgram(0);
}

void Renderer::RenderWithGeometryShader(const glm::vec3 &wind_force) {
   if (m_particleData.empty()) return;
    
    glEnable(GL_BLEND);
    glBlendFunc(GL_SRC_ALPHA, GL_ONE);

    glDepthMask(GL_FALSE);

    m_universalParticleShader->Bind();
    
    // Общие uniform'ы
    m_universalParticleShader->SetMat4("u_view", m_viewMat);
    m_universalParticleShader->SetMat4("u_projection", m_projectionMat);
    m_universalParticleShader->SetFloat("u_time", glfwGetTime());
    m_universalParticleShader->SetVec3("u_wind_force", wind_force);
    
    // Камерные векторы
    glm::mat4 view = m_viewMat;
    glm::vec3 camera_right = glm::vec3(view[0][0], view[1][0], view[2][0]);
    glm::vec3 camera_up = glm::vec3(view[0][1], view[1][1], view[2][1]);
    m_universalParticleShader->SetVec3("u_camera_right", camera_right);
    m_universalParticleShader->SetVec3("u_camera_up", camera_up);
    
    // БИНДИМ ТЕКСТУРЫ (КОТОРЫЕ УЖЕ ЗАГРУЖЕНЫ В SetFireParams)
    m_fireTexture->Bind(0);
    m_smokeTexture->Bind(1);
    m_sparksTexture->Bind(2);
    m_ashTexture->Bind(3);
    m_heatTexture->Bind(4);
    
    m_universalParticleShader->SetInt("u_fire_texture", 0);
    m_universalParticleShader->SetInt("u_smoke_texture", 1);
    m_universalParticleShader->SetInt("u_sparks_texture", 2);
    m_universalParticleShader->SetInt("u_ash_texture", 3);
    m_universalParticleShader->SetInt("u_heat_texture", 4);
    
    m_particleVAO->Bind();
    glDrawArrays(GL_POINTS, 0, m_particleData.size());
    m_particleVAO->Unbind();

    m_universalParticleShader->Unbind();

    glDepthMask(GL_TRUE);
    glDisable(GL_BLEND);
}
void Renderer::DrawTerrain(std::shared_ptr<VertexArrayObject> vao,
                           const glm::vec3 &terrainPos,
                           std::shared_ptr<Texture> terrainTexture) { 
  if (!vao || !m_terrainShader)
    return;

  auto ib = vao->GetIndexBuffer();
  uint32_t count = ib ? ib->GetCount() : 0;
  glm::mat4 model = glm::translate(glm::mat4(1.0f), terrainPos);
  
  m_terrainShader->Bind();
  
  if (terrainTexture) {
    terrainTexture->Bind(0); 
    m_terrainShader->SetInt("u_DiffuseMap", 0);
  }
  
  m_terrainShader->SetMat4("u_View", m_viewMat);
  m_terrainShader->SetMat4("u_Projection", m_projectionMat);
  m_terrainShader->SetMat4("u_Model", model);

  m_terrainShader->SetVec3("u_LightPos", glm::vec3(100.0f, 100.0f, 100.0f));
  m_terrainShader->SetFloat("u_MaxHeight", 80.0f);

  vao->Bind();
  glDrawElements(GL_TRIANGLES, count, GL_UNSIGNED_INT, nullptr);
  vao->Unbind();
  
  m_terrainShader->Unbind();
  
  glBindTexture(GL_TEXTURE_2D, 0);
}

void Renderer::SetEffectParams(const std::string &uniformName, const FireParams::EffectParams &params)
{
 m_universalParticleShader->SetFloat(uniformName + ".particleSize", params.particleSize);
    m_universalParticleShader->SetFloat(uniformName + ".sizeVariation", params.sizeVariation);
    m_universalParticleShader->SetFloat(uniformName + ".animationSpeed", params.animationSpeed);
    m_universalParticleShader->SetFloat(uniformName + ".lifetime", params.lifetime);
    m_universalParticleShader->SetFloat(uniformName + ".spawnRate", params.spawnRate);
    m_universalParticleShader->SetFloat(uniformName + ".riseSpeed", params.riseSpeed);
    m_universalParticleShader->SetFloat(uniformName + ".turbulence", params.turbulence);
    m_universalParticleShader->SetFloat(uniformName + ".windInfluence", params.windInfluence);
    m_universalParticleShader->SetFloat(uniformName + ".spread", params.spread);
    m_universalParticleShader->SetVec3(uniformName + ".baseColor", params.baseColor);
    m_universalParticleShader->SetVec3(uniformName + ".endColor", params.endColor);
    m_universalParticleShader->SetFloat(uniformName + ".colorVariation", params.colorVariation);
    
    m_universalParticleShader->SetFloat(uniformName + ".redIntensity", params.redIntensity);
    m_universalParticleShader->SetFloat(uniformName + ".yellowReduction", params.yellowReduction);
    m_universalParticleShader->SetFloat(uniformName + ".alphaMultiplier", params.alphaMultiplier);
    m_universalParticleShader->SetFloat(uniformName + ".textureInfluence", params.textureInfluence);
 }

void Renderer::SetFireParams(const FireParams &params) {
    m_fireParams = params;
    try {
        m_fireTexture = Texture::Create(params.fireTexturePath);
    } catch (const std::exception &e) {
        std::cout << "Failed to load fire texture: " << e.what() << std::endl;
        m_fireTexture = Texture::Create("res/textures/fire_texture3.png");
    }
    
    try {
        m_smokeTexture = Texture::Create(params.smokeTexturePath);
    } catch (const std::exception &e) {
        std::cout << "Failed to load smoke texture: " << e.what() << std::endl;
        m_smokeTexture = Texture::Create("res/textures/smoke_texture.png");
    }
    
    try {
        m_sparksTexture = Texture::Create(params.sparksTexturePath);
    } catch (const std::exception &e) {
        std::cout << "Failed to load sparks texture: " << e.what() << std::endl;
        m_sparksTexture = Texture::Create("res/textures/sparks_texture.png");
    }
    
    try {
        m_ashTexture = Texture::Create(params.ashTexturePath);
    } catch (const std::exception &e) {
        std::cout << "Failed to load ash texture: " << e.what() << std::endl;
        m_ashTexture = Texture::Create("res/textures/ash_texture.png");
    }
    
    try {
        m_heatTexture = Texture::Create(params.heatTexturePath);
    } catch (const std::exception &e) {
        std::cout << "Failed to load heat texture: " << e.what() << std::endl;
        m_heatTexture = Texture::Create("res/textures/heat_texture.png");
    }

    // Устанавливаем параметры в шейдер
    if (m_universalParticleShader) {
        m_universalParticleShader->Bind();
        
        // Параметры для каждого эффекта
        SetEffectParams("u_fire_params", m_fireParams.fire);
        SetEffectParams("u_smoke_params", m_fireParams.smoke);
        SetEffectParams("u_sparks_params", m_fireParams.sparks);
        SetEffectParams("u_ash_params", m_fireParams.ash);
        SetEffectParams("u_heat_params", m_fireParams.heat);
        
        m_universalParticleShader->Unbind();
    }
}
} // namespace engine


===== src/graphics/OpenGL/OpenGLVertexArrayObject.cc =====
#include "OpenGLVertexArrayObject.h"
#include <iostream>
namespace engine {

std::shared_ptr<VertexArrayObject> CreateOpenGLVertexArrayObject() {
  return std::make_shared<OpenGLVertexArrayObject>();
}
static GLenum ShaderDataTypeToOpenGLBaseType(ShaderDataType type) {
  switch (type) {
  case ShaderDataType::Float:
    return GL_FLOAT;
  case ShaderDataType::Float2:
    return GL_FLOAT;
  case ShaderDataType::Float3:
    return GL_FLOAT;
  case ShaderDataType::Float4:
    return GL_FLOAT;
  case ShaderDataType::Mat3:
    return GL_FLOAT;
  case ShaderDataType::Mat4:
    return GL_FLOAT;
  case ShaderDataType::Int:
    return GL_INT;
  case ShaderDataType::Int2:
    return GL_INT;
  case ShaderDataType::Int3:
    return GL_INT;
  case ShaderDataType::Int4:
    return GL_INT;
  case ShaderDataType::Bool:
    return GL_BOOL;
  }
  return 0;
}
OpenGLVertexArrayObject::OpenGLVertexArrayObject() {
  glGenVertexArrays(1, &m_id);
  glBindVertexArray(m_id);
}

OpenGLVertexArrayObject::~OpenGLVertexArrayObject() {
  glDeleteVertexArrays(1, &m_id);
}

void OpenGLVertexArrayObject::Bind() const { glBindVertexArray(m_id); }

void OpenGLVertexArrayObject::Unbind() const { glBindVertexArray(0); }

void OpenGLVertexArrayObject::AddVertexBuffer(
    const std::shared_ptr<VertexBuffer> &vertexBuffer) {
  glBindVertexArray(m_id);
  vertexBuffer->Bind();
  const auto &layout = vertexBuffer->GetLayout();

  for (const auto &elem : layout) {
    switch (elem.type) {
    case ShaderDataType::Float:
    case ShaderDataType::Float2:
    case ShaderDataType::Float3:
    case ShaderDataType::Float4: {
      glEnableVertexAttribArray(m_VertexBufferIndex);
      glVertexAttribPointer(m_VertexBufferIndex, elem.GetComponentCount(),
                            ShaderDataTypeToOpenGLBaseType(elem.type),
                            elem.normalized ? GL_TRUE : GL_FALSE,
                            layout.GetStride(),
                            reinterpret_cast<void *>(elem.offset));
      m_VertexBufferIndex++;
      break;
    }

    case ShaderDataType::Int:
    case ShaderDataType::Int2:
    case ShaderDataType::Int3:
    case ShaderDataType::Int4:
    case ShaderDataType::Bool: {
      glEnableVertexAttribArray(m_VertexBufferIndex);

      glVertexAttribIPointer(m_VertexBufferIndex, elem.GetComponentCount(),
                             ShaderDataTypeToOpenGLBaseType(elem.type),
                             layout.GetStride(),
                             reinterpret_cast<void *>(elem.offset));
      m_VertexBufferIndex++;
      break;
    }

    case ShaderDataType::Mat3:
    case ShaderDataType::Mat4: {
      uint8_t count = elem.GetComponentCount();
      for (uint8_t i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
        glEnableVertexAttribArray(m_VertexBufferIndex);
        glVertexAttribPointer(
            m_VertexBufferIndex, count,
            ShaderDataTypeToOpenGLBaseType(elem.type),
            elem.normalized ? GL_TRUE : GL_FALSE, layout.GetStride(),
            reinterpret_cast<void *>(elem.offset + sizeof(float) * count * i));
        glVertexAttribDivisor(m_VertexBufferIndex, 1);
        m_VertexBufferIndex++;
      }
      break;
    }
    default:
      std::cout << "Unknown ShaderDataType!" << std::endl;
    }
  }
  m_vertexBuffers.push_back(vertexBuffer);
}

void OpenGLVertexArrayObject::SetIndexBuffer(
    const std::shared_ptr<IndexBuffer> &indexBuffer) {
  glBindVertexArray(m_id);
  indexBuffer->Bind();
  m_indexBuffer = indexBuffer;
}
} // namespace engine


===== src/graphics/OpenGL/OpenGLTexture.cc =====
#include "OpenGLTexture.h"
#include <stb_image.h>
#include <stdexcept>
namespace engine {
std::shared_ptr<Texture> CreateOpenGLTexture(const std::string &path) {
  return std::make_shared<OpenGLTexture>(path);
}

std::shared_ptr<Texture> CreateOpenGLTexture(const TextureSpecification &spec) {
  return std::make_shared<OpenGLTexture>(spec);
}

OpenGLTexture::OpenGLTexture(const std::string &path) {
  int height, width, channels;
  stbi_set_flip_vertically_on_load(1);
  stbi_uc *data = stbi_load(path.c_str(), &width, &height, &channels, 0);
  if (data) {

    m_width = width;
    m_height = height;

    GLenum internalFormat = 0, dataFormat = 0;

    if (channels == 4) {
      internalFormat = GL_RGBA8;
      dataFormat = GL_RGBA;
    } else if (channels == 3) {
      internalFormat = GL_RGB8;
      dataFormat = GL_RGB;
    } else {
      stbi_image_free(data);
      throw std::runtime_error("Unsupported image format: " + path);
    }
    glGenTextures(1, &m_rendererID);
    glBindTexture(GL_TEXTURE_2D, m_rendererID);
    glTexImage2D(GL_TEXTURE_2D, 0, internalFormat, m_width, m_height, 0, dataFormat, GL_UNSIGNED_BYTE, data);
    glTexParameteri(GL_TEXTURE_2D, GL_TEXTURE_MIN_FILTER, GL_LINEAR);
    glTexParameteri(GL_TEXTURE_2D, GL_TEXTURE_MAG_FILTER, GL_LINEAR);
    glTexParameteri(GL_TEXTURE_2D, GL_TEXTURE_WRAP_S, GL_REPEAT);
    glTexParameteri(GL_TEXTURE_2D, GL_TEXTURE_WRAP_T, GL_REPEAT);

    stbi_image_free(data);
  } else {
    throw std::runtime_error("Failed to load texture: " + path);
  }
}
OpenGLTexture::OpenGLTexture(const TextureSpecification &spec) {}
OpenGLTexture::~OpenGLTexture() { glDeleteTextures(1, &m_rendererID); }
void OpenGLTexture::Bind(uint32_t slot) const {
  glActiveTexture(GL_TEXTURE0 + slot);
  glBindTexture(GL_TEXTURE_2D, m_rendererID);
}
void OpenGLTexture::Unbind() const {}
} // namespace nova

                                                     uint32_t count) {
  return std::make_unique<OpenGLIndexBuffer>(indices, count);
}

OpenGLIndexBuffer::OpenGLIndexBuffer(uint32_t *indices, uint32_t count) {
  m_count = count;
  glGenBuffers(1, &m_id);
  glBindBuffer(GL_ELEMENT_ARRAY_BUFFER, m_id);
  glBufferData(GL_ELEMENT_ARRAY_BUFFER, count * sizeof(uint32_t), indices,
               GL_STATIC_DRAW);
}

OpenGLIndexBuffer::~OpenGLIndexBuffer() { glDeleteBuffers(1, &m_id); }

void OpenGLIndexBuffer::Bind() const {
  glBindBuffer(GL_ELEMENT_ARRAY_BUFFER, m_id);
}

void OpenGLIndexBuffer::Unbind() const {
  glBindBuffer(GL_ELEMENT_ARRAY_BUFFER, 0);
}

} // namespace engine

#include "graphics/Shader.h"

namespace engine {
class OpenGLShader final : public Shader {
public:

  OpenGLShader(const std::string &vertexSrc, const std::string &fragmentSrc);

                           const std::string &geometrySrc,
                           const std::string &fragmentSrc);
  virtual ~OpenGLShader();

  void Bind() const override;
  void Unbind() const override;

  void SetMat4(const std::string &name, const glm::mat4 &matrix) override;
  void SetVec3(const std::string &name, const glm::vec3 &vector) override;
  void SetVec4(const std::string &name, const glm::vec4 &vector) override;
  void SetInt(const std::string &name, int value) override;
  void SetFloat(const std::string &name, float value) override;
  void SetBool(const std::string &name, bool value) override;

private:
  GLuint CompileShader(GLenum type, const std::string &source);
  uint32_t m_rendererID;
};

===== src/graphics/OpenGL/OpenGLShader.cc =====
#include "OpenGLShader.h"
#include <fstream>
#include <iostream>
#include <memory>
#include <sstream>
namespace engine {
std::unique_ptr<Shader> CreateOpenGLShader(const std::string &filename) {
  return std::make_unique<OpenGLShader>(filename);
}
std::unique_ptr<Shader> CreateOpenGLShader(const std::string &vertexSrc,
                                           const std::string &fragmentSrc) {
  return std::make_unique<OpenGLShader>(vertexSrc, fragmentSrc);
}

std::unique_ptr<Shader> CreateOpenGLShader(const std::string &vertexSrc,
                                           const std::string &fragmentSrc,
                                           const std::string &geometrySrc) {
  return std::make_unique<OpenGLShader>(vertexSrc, fragmentSrc, geometrySrc);
}
OpenGLShader::OpenGLShader(const std::string &filename) {
  std::ifstream stream(filename);
  std::string line;
  enum ShaderType { NONE = -1, VERTEX = 0, GEOMETRY = 1, FRAGMENT = 2 };
  std::stringstream ss[3];
  ShaderType type = ShaderType::NONE;

  while (getline(stream, line)) {
    if (line.find("#shader") != std::string::npos) {
      if (line.find("vertex") != std::string::npos) {
        type = ShaderType::VERTEX;
      } else if (line.find("geometry") != std::string::npos) { // ДОБАВИЛ
        type = ShaderType::GEOMETRY;
      } else if (line.find("fragment") != std::string::npos) {
        type = ShaderType::FRAGMENT;
      }
    } else {
      if (type != ShaderType::NONE) {
        ss[(int)type] << line << '\n';
      }
    }
  }

  m_rendererID = glCreateProgram();

  GLuint vertexShader = CompileShader(GL_VERTEX_SHADER, ss[0].str());
  glAttachShader(m_rendererID, vertexShader);

  if (!ss[1].str().empty()) {
    GLuint geometryShader = CompileShader(GL_GEOMETRY_SHADER, ss[1].str());
    glAttachShader(m_rendererID, geometryShader);
  }

  GLuint fragmentShader = CompileShader(GL_FRAGMENT_SHADER, ss[2].str());
  glAttachShader(m_rendererID, fragmentShader);

  glLinkProgram(m_rendererID);

  GLint isLinked;
  glGetProgramiv(m_rendererID, GL_LINK_STATUS, &isLinked);
  if (!isLinked) {
    GLint maxLength;
    glGetProgramiv(m_rendererID, GL_INFO_LOG_LENGTH, &maxLength);
    std::string infoLog(maxLength, ' ');
    glGetProgramInfoLog(m_rendererID, maxLength, &maxLength, &infoLog[0]);
    std::cout << "Shader linking failed: " + infoLog << '\n';
  }
}

OpenGLShader::OpenGLShader(const std::string &vertexSrc,
                           const std::string &fragmentSrc) {
  m_rendererID = glCreateProgram();
  GLuint vertexShader = CompileShader(GL_VERTEX_SHADER, vertexSrc);
  GLuint fragmentShader = CompileShader(GL_FRAGMENT_SHADER, fragmentSrc);
  glAttachShader(m_rendererID, vertexShader);
  glAttachShader(m_rendererID, fragmentShader);
  glLinkProgram(m_rendererID);
}
OpenGLShader::OpenGLShader(const std::string &vertexSrc,
                           const std::string &geometrySrc,
                           const std::string &fragmentSrc) {
  m_rendererID = glCreateProgram();

  GLuint vertexShader = CompileShader(GL_VERTEX_SHADER, vertexSrc);
  GLuint geometryShader = CompileShader(GL_GEOMETRY_SHADER, geometrySrc);
  GLuint fragmentShader = CompileShader(GL_FRAGMENT_SHADER, fragmentSrc);

  glAttachShader(m_rendererID, vertexShader);
  glAttachShader(m_rendererID, geometryShader);
  glAttachShader(m_rendererID, fragmentShader);

  glLinkProgram(m_rendererID);

  // ПРОВЕРКА ОШИБОК
  GLint isLinked;
  glGetProgramiv(m_rendererID, GL_LINK_STATUS, &isLinked);
  if (!isLinked) {
    GLint maxLength;
    glGetProgramiv(m_rendererID, GL_INFO_LOG_LENGTH, &maxLength);
    std::string infoLog(maxLength, ' ');
    glGetProgramInfoLog(m_rendererID, maxLength, &maxLength, &infoLog[0]);
    std::cout << "Shader linking failed: " + infoLog << '\n';
  }
}

OpenGLShader::~OpenGLShader() { glDeleteProgram(m_rendererID); }

void OpenGLShader::Bind() const { glUseProgram(m_rendererID); }

void OpenGLShader::Unbind() const { glUseProgram(0); }

void OpenGLShader::SetMat4(const std::string &name, const glm::mat4 &matrix) {
  auto loc = glGetUniformLocation(m_rendererID, name.c_str());
  if (loc != -1) {
    glUniformMatrix4fv(loc, 1, GL_FALSE, &matrix[0][0]);
  } else {
    std::cout << "Warning: uniform '" + name + "' doesn't exist!\n";
  }
}

void OpenGLShader::SetVec3(const std::string &name, const glm::vec3 &vector) {
  auto loc = glGetUniformLocation(m_rendererID, name.c_str());
  if (loc != -1) {
    glUniform3fv(loc, 1, &vector[0]);
  } else {
    std::cout << "Warning: uniform '" + name + "' doesn't exist!\n";
  }
}

void OpenGLShader::SetVec4(const std::string &name, const glm::vec4 &vector) {
  auto loc = glGetUniformLocation(m_rendererID, name.c_str());
  if (loc != -1) {
    glUniform4fv(loc, 1, &vector[0]);
  } else {
    std::cout << "Warning: uniform '" + name + "' doesn't exist!\n";
  }
}
void OpenGLShader::SetInt(const std::string &name, int value) {
  auto loc = glGetUniformLocation(m_rendererID, name.c_str());
  if (loc != -1) {
    glUniform1i(loc, value);
  } else {
    std::cout << "Warning: uniform '" + name + "' doesn't exist!\n";
  }
}

void OpenGLShader::SetFloat(const std::string &name, float value) {
  auto loc = glGetUniformLocation(m_rendererID, name.c_str());
  if (loc != -1) {
    glUniform1f(loc, value);
  } else {
    std::cout << "Warning: uniform '" + name + "' doesn't exist!\n";
  }
}

void OpenGLShader::SetBool(const std::string &name, bool value) {
  auto loc = glGetUniformLocation(m_rendererID, name.c_str());
  if (loc != -1) {
    glUniform1i(loc, (int)value);
  } else {
    std::cout << "Warning: uniform '" + name + "' doesn't exist!\n";
  }
}

GLuint OpenGLShader::CompileShader(GLenum type, const std::string &source) {
  GLuint shader = glCreateShader(type);
  const char *src = source.c_str();
  glShaderSource(shader, 1, &src, nullptr);
  glCompileShader(shader);
  GLint isCompiled;
  glGetShaderiv(shader, GL_COMPILE_STATUS, &isCompiled);
  if (!isCompiled) {
    GLint maxLength;
    glGetShaderiv(shader, GL_INFO_LOG_LENGTH, &maxLength);
    std::string infoLog(maxLength, ' ');
    glGetShaderInfoLog(shader, maxLength, &maxLength, &infoLog[0]);
    glDeleteShader(shader);
    std::cout << "Shader compilation failed: " + infoLog << " in file "
              << source << '\n';
  }
  return shader;
}
} // namespace engine


===== src/graphics/OpenGL/OpenGLVertexBuffer.cc =====
#include "OpenGLVertexBuffer.h"

namespace engine {
std::unique_ptr<VertexBuffer> CreateOpenGLVertexBuffer(int32_t size) {
  return std::make_unique<OpenGLVertexBuffer>(size);

                                                       int32_t size) {
  return std::make_unique<OpenGLVertexBuffer>(vertices, size);
}

OpenGLVertexBuffer::OpenGLVertexBuffer(int32_t size) : m_size(size) {
  glGenBuffers(1, &m_id);
  glBindBuffer(GL_ARRAY_BUFFER, m_id);
  glBufferData(GL_ARRAY_BUFFER, size, nullptr, GL_DYNAMIC_DRAW);
}
OpenGLVertexBuffer::OpenGLVertexBuffer(float *vertices, int32_t size) : m_size(size) {
  glGenBuffers(1, &m_id);
  glBindBuffer(GL_ARRAY_BUFFER, m_id);
  glBufferData(GL_ARRAY_BUFFER, size, vertices, GL_STATIC_DRAW);
}
OpenGLVertexBuffer::~OpenGLVertexBuffer() { glDeleteBuffers(1, &m_id); }

void OpenGLVertexBuffer::Bind() const { glBindBuffer(GL_ARRAY_BUFFER, m_id); }

void OpenGLVertexBuffer::Unbind() const { glBindBuffer(GL_ARRAY_BUFFER, 0); }

void OpenGLVertexBuffer::SetData(const void *data, uint32_t size) {
    glBindBuffer(GL_ARRAY_BUFFER, m_id);
    
    // ИСПОЛЬЗУЙ glBufferData вместо glBufferSubData!
    glBufferData(GL_ARRAY_BUFFER, size, data, GL_DYNAMIC_DRAW);
    m_size = size;
}

void OpenGLVertexBuffer::SetLayout(const BufferLayout &layout) {
  m_layout = layout;
}

const BufferLayout &OpenGLVertexBuffer::GetLayout() const { return m_layout; }
} // namespace engine

===== src/graphics/ComputeShader.h =====
#pragma once
#include <glm/glm.hpp>
#include <memory>
#include <string>
#include <unordered_map>

namespace engine {

class ComputeShader {
public:
  static std::shared_ptr<ComputeShader> Create(const std::string &filepath);
  static std::shared_ptr<ComputeShader>
  CreateFromSource(const std::string &source);

  ~ComputeShader();
  ComputeShader() = default;

  void Bind() const;
  void Unbind() const;

  void Dispatch(uint32_t groupsX, uint32_t groupsY = 1, uint32_t groupsZ = 1);
  void DispatchIndirect(uint32_t indirectBufferID);

  // Memory barrier
  void MemoryBarrier();

  // Uniform setters
  void SetInt(const std::string &name, int value);
  void SetUInt(const std::string &name, uint32_t value);
  void SetFloat(const std::string &name, float value);
  void SetFloat2(const std::string &name, const glm::vec2 &value);
  void SetFloat3(const std::string &name, const glm::vec3 &value);
  void SetFloat4(const std::string &name, const glm::vec4 &value);
  void SetFloatArray(const std::string &name, const float *values,
                     uint32_t count);
  void SetFloat2Array(const std::string &name, const glm::vec2 *values,
                      uint32_t count);
  void SetFloat3Array(const std::string &name, const glm::vec3 *values,
                      uint32_t count);
  void SetFloat4Array(const std::string &name, const glm::vec4 *values,
                      uint32_t count);
  void SetIntArray(const std::string &name, const int *values, uint32_t count);
  void SetMat4(const std::string &name, const glm::mat4 &value);
  void SetBool(const std::string &name, bool value) {
    SetInt(name, value ? 1 : 0);
  }
  uint32_t GetID() const { return m_rendererID; }

private:
  bool LoadFromFile(const std::string &filepath);
  bool Compile(const std::string &source);
  int GetUniformLocation(const std::string &name);

  uint32_t m_rendererID = 0;
  std::unordered_map<std::string, int> m_uniformLocationCache;
};

} // namespace engine

===== src/graphics/TextureCache.cc =====
// TextureCache.cpp
#include "TextureCache.h"

namespace engine {

TextureCache &TextureCache::Instance() {
  static TextureCache instance;
  return instance;
}

std::shared_ptr<Texture> TextureCache::GetOrLoad(const std::string &path) {
  if (path.empty())
    return nullptr;

  auto it = m_cache.find(path);
  if (it != m_cache.end()) {
    if (auto texture = it->second.lock()) {
      return texture;
    }
  }

  auto texture = Texture::Create(path);
  m_cache[path] = texture;
  return texture;
}

void TextureCache::Clear() { m_cache.clear(); }

} // namespace engine

  SetIndexBuffer(const std::shared_ptr<IndexBuffer> &indexBuffer) = 0;
