===== src/core/scene/Components.h =====
#pragma once
#include "entt.hpp"

#include "graphics/ParticleSystem.h"
#include <glm/glm.hpp>
#include <glm/gtc/matrix_transform.hpp>

#define GLM_ENABLE_EXPERIMENTAL
#include <glm/gtx/quaternion.hpp>

namespace engine {
using EntityID = std::string;
struct IDComponent {
  EntityID ID;
  IDComponent(const std::string &id = "") : ID(id) {}
  std::string toString() const { return "IDComponent { ID: " + ID + " }"; }
  friend std::ostream &operator<<(std::ostream &os, const IDComponent &comp) {
    return os << comp.toString();
  }
};

struct TransformComponent {
  glm::vec3 position{0.0f};
  glm::vec3 scale{1.0f};
  glm::quat rotation{1.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f};

  // ДОБАВЬ КОНСТРУКТОРЫ
  TransformComponent() = default;
  TransformComponent(const glm::vec3 &position) : position(position) {}
  TransformComponent(const glm::vec3 &position, const glm::vec3 &scale)
      : position(position), scale(scale) {}

  std::string toString() const {
    return "TransformComponent{Position: " + std::to_string(position.x) + ", " +
           std::to_string(position.y) + ", " + std::to_string(position.z) + "}";
  }
};
struct ParticleComponent {
  std::shared_ptr<ParticleSystem> system;
  glm::vec3 offset = glm::vec3(0.0f); // Смещение относительно позиции entity

  ParticleComponent() { system = std::make_shared<ParticleSystem>(); }

  void Play() {
    if (system)
      system->Play();
  }
  void Stop() {
    if (system)
      system->Stop();
  }
  void SetEffect(const std::shared_ptr<ParticleEffect> &effect) {
    if (system)
      system->SetEffect(effect);
  }
};

struct RenderComponent {
  std::string prefab_id;
  glm::vec3 scale{1, 1, 1};
  bool is_visible = true;

  RenderComponent(const std::string &id = "", const glm::vec3 &s = {1, 1, 1},
                  bool v = true)
      : prefab_id(id), scale(s), is_visible(v) {}
};

struct AgentLinkComponent {
  int gpu_index;
  std::string prefab_id;

  AgentLinkComponent(int idx = -1, const std::string &id = "")
      : gpu_index(idx), prefab_id(id) {}
};

struct FireEmitterComponent {
  float Intensity = 1.0f;

  FireEmitterComponent() = default;
  FireEmitterComponent(float intensity) : Intensity(intensity) {}
};

struct ParticleSystemComponent {
  std::shared_ptr<ParticleSystem> system;
  bool isActive = false;
  float activationTimer = 0.0f;
  glm::vec3 instancePosition;
};
} // namespace engine



===== src/core/scene/Camera.h =====
#pragma once
#include <glm/glm.hpp>
#include <glm/gtc/matrix_transform.hpp>
#define YAW -90.0f
#define PITCH -40.0f
#define SPEED 50.0f
#define SENSITIVITY 0.1f
#define ZOOM 45.0f
namespace engine {

enum Camera_Movement { FORWARD, BACKWARD, LEFT, RIGHT };

class Camera {
public:
  Camera(glm::vec3 position = glm::vec3(0.0f, 0.0f, -3.0f),
         glm::vec3 up = glm::vec3(0.0f, 1.0f, 0.0f), float yaw = YAW,
         float pitch = PITCH)
      : m_front(glm::vec3(0.0f, 0.0f, 1.0f)), m_movementSpeed(SPEED),
        m_mouseSensitivity(SENSITIVITY), m_zoom(ZOOM) {
    m_position = position;
    m_worldUp = up;
    m_yaw = yaw;
    m_pitch = pitch;
    updateCameraVectors();
  }

  glm::mat4 GetViewMatrix() {
    return glm::lookAt(m_position, m_position + m_front, m_up);
  }

  void ProcessKeyboard(Camera_Movement direction, float deltaTime) {
    float velocity = m_movementSpeed * deltaTime;
    if (direction == FORWARD)
      m_position += m_front * velocity;
    if (direction == BACKWARD)
      m_position -= m_front * velocity;
    if (direction == LEFT)
      m_position -= m_right * velocity;
    if (direction == RIGHT)
      m_position += m_right * velocity;
  }

  void ProcessMouseMovement(float xoffset, float yoffset,
                            bool constrainPitch = true) {
    xoffset *= m_mouseSensitivity;
    yoffset *= m_mouseSensitivity;

    m_yaw += xoffset;
    m_pitch -= yoffset;

    if (constrainPitch) {
      if (m_pitch > 89.0f)
        m_pitch = 89.0f;
      if (m_pitch < -89.0f)
        m_pitch = -89.0f;
    }

    updateCameraVectors();
  }

  void updateCameraVectors() {
    // calculate the new Front vector
    glm::vec3 front;
    front.x = cos(m_yaw * (M_PI / 180.0)) * cos(m_pitch * (M_PI / 180.0));
    front.y = sin(m_pitch * (M_PI / 180.0));
    front.z = sin(m_yaw * (M_PI / 180.0)) * cos(m_pitch * (M_PI / 180.0));
    if (front.length() > 0) {
      m_front = glm::normalize(front);
    }

    // also re-calculate the Right and Up vector
    m_right = glm::normalize(glm::cross(m_front, m_worldUp));
    m_up = glm::normalize(glm::cross(m_right, m_front));
  }

  void ProcessMouseScroll(float yoffset) {
    m_zoom += (float)yoffset;
    if (m_zoom < 1.0f)
      m_zoom = 1.0f;
    if (m_zoom > 45.0f)
      m_zoom = 45.0f;
  }

  float GetZoom() const { return m_zoom; }
  glm::vec3 GetPosition() const { return m_position; }
  void LookAt(const glm::vec3 &target) {
    m_front = glm::normalize(target - m_position);
    // Пересчитываем остальные векторы
    updateCameraVectors();
  }

  void SetPosition(const glm::vec3 &position) {
    m_position = position;
    updateCameraVectors();
  }

private:
  glm::vec3 m_position;
  glm::vec3 m_front;
  glm::vec3 m_up;
  glm::vec3 m_right;
  glm::vec3 m_worldUp;
  // euler Angles
  float m_yaw;
  float m_pitch;
  // camera options
  float m_movementSpeed;
  float m_mouseSensitivity;
  float m_zoom;
};
} // namespace engine



===== src/core/scene/Scene.cc =====
#include "Scene.h"
#include "Entity.h"

#include "core/AgentManager.h"
#include "core/Input.h"
#include "core/Window.h"
#include "core/agent.h"
#include "core/scene/Terrain.h"
#include "graphics/Model.h"
#include "graphics/Renderer.h"

#include <chrono>
#include <glm/gtc/matrix_transform.hpp>
#include <iostream>
#include <memory>
#include "graphics/ParticleSystemPool.h"
namespace engine {

Scene::Scene() : m_agentManager(1000000), m_camera(glm::vec3(500.0F, 100.0F, 600.0F)) {}

void Scene::Init() {
  m_terrain = std::make_unique<Terrain>();
  GetPrefabSystem().LoadAllFromDirectory("res/prefabs");
  engine::Renderer::Instance().Init();
  ParticleSystemPool::Initialize(1000);
}

auto Scene::CreateEntity(const std::string& id) -> Entity {
  auto handle = m_registry.create();
  Entity entity(handle, &m_registry);

  entity.AddComponent<IDComponent>(id);
  return entity;
}

void Scene::GenerateTerrain(const TerrainGenerationSettings& s) {
  m_terrain->GenerateRandom(s);
}

void Scene::EnsureModelsForPrefab(const std::string& id, const engine::data::AgentPrefab& prefab) {
  if (m_objectModels.Find(id)) {
    return;
  }

  const std::string& path = prefab.m_modelPath;
  if (path.empty()) {
    std::cerr << "[Scene] prefab '" << id << "' has empty model_path\n";
    return;
  }

  LoadObjectModels(id, path, path, path);
}

void Scene::Update(float dt) {
    auto startTime = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();

  m_windTime += dt;
  glm::vec3 currentWind = m_windConfig.getCurrentWind(m_windTime);

  //Шаг симуляции через FireModule
  auto simStart = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
  if (m_agentManager.GetAgentCount() > 0) {
    m_fire.Step(m_agentManager, dt, currentWind);
  }
  auto simTime = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();

  const bool UI_MOUSE = engine::InputSystem::Instance().UIMouseCaptured();
  const bool UI_KBD = engine::InputSystem::Instance().UIKeyboardCaptured();

  if (!UI_KBD) {
    if (engine::InputSystem::Instance().GetKey(engine::KeyCode::W)) {
      m_camera.ProcessKeyboard(FORWARD, dt);
    }
    if (engine::InputSystem::Instance().GetKey(engine::KeyCode::S)) {
      m_camera.ProcessKeyboard(BACKWARD, dt);
    }
    if (engine::InputSystem::Instance().GetKey(engine::KeyCode::D)) {
      m_camera.ProcessKeyboard(RIGHT, dt);
    }
    if (engine::InputSystem::Instance().GetKey(engine::KeyCode::A)) {
      m_camera.ProcessKeyboard(LEFT, dt);
    }
  }

  // Поворот камеры
  const glm::vec2 DELTA = engine::InputSystem::Instance().GetMouseDelta();
  if (!UI_MOUSE && engine::InputSystem::Instance().GetMouseButton(engine::MouseButton::Right,
                                                                  InputState::Held)) {
    if (DELTA.x != 0.F || DELTA.y != 0.F) {
      m_camera.ProcessMouseMovement(DELTA.x, DELTA.y);
    }
  }

  if (!UI_MOUSE) {
    const float SCROLL_Y = engine::InputSystem::Instance().GetMouseScrollY();
    if (SCROLL_Y != 0.0F) {
      // zoom / fov, если нужно
    }
  }

  // Частицы используют тот же ветер
  auto particleView = m_registry.view<ParticleSystemComponent>();
  for (auto [entity, particleComp] : particleView.each()) {
    if (particleComp.isActive) {
      particleComp.system->Update();
      particleComp.system->SetWind(currentWind);
      // Автоматическое выключение через некоторое время после деактивации
      if (!particleComp.system->IsPlaying() && particleComp.activationTimer > 0.0f) {
        particleComp.activationTimer -= dt;
        if (particleComp.activationTimer <= 0.0f) {
          particleComp.isActive = false;
        }
      }
    }
  }

  for (auto& system : m_particleSystems) {
    system->SetWind(currentWind);
    system->Update();
  }

}

void Scene::Render() {
  const int FB_W = static_cast<int>(m_vpW * m_vpScaleX);
  const int FB_H = static_cast<int>(m_vpH * m_vpScaleY);
  glViewport(m_vpX, m_vpY, FB_W, FB_H);

  const float aspect = FB_H > 0 ? FB_W / (float)FB_H : 1.0f;
  auto projection = glm::perspective(m_camera.GetZoom(), aspect, 0.1f, 2000.0f);
  auto view = m_camera.GetViewMatrix();

  Renderer::Instance().BeginScene(view, projection);

  auto agents = LoadAgentsFromGPU();
  
  std::unordered_map<std::string, std::vector<glm::mat4>> instanceData;
  
  auto render_view = m_registry.view<TransformComponent, RenderComponent, AgentLinkComponent>();

  for (auto [entity, transform, render, link] : render_view.each()) {
    if (!render.is_visible) continue;

    const Agent& agent = agents[link.gpu_index];
    const auto& prefab = m_prefabSystem.GetPrefab(link.prefab_id);

    AgentType state = AgentType::NORMAL;
    if (agent.type == AgentType::BURNED)
      state = AgentType::BURNED;
    else if (agent.isBurning() || agent.type == AgentType::BURNING)
      state = AgentType::BURNING;

    auto& particleComp = m_registry.get<ParticleSystemComponent>(entity);

    if (state == AgentType::BURNING && !particleComp.isActive) {
      particleComp.isActive = true;
      particleComp.system->Play();
    } else if (state != AgentType::BURNING && particleComp.isActive) {
      particleComp.isActive = false;
      particleComp.system->Stop();
    }

    auto material = prefab.GetMaterialForState(state);
    auto model = m_objectModels.Find(link.prefab_id);
    if (!model || !model->m_normal)
      continue;

    auto* draw_model = model->m_normal.get();
    if (state == AgentType::BURNING && model->m_burning)
      draw_model = model->m_burning.get();
    if (state == AgentType::BURNED && model->m_burned)
      draw_model = model->m_burned.get();

    if (draw_model && material) {
      Renderer::Instance().DrawModelWithMaterial(*draw_model, transform.position, render.scale,
                                                 m_camera.GetPosition(), material);
    }
  }

  Renderer::Instance().UploadAgentsData(agents);
  Renderer::Instance().DrawTerrain(m_terrain->GetVAO(), m_terrain->GetPosition(), m_terrain->GetDiffuseTexture());
  auto particleView = m_registry.view<ParticleSystemComponent, TransformComponent>();
  for (auto [entity, particleComp, transform] : particleView.each()) {
    if (particleComp.isActive) {
      particleComp.system->SetPosition(transform.position);
      particleComp.system->Render(view, projection);
    }
  }



  for (auto& system : m_particleSystems) {
    system->Render(view, projection);
  }

  Renderer::Instance().EndScene();

}

void Scene::AddParticleSystem(const std::shared_ptr<ParticleSystem>& system) {
  m_particleSystems.push_back(system);
}

void Scene::RemoveParticleSystem(const std::shared_ptr<ParticleSystem>& system) {
  m_particleSystems.erase(std::remove(m_particleSystems.begin(), m_particleSystems.end(), system),
                          m_particleSystems.end());
}

void Scene::Generate() {
  for (int i = 0; i < 50; i += 3) {
    for (int j = 0; j < 50; j += 3) {
      AddTree(500 + i, 500 + j, "default_tree");
    }
  }
}

std::vector<Agent> Scene::LoadAgentsFromGPU() {
  return m_agentManager.LoadAgentsFromGPU();
}
void Scene::LoadObjectModels(const std::string& object_name, const std::string& normal_path,
                             const std::string& burning_path, const std::string& burned_path) {
  try {
    ObjectModels models;
    models.m_normal = std::make_shared<Model>(normal_path);
    models.m_burning = std::make_shared<Model>(burning_path);
    models.m_burned = std::make_shared<Model>(burned_path);
    m_objectModels.Put(object_name, std::move(models));
  } catch (const std::exception& e) {
    std::cout << "Failed to load model '" << object_name << "': " << e.what() << '\n';
  }
}

glm::mat4 Scene::GetProjMatrix(int w, int h) {
  return glm::perspective(m_camera.GetZoom(), static_cast<float>(w) / static_cast<float>(h), 0.1F,
                          2000.0F);
}

void Scene::Clear() {
  m_agentManager.Clear();
  m_registry.clear();
}

void Scene::Shutdown() {
  m_objectModels.Clear();

  m_prefabSystem.ReleaseAllMaterials();

  m_agentManager.Clear();
  m_terrain->Cleanup();
  Renderer::Instance().Shutdown();
}

void Scene::AddPrefab(const std::string& prefab_id, float x, float z, float scale) {
  const auto& prefab = m_prefabSystem.GetPrefab(prefab_id);
  EnsureModelsForPrefab(prefab_id, prefab);
  const float y = GetGroundHeight(x, z);

  glm::vec3 finalScale = prefab.m_modelScale * scale;

  // 1. GPU агент
  Agent agent = m_prefabSystem.CreateAgentFromPrefab(prefab, {x, y, z}, scale);

  // 🔥 Применяем материал огня из префаба
  // prefab.m_fireMaterialId / m_fireIgnitionMult / m_fireMoistureOffset
  m_fire.ApplyMaterialToAgent(agent, prefab.m_fireMaterialId);

  // Небольшие модификаторы из префаба
  auto& coll = agent.fire_collider;

  // ignition_temp у тебя скаляр — скейлим один раз
  coll.ignition_temp *= prefab.m_fireIgnitionMult;

  // moisture — массив по точкам
  for (int i = 0; i < coll.point_count; ++i) {
    coll.moisture[i] += prefab.m_fireMoistureOffset;
  }

  int gpu_index = m_agentManager.AddAgent(agent);

  // 2. ECS Entity
  Entity entity = CreateEntity(prefab_id);
  entity.AddComponent<TransformComponent>(glm::vec3{x, y, z});
  entity.AddComponent<RenderComponent>(prefab_id, finalScale, true);
  entity.AddComponent<AgentLinkComponent>(gpu_index, prefab_id);

  auto& particleComp = entity.AddComponent<ParticleSystemComponent>();
  particleComp.system = ParticleSystemPool::AcquireFire();

  // ставим систему частиц примерно над объектом
  glm::vec3 particlePos{x, y + finalScale.y, z};
  particleComp.system->SetPosition(particlePos);

  particleComp.system->Stop();
  particleComp.isActive = false;

  // 3. FireEmitter
  if (prefab_id.find("fire") != std::string::npos) {
    entity.AddComponent<FireEmitterComponent>();
  }
}

void Scene::AddTree(float x, float z, const std::string& tree_type, float scale) {
  AddPrefab(tree_type, x, z, scale);
}

const std::vector<std::string>& Scene::ListPrefabIds() const {
  return m_prefabSystem.ListIds();
}

void Scene::ReloadModelForPrefab(const std::string& prefab_id,
                                 const engine::data::AgentPrefab& prefab) {
  // Перезагрузка модели
  ObjectModels models;
  models.m_normal = std::make_shared<Model>(prefab.m_modelPath);
  models.m_burning = std::make_shared<Model>(prefab.m_modelPath);
  models.m_burned = std::make_shared<Model>(prefab.m_modelPath);
  m_objectModels.Put(prefab_id, std::move(models));

  // 2. Обновляем префаб (материалы)
  auto& system_prefab = m_prefabSystem.GetPrefabMutable(prefab_id);
  system_prefab.normalMaterial = prefab.normalMaterial;
  system_prefab.burningMaterial = prefab.burningMaterial;
  system_prefab.burnedMaterial = prefab.burnedMaterial;

  auto view = m_registry.view<RenderComponent>();
  for (auto [entity, render] : view.each()) {
    if (render.prefab_id == prefab_id) {
      render.scale = prefab.m_modelScale;
    }
  }

  std::cout << "[Scene] Reloaded model for prefab '" << prefab_id << "'\n";
}

float Scene::GetGroundHeight(float x, float z) const {
  return m_terrain ? m_terrain->GetHeightAt(x, z) : 0.0F;
}

void Scene::AddFire(float x, float z, float intensity) {
  const float y = GetGroundHeight(x, z);
  AddFireAt(glm::vec3{x, y, z}, intensity);
}

void Scene::AddFireAt(const glm::vec3& world, float intensity) {
  m_agentManager.AddFireAt(Float3{world.x, world.y, world.z}, intensity);

  Entity fireEntity = CreateEntity("fire_emitter");
  fireEntity.AddComponent<TransformComponent>(world);

  auto& particleComp = fireEntity.AddComponent<ParticleSystemComponent>();
  particleComp.system = ParticleSystemPool::AcquireFire();
  particleComp.system->SetPosition(world);
  particleComp.system->Play();
  particleComp.isActive = true;

  fireEntity.AddComponent<FireEmitterComponent>();
}
bool Scene::RemoveNearestAt(const glm::vec3& world, float maxXZDist) {
  float bestDist2 = maxXZDist * maxXZDist;
  entt::entity best = entt::null;

  auto view = m_registry.view<TransformComponent, AgentLinkComponent>();
  view.each([&](entt::entity entity, const TransformComponent& transform,
                const AgentLinkComponent& link) {
    float dx = transform.position.x - world.x;
    float dz = transform.position.z - world.z;
    float dist2 = dx * dx + dz * dz;
    if (dist2 < bestDist2) {
      bestDist2 = dist2;
      best = entity;
    }
  });

  if (m_registry.all_of<ParticleSystemComponent>(best)) {
    m_registry.remove<ParticleSystemComponent>(best);
  }

  if (best == entt::null)
    return false;

  auto& link = m_registry.get<AgentLinkComponent>(best);
  int gpu_idx = link.gpu_index;

  int swapped_from = -1, swapped_to = -1;
  if (!m_agentManager.RemoveAgentAt(gpu_idx, &swapped_from, &swapped_to)) {
    return false;
  }

  if (swapped_from != -1 && swapped_to != -1 && swapped_from != swapped_to) {
    auto update_view = m_registry.view<AgentLinkComponent>();
    for (auto [entity, link_comp] : update_view.each()) {
      if (link_comp.gpu_index == swapped_from) {
        link_comp.gpu_index = swapped_to;
        break;
      }
    }
  }

  m_registry.destroy(best);

  // Удаляем из мапы по ID
  if (m_registry.all_of<IDComponent>(best)) {
    auto& id = m_registry.get<IDComponent>(best);
  }

  return true;
}
}  // namespace engine



===== src/core/scene/Terrain.cc =====
#include "Terrain.h"
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdint>
#include <memory>
#include <vector>

#include <glm/gtc/noise.hpp>
#include <random>
#include "graphics/IndexBuffer.h"
#include "graphics/Texture.h"
#include "graphics/VertexArrayObject.h"
#include "graphics/VertexBuffer.h"
namespace engine {
class Terrain::PImpl {
 public:
  int m_width = 0;  // на всякий случай
  int m_depth = 0;  // тоже
  float m_scale = 1.0f;
  float m_maxHeight = 100.0f;
  uint32_t m_seed = 0;
  float m_frequency = 0.02f;
  int m_octaves = 6;
  float m_persistence = 0.5f;
  float m_lacunarity = 2.0f;
  glm::vec3 m_position = glm::vec3(0.0f);
  std::vector<std::vector<float>> m_heightMap;
  std::shared_ptr<VertexArrayObject> m_vao;

  TerrainTextureSettings m_textureSettings;
  std::shared_ptr<Texture> m_diffuseTexture;

  [[nodiscard]] static float FBM(glm::vec2 p, const TerrainGenerationSettings& s) {
    float total = 0.0F;
    float amplitude = 1.0F;
    float freq = s.frequency;
    float maxValue = 0.0F;

    for (int i = 0; i < s.octaves; ++i) {
      glm::vec2 coord = p * freq;
      total += glm::perlin(coord) * amplitude;
      maxValue += amplitude;
      amplitude *= s.persistence;
      freq *= s.lacunarity;
    }
    return total / maxValue;
  }

  void GenerateHeightmapRandom(const TerrainGenerationSettings& s) {
    m_width = s.width;
    m_depth = s.depth;
    m_scale = s.scale;
    m_maxHeight = s.maxHeight;
    m_seed = s.seed;
    m_frequency = s.frequency;
    m_octaves = s.octaves;
    m_persistence = s.persistence;
    m_lacunarity = s.lacunarity;
    m_textureSettings = s.textureSettings;

    m_heightMap.assign(m_width, std::vector<float>(m_depth, 0.0F));

    // добавил генерацию тут, не в цикле (на карту а не точку)
    std::mt19937 gen(m_seed);
    std::uniform_real_distribution<float> dist(0.0F, 10000.0F);
    glm::vec2 offset(dist(gen), dist(gen));

    for (int z = 0; z < m_depth; ++z) {
      for (int x = 0; x < m_width; ++x) {
        glm::vec2 pos = glm::vec2(x, z) * m_scale + offset;
        float h = (FBM(pos, s) + 1.0F) * 0.5F;
        m_heightMap[x][z] = h * m_maxHeight;
      }
    }
  }

  void GenerateMesh() {
    // защита, а то без генерации терреин бывало падало при добавлении пребафов (может и не тут причина)
    if (m_width <= 0 || m_depth <= 0) {
      m_vao.reset();
      return;
    }
    std::vector<float> vertices;
    std::vector<uint32_t> indices;
    // гггг
    vertices.reserve(static_cast<size_t>(m_width) * m_depth * 8);
    indices.reserve(static_cast<size_t>(m_width - 1) * (m_depth - 1) * 6);

    float textureScale = m_textureSettings.textureScale;

    for (int z = 0; z < m_depth; ++z) {
      for (int x = 0; x < m_width; ++x) {
        float worldX = x * m_scale;
        float worldZ = z * m_scale;
        float y = m_heightMap[x][z];

        glm::vec3 normal = {0, 1, 0};
        if (x > 0 && x < m_width - 1 && z > 0 && z < m_depth - 1) {
          float hL = m_heightMap[x - 1][z];
          float hR = m_heightMap[x + 1][z];
          float hD = m_heightMap[x][z - 1];
          float hU = m_heightMap[x][z + 1];
          normal = glm::normalize(glm::vec3(hL - hR, 2.0F * m_scale, hD - hU));
        }

        float u = (static_cast<float>(x) / (m_width - 1)) * textureScale;
        float v = (static_cast<float>(z) / (m_depth - 1)) * textureScale;

        vertices.insert(vertices.end(), {worldX, y, worldZ, normal.x, normal.y, normal.z, u, v});
      }
    }

    for (int z = 0; z < m_depth - 1; ++z) {
      for (int x = 0; x < m_width - 1; ++x) {
        uint32_t i = x + (z * m_width);
        indices.insert(indices.end(), {i, i + m_width, i + 1, i + 1, i + m_width, i + m_width + 1});
      }
    }

    auto vbo = VertexBuffer::Create(vertices.data(), vertices.size() * sizeof(float));
    vbo->SetLayout({{ShaderDataType::Float3, "a_Position"},
                    {ShaderDataType::Float3, "a_Normal"},
                    {ShaderDataType::Float2, "a_TexCoord"}});

    auto ibo = IndexBuffer::Create(indices.data(), indices.size());
    m_vao = VertexArrayObject::Create();
    m_vao->AddVertexBuffer(vbo);
    m_vao->SetIndexBuffer(ibo);
  }

  bool LoadTexture(const std::string &path) {
    m_diffuseTexture = Texture::Create(path);
    if (m_diffuseTexture && m_diffuseTexture->GetRendererID() != 0) {
      m_textureSettings.diffuseMap = path;
      return true;
    }
    m_diffuseTexture.reset();
    return false;
  }

  void UnloadTexture() {
    m_diffuseTexture.reset();
    m_textureSettings.diffuseMap.clear();
  }
  void Cleanup() {
    m_vao.reset();
    m_diffuseTexture.reset();
  }
};

Terrain::Terrain() : m_impl(std::make_unique<PImpl>()) {}
Terrain::~Terrain() = default;

void Terrain::GenerateRandom(const TerrainGenerationSettings& s) {
  m_impl->GenerateHeightmapRandom(s);
  m_impl->GenerateMesh();

  if (!s.textureSettings.diffuseMap.empty()) {
    m_impl->LoadTexture(s.textureSettings.diffuseMap);
  }
}

int Terrain::GetWidth() const {
  return m_impl->m_width;
}
int Terrain::GetDepth() const {
  return m_impl->m_depth;
}

float Terrain::GetHeightAt(float worldX, float worldZ) const {
  // билинейная интерполяция, а то скачет
  float localX = (worldX - m_impl->m_position.x) / m_impl->m_scale;
  float localZ = (worldZ - m_impl->m_position.z) / m_impl->m_scale;
  int x = static_cast<int>(localX);
  int z = static_cast<int>(localZ);
  
  // возвращаю y если за терраин вышли
  if (x < 0 || x >= m_impl->m_width - 1 || z < 0 || z >= m_impl->m_depth - 1) {
    return m_impl->m_position.y;
}
  float fracX = localX - x;
  float fracZ = localZ - z;
  float h00 = m_impl->m_heightMap[x][z];
  float h10 = m_impl->m_heightMap[x + 1][z];
  float h01 = m_impl->m_heightMap[x][z + 1];
  float h11 = m_impl->m_heightMap[x + 1][z + 1];
  float h0 = (h00 * (1 - fracX)) + (h10 * fracX);
  float h1 = (h01 * (1 - fracX)) + (h11 * fracX);
  return m_impl->m_position.y + (h0 * (1 - fracZ) + h1 * fracZ);
}

std::shared_ptr<VertexArrayObject> Terrain::GetVAO() const {
  return m_impl->m_vao;
}
float Terrain::GetScale() const { return m_impl->m_scale; }
float Terrain::GetMaxHeight() const { return m_impl->m_maxHeight; }
uint32_t Terrain::GetSeed() const { return m_impl->m_seed; }
float Terrain::GetFrequency() const { return m_impl->m_frequency; }
int Terrain::GetOctaves() const { return m_impl->m_octaves; }
float Terrain::GetPersistence() const { return m_impl->m_persistence; }
float Terrain::GetLacunarity() const { return m_impl->m_lacunarity; }
glm::vec3 Terrain::GetPosition() const { return m_impl->m_position; }
void Terrain::SetPosition(const glm::vec3 &pos) { m_impl->m_position = pos; }
void Terrain::Cleanup() { m_impl->Cleanup(); }
std::shared_ptr<Texture> Terrain::GetDiffuseTexture() const {
  return m_impl->m_diffuseTexture;
}

const TerrainTextureSettings &Terrain::GetTextureSettings() const {
  return m_impl->m_textureSettings;
}
bool Terrain::LoadTexture(const std::string &path) {
  return m_impl->LoadTexture(path);
}

void Terrain::UnloadTexture() { m_impl->UnloadTexture(); }

}  // namespace engine


===== src/core/scene/Terrain.h =====
#pragma once
#include <cstdint>
#include <glm/glm.hpp>
#include <memory>
#include <string>
namespace engine {
class VertexArrayObject;
class Texture;

struct TerrainTextureSettings {
  std::string diffuseMap = "res/textures/rocky_terrain.jpg";
  float textureScale = 10.0f;
  glm::vec3 specular = glm::vec3(0.3f);
  float shininess = 16.0f;
};
struct TerrainGenerationSettings {
  glm::vec3 position;
  int width = 256;
  int depth = 256;
  uint32_t seed = 12345;
  float maxHeight = 50.0f;
  float frequency = 0.02f;
  int octaves = 6;
  float persistence = 0.5f;
  float lacunarity = 2.0f;
  bool smooth = true;
  float scale = 1.0f;
  TerrainTextureSettings textureSettings;
};
class Terrain {
public:
  Terrain();
  ~Terrain();

  void GenerateRandom(const TerrainGenerationSettings &settings);
  bool LoadHeightmap(const std::string &path);

  float GetHeightAt(float worldX, float worldZ) const;
  glm::vec3 GetNormalAt(float worldX, float worldZ) const;

  std::shared_ptr<VertexArrayObject> GetVAO() const;
  std::shared_ptr<Texture> GetDiffuseTexture() const;
  bool LoadTexture(const std::string &path);
  void UnloadTexture();
  // Геттеры для настроек текстуры
  const TerrainTextureSettings &GetTextureSettings() const;
  void SetTextureSettings(const TerrainTextureSettings &settings);
  int GetWidth() const;
  int GetDepth() const;
  float GetScale() const;
  float GetMaxHeight() const;
  uint32_t GetSeed() const;
  float GetFrequency() const;
  int GetOctaves() const;
  float GetPersistence() const;
  float GetLacunarity() const;
  glm::vec3 GetPosition() const;
  void SetPosition(const glm::vec3 &pos);
  void Cleanup();

private:
  class PImpl;
  std::unique_ptr<PImpl> m_impl;
};
} // namespace engine

  cudaMalloc(&m_agents, max_agents * sizeof(Agent));

  cudaFree(m_agents);

std::vector<Agent> AgentManager::LoadAgentsFromGPU() {
  size_t currentCount = GetAgentCount();
  std::vector<Agent> agents(currentCount);

  cudaMemcpy(agents.data(), GetDeviceAgents(), currentCount * sizeof(Agent),
             cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost);

  cudaError_t err = cudaGetLastError();
  if (err != cudaSuccess) {
    std::println("CUDA memcpy error: {}", cudaGetErrorString(err));
  }
  return agents;
}

// void AgentManager::AddFire(float x, float z, float intensity) {
//   AddFireAt(Float3{x, 0.0f, z}, intensity);
// }

void AgentManager::AddFireAt(const Float3& world, float intensity) {
  if (m_count >= m_capacity)
    return;

  Agent fire{};
  fire.type = BURNING;
  fire.object_id = -1;
  fire.position = world;

  fire.fire_collider.point_count = 1;
  fire.fire_collider.heat_points[0] = world;  // мировая точка источника
  fire.fire_collider.fuels[0] = 100.0F;
  fire.fire_collider.intensities[0] = intensity;

  const float HALF_HEIGHT = FIRE_SOURCE_HEIGHT * 0.5F;

  fire.fire_collider.bounds_min = Float3(world.x - FIRE_SOURCE_BASE_RADIUS, world.y - HALF_HEIGHT,
                                         world.z - FIRE_SOURCE_BASE_RADIUS);

  fire.fire_collider.bounds_max = Float3(world.x + FIRE_SOURCE_BASE_RADIUS, world.y + HALF_HEIGHT,
                                         world.z + FIRE_SOURCE_BASE_RADIUS);

  AddAgent(fire);
}

// void AgentManager::setupFireSourceCollider(Agent& fire, float x, float y) {

//   fire.fire_collider.fuels[0] = 50.0f;
//   fire.fire_collider.intensities[0] = 0.0f;
// }

int AgentManager::AddAgent(const Agent& agent) {
  if (m_count >= m_capacity) {
    std::cerr << "AgentManager: capacity exceeded!" << std::endl;
    return -1;
  }

  cudaMemcpy(m_agents + m_count, &agent, sizeof(Agent), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice);
  return m_count++;
}
void AgentManager::Clear() {
  m_count = 0;
  cudaMemset(m_agents, 0, m_capacity * sizeof(Agent));
}

// Agent AgentManager::CreateAgent(AgentType type, Float3 position,

//   agent.type = type;

//   agent.object_id = components.object_id;
//   agent.intensity = components.intencity;

//   agent.fire_collider.bounds_min = components.collider_bounds_min;
//   agent.fire_collider.bounds_max = components.collider_bounds_max;

//   float scale = components.scale;

//   return agent;
// }

bool AgentManager::RemoveAgentAt(int idx, int* outSwappedFrom, int* outSwappedTo) {
  if (idx < 0 || (size_t)idx >= m_count)
    return false;
  const int last = (int)m_count - 1;
  if (outSwappedFrom)
    *outSwappedFrom = -1;
  if (outSwappedTo)
    *outSwappedTo = -1;

  if (idx != last) {
    // копируем последнего агента на место удаляемого
    cudaMemcpy(m_agents + idx, m_agents + last, sizeof(Agent), cudaMemcpyDeviceToDevice);
    if (outSwappedFrom)
      *outSwappedFrom = last;
    if (outSwappedTo)
      *outSwappedTo = idx;
  }
  // просто уменьшаем "логическое" количество
  --m_count;
  // (опционально) cudaGetLastError проверка
  return true;
}



===== src/core/agent.h =====
#pragma once
#include <glm/glm.hpp>

#include "cuda_include.h"
enum AgentType {
    NORMAL = 0,
    BURNING = 1,   
    BURNED = 2
};

struct Float3 {
    float x, y, z;
    
    __host__ __device__ Float3() : x(0), y(0), z(0) {}
    __host__ __device__ Float3(float x, float y, float z) : x(x), y(y), z(z) {}
    __host__ __device__ static Float3 fromGlm(const glm::vec3& v) {
        return Float3(v.x, v.y, v.z);
    }
    
    __host__ __device__ glm::vec3 toGlm() const {
        return glm::vec3(x, y, z);
    }
    __host__ __device__ float& operator[](int i) { 
        return i == 0 ? x : (i == 1 ? y : z); 
    }
    __host__ __device__ float operator[](int i) const { 
        return i == 0 ? x : (i == 1 ? y : z); 
    }
};

struct FireCollider {
    Float3 bounds_min, bounds_max;
    Float3 heat_points[64];
    float fuels[64];
    float intensities[64];   // "мощность" горения (визуал/источник тепла)
    float temperatures[64];  // температура точки (K или °C — главное, везде одинаково)
    float moisture[64];      // локальная влажность (0..1), пока не используем, но пригодится

        // Материал / физические свойства
    float conductivity;      // базовая теплопроводность коллайдера (дерево/трава)
    float ignition_temp;     // базовый порог воспламенения материала

    int point_count;

    __host__ __device__
    FireCollider()
        : conductivity(0.1f),
          ignition_temp(600.0f),
          point_count(0)
    {
        for (int k = 0; k < 3; ++k) {
            bounds_min[k] = 0.0f;
            bounds_max[k] = 0.0f;
        }
        for (int i = 0; i < 64; ++i) {
            fuels[i]        = 0.0f;
            intensities[i]  = 0.0f;
            temperatures[i] = 293.15f; // ~20°C
            moisture[i]     = 0.3f;    // немного влажности по умолчанию
        }
    }
};

struct Agent {
    Float3 position;
    AgentType type;
    float intensity{};
    int object_id; 
    
    // Универсальный коллайдер для огня
    FireCollider fire_collider;
    
    __host__ __device__
    Agent() : position(), type(NORMAL), object_id(-1) {}
    
    __host__ __device__
    Agent(float x, float y, float z, AgentType type, int id) 
        : position(x, y, z), type(type), object_id(id) {}
    
    __host__ __device__
    bool isBurning() const { 
        for (int i = 0; i < fire_collider.point_count; i++) {
            if (fire_collider.intensities[i] > 0.1f) {
                return true;  
            }
        }
        return false;
    }
    
    __host__ __device__
    float getOverallIntensity() const {
        float max = 0.0f;
        for (int i = 0; i < fire_collider.point_count; i++) {
            if (fire_collider.intensities[i] > max) {
                max = fire_collider.intensities[i];
            }
        }
        return max;
    }
};



===== src/core/Window.cc =====
#include "Window.h"
#include "Input.h"

#include <stdexcept>
namespace engine {
static std::unordered_map<int, engine::KeyCode> keyMap = {
    {GLFW_KEY_W, engine::KeyCode::W},
    {GLFW_KEY_A, engine::KeyCode::A},
    {GLFW_KEY_S, engine::KeyCode::S},
    {GLFW_KEY_D, engine::KeyCode::D},
    {GLFW_KEY_SPACE, engine::KeyCode::Space},
    {GLFW_KEY_ENTER, engine::KeyCode::Enter},
    {GLFW_KEY_ESCAPE, engine::KeyCode::Escape},
    {GLFW_KEY_UP, engine::KeyCode::Up},
    {GLFW_KEY_DOWN, engine::KeyCode::Down},
    {GLFW_KEY_LEFT, engine::KeyCode::Left},
    {GLFW_KEY_RIGHT, engine::KeyCode::Right}};
Window::Window(int width, int height, const std::string &title) {
  if (glfwInit() == 0) {
    throw std::runtime_error("Failed to start GLFW");
  }

  glfwWindowHint(GLFW_CONTEXT_VERSION_MAJOR, 4);
  glfwWindowHint(GLFW_CONTEXT_VERSION_MINOR, 3);
  glfwWindowHint(GLFW_OPENGL_PROFILE, GLFW_OPENGL_CORE_PROFILE);
  m_window = glfwCreateWindow(width, height, title.c_str(), nullptr, nullptr);
  if (m_window == nullptr) {
    glfwTerminate();
    throw std::runtime_error("Failed to initialize window");
  }
  glfwMakeContextCurrent(m_window);
  glfwSetCursorPosCallback(m_window, MousePositionCallback);
  glfwSetMouseButtonCallback(m_window, MouseButtonCallback);
  glfwSetWindowUserPointer(m_window, this);
  glfwSetScrollCallback(m_window, MouseScrollCallback);
}
Window::~Window() {
  if (m_window != nullptr) {
    glfwDestroyWindow(m_window);
  }
  glfwTerminate();
}
void Window::PollEvents() {
  InputSystem::Instance().Update();
  glfwPollEvents();

  for (const auto &[glfwKey, ourKey] : keyMap) {
    bool pressed = glfwGetKey(m_window, glfwKey) == GLFW_PRESS;

    InputSystem::Instance().SetKeyState(ourKey, pressed);
  }
}
bool Window::ShouldClose() const {
  return glfwWindowShouldClose(m_window) != 0;
}
void Window::SwapBuffers() { glfwSwapBuffers(m_window); }
GLFWwindow *Window::GetWindow() const { return m_window; }
} // namespace engine



===== src/core/fire/FireModule.h =====
// core/fire/FireModule.h
#pragma once

#include "FireMaterialLibrary.h"
#include "FireSimulationSystem.h"
#include "core/AgentManager.h"

#include <glm/glm.hpp>
#include <string_view>

class FireModule {
public:
  FireModule();

  FireConfig &Config() { return m_sim.Config(); }
  const FireConfig &Config() const { return m_sim.Config(); }

  FireSimulationSystem &Simulation() { return m_sim; }
  const FireSimulationSystem &Simulation() const { return m_sim; }

  FireMaterialLibrary &Materials() { return m_materials; }
  const FireMaterialLibrary &Materials() const { return m_materials; }

  // Один шаг симуляции для текущих агентов
  void Step(AgentManager &manager, float dt, const glm::vec3 &wind);

  // Применить материал к только что созданному агенту
  void ApplyMaterialToAgent(Agent &agent, std::string_view material_id);

private:
  FireSimulationSystem m_sim;
  FireMaterialLibrary m_materials;
};



===== src/core/fire/FireSimulationSystem.cc =====
// core/FireSimulationSystem.cpp
#include "FireSimulationSystem.h"
#include "core/simulation.cuh"  // SimulationStep(...)

// Конструктор — можно потом добавить загрузку конфига из файла
FireSimulationSystem::FireSimulationSystem() = default;

void FireSimulationSystem::Step(Agent* agents_gpu, int count, float dt) {
  if ((agents_gpu == nullptr) || count <= 0) {
    return;
  }

  // Собираем "готовые" параметры для ядра из доменного конфига
  FireSimulationParams params{};
  BuildSimulationParams(m_config, params);

  // Вектор ветра берём из поля m_wind (его должен установить вызывающий код)
  glm::vec3 wind = m_wind;

  // Пример: усиливаем прогрев по направлению ветра в зависимости от скорости
  const float wind_speed = glm::length(wind);
  params.m_wind_heat_bonus = 1.0f + wind_speed * 0.1f;

  // Вызываем уже существующую CUDA-функцию
  SimulationStep(agents_gpu, count, dt, wind, params);
}




===== src/core/fire/FireConfig.cc =====
// core/FireConfig.cc
#include "FireConfig.h"

FireConfig::FireConfig() : m_default_material_id("wood") {
  m_materials["wood"] = FireMaterialParams{
      .m_ignition_temperature = 600.0F,
      .m_fuel_density         = 100.0F,
      .m_conductivity         = 0.08F,
      .m_base_moisture        = 0.25F,
  };

  m_materials["grass"] = FireMaterialParams{
      .m_ignition_temperature = 500.0F,
      .m_fuel_density         = 40.0F,
      .m_conductivity         = 0.05F,
      .m_base_moisture        = 0.15F,
  };

  m_materials["house"] = FireMaterialParams{
      .m_ignition_temperature = 700.0F,
      .m_fuel_density         = 150.0F,
      .m_conductivity         = 0.12F,
      .m_base_moisture        = 0.20F,
  };
}

FireMaterialParams& FireConfig::Material(const std::string& id) {
  auto it = m_materials.find(id);
  if (it != m_materials.end()) {
    return it->second;
  }
  auto def_it = m_materials.find(m_default_material_id);
  if (def_it != m_materials.end()) {
    return def_it->second;
  }
  // если вдруг и дефолта нет — создаём "пустой" дефолт
  return m_materials[m_default_material_id];
}

const FireMaterialParams& FireConfig::Material(const std::string& id) const {
  auto it = m_materials.find(id);
  if (it != m_materials.end()) {
    return it->second;
  }
  auto def_it = m_materials.find(m_default_material_id);
  if (def_it != m_materials.end()) {
    return def_it->second;
  }
  return m_materials.begin()->second;
}

void BuildSimulationParams(const FireConfig& cfg,
                           FireSimulationParams& out_params) {
  out_params.m_base_check_distance   = 15.0F;
  out_params.m_base_spread_distance  = 3.0F;
  out_params.m_local_spread_distance = 2.0F;

  out_params.m_cross_agent_spread_strength = 10.0F;
  out_params.m_local_spread_strength       = 20.0F;

  // Здесь можешь уже завязаться на cfg.m_env.m_air_humidity,
  // пока оставлены константы:
  out_params.m_humidity_ignition_factor = 1.5F;
  out_params.m_humidity_heat_dampen     = 0.8F;

  // Влияние ветра из конфига
  out_params.m_wind_heat_bonus = 1.0F + (cfg.m_env.m_wind_speed * 0.1F);

  out_params.m_base_conductivity = 0.1F;
  out_params.m_max_heat_distance = 8.0F;
}



===== src/core/fire/FireModule.cc =====
// core/fire/FireModule.cc
#include "FireModule.h"

FireModule::FireModule()
    : m_materials(m_sim.Config()) // начальная копия конфига
{}

void FireModule::Step(AgentManager &manager, float dt, const glm::vec3 &wind) {
  // 1) синхронизируем ветер (можно дёргать из WindConfig снаружи)
  m_sim.SetWind(wind);

  // 2) конфиг мог поменяться из UI -> обновляем библиотеку материалов
  m_materials.SetConfig(m_sim.Config());

  // 3) запускаем CUDA ядро
  m_sim.Step(manager.GetDeviceAgents(), manager.GetAgentCount(), dt);
}

void FireModule::ApplyMaterialToAgent(Agent &agent,
                                      std::string_view material_id) {
  // На всякий случай держим конфиги в синхроне
  m_materials.SetConfig(m_sim.Config());
  m_materials.ApplyToCollider(agent.fire_collider, material_id);
}



===== src/core/fire/FireSimulationParams.h =====
#pragma once

// Набор "готовых" коэффициентов для CUDA-ядра.
// Чистый POD без CUDA-зависимостей.
struct FireSimulationParams {
  // Поиск соседних агентов (объём, в котором смотрим)
  float m_base_check_distance = 15.0F;

  // Радиус распространения от точки к точке между агентами
  float m_base_spread_distance = 3.0F;

  // Радиус распространения внутри одного агента (крона/ствол и т.п.)
  float m_local_spread_distance = 2.0F;

  // Насколько сильно огонь перекидывается между агентами
  float m_cross_agent_spread_strength = 10.0F;

  // Насколько сильно огонь “растекается” по точкам внутри одного агента
  float m_local_spread_strength = 20.0F;

  // На будущее, под физику:
  float m_humidity_ignition_factor =
      1.5F; // как влажность повышает порог воспламенения
  float m_humidity_heat_dampen = 0.8F; // как влажность гасит теплопередачу
  float m_wind_heat_bonus = 2.0F;      // усиление тепла по ветру
  float m_base_conductivity = 0.1F;    // базовая теплопроводность материала
  float m_max_heat_distance = 8.0F;    // макс. расстояние для теплопередачи
};



===== src/core/fire/FireSimulationSystem.h =====
// core/FireSimulationSystem.h
#pragma once

#include "FireConfig.h"
#include "FireSimulationParams.h"
#include "core/agent.h" // Agent*
#include <glm/glm.hpp>

// Обёртка над CUDA-симуляцией огня.
// Scene / UI работают с FireConfig и FireSimulationSystem,
// а не напрямую с SimulationStep(...)
class FireSimulationSystem {
public:
  FireSimulationSystem();

  FireConfig &Config() { return m_config; }
  [[nodiscard]] const FireConfig &Config() const { return m_config; }

  

  void SetWind(const glm::vec3 &wind) { m_wind = wind; }
  [[nodiscard]] const glm::vec3 &Wind() const { return m_wind; }

  // Один шаг симуляции. Agents должны жить уже на GPU.
  void Step(Agent *agents_gpu, int count, float dt);

private:
  FireConfig m_config;
  glm::vec3 m_wind{1.0F, 0.0F, 0.0F};
};



===== src/core/fire/FireMaterialLibrary.cc =====
// core/FireMaterialLibrary.cpp
#include "FireMaterialLibrary.h"

const FireMaterialParams& FireMaterialLibrary::Get(std::string_view id) const {
  // Используем FireConfig::Material, он уже делает fallback на дефолт
  // конвертируем string_view -> string (id маленькиe, это дёшево)
  return m_config.Material(std::string{id});
}

void FireMaterialLibrary::ApplyToCollider(FireCollider& f_collider,
                                          std::string_view material_id) const {
  const FireMaterialParams& m = Get(material_id);

  // Базовые свойства коллайдера
  f_collider.conductivity  = m.m_conductivity;
  f_collider.ignition_temp = m.m_ignition_temperature;

  // Инициализация точек
  for (int i = 0; i < f_collider.point_count; ++i) {  // NOLINT(...)
    f_collider.fuels[i]        = m.m_fuel_density;
    f_collider.intensities[i]  = 0.0F;
    f_collider.temperatures[i] = m_config.m_env.m_ambient_temperature;
    f_collider.moisture[i]     = m.m_base_moisture;
  }
}



===== src/core/fire/FireConfig.h =====
// core/FireConfig.h
#pragma once

#include "FireSimulationParams.h"
#include <glm/glm.hpp>

#include <string>
#include <unordered_map>

// Параметры "материала" префаба (дерево, трава, дом и т.п.)
struct FireMaterialParams {
  float m_ignition_temperature = 600.0F; // °C или K — главное, везде одинаково
  float m_fuel_density         = 100.0F; // условный запас топлива на точку
  float m_conductivity         = 0.1F;   // теплопроводность материала
  float m_base_moisture        = 0.3F;   // типичная влажность 0..1
};

// Параметры окружающей среды (глобальные, одна на всю сцену)
struct FireEnvironmentConfig {
  float m_ambient_temperature    = 293.15F; // ~20°C
  float m_air_humidity           = 0.3F;    // влажность воздуха 0..1
  float m_humidity_dampen_coeff  = 0.8F;    // как влажность гасит теплопередачу
  float m_humidity_ignition_mult = 0.5F;    // как влажность поднимает порог
  float m_wind_speed             = 5.0F;    // м/с, условно
};

// Общий конфиг огня на CPU
struct FireConfig {
  FireEnvironmentConfig m_env;  // ← ОДНО поле

  // Материалы по строковому id: "wood", "grass", "house", "stone" и т.п.
  std::unordered_map<std::string, FireMaterialParams> m_materials;

  // Какой материал считать дефолтным, если id не найден
  std::string m_default_material_id = "wood";

  FireConfig();  // задаёт дефолтные материалы wood/grass/house

  // Удобный доступ по строковому id
  FireMaterialParams& Material(const std::string& id);
  const FireMaterialParams& Material(const std::string& id) const;
};

// Построение GPU-параметров для ядра из доменного конфига.
void BuildSimulationParams(const FireConfig& cfg,
                           FireSimulationParams& out_params);

class FireMaterialLibrary {

      : m_config(std::move(cfg)) {}

  void SetConfig(const FireConfig& cfg) { m_config = cfg; }
  [[nodiscard]] const FireConfig& GetConfig() const { return m_config; }

  // Вернуть параметры материала по строковому id ("wood", "grass", ...)
  [[nodiscard]] const FireMaterialParams& Get(std::string_view id) const;

  // Применить материал к коллайдеру: заполнить fuel/temperature/moisture/cond/ignition
  void ApplyToCollider(FireCollider& f_collider, std::string_view material_id) const;

 private:
  FireConfig m_config;
};


===== src/core/Input.h =====
#pragma once
#include <functional>
#include <glm/glm.hpp>
#include <unordered_map>
namespace engine {

enum class KeyCode {
  W,
  A,
  S,
  D,
  Space,
  Enter,
  Escape,
  Up,
  Down,
  Left,
  Right,
};

enum class MouseButton { Left = 0, Right = 1, Middle = 2 };

enum class InputState { Pressed, Released, Held };

class InputSystem {
public:
  static InputSystem &Instance() {
    static InputSystem inputSystem;
    return inputSystem;
  }
  void Update();
  bool GetKey(KeyCode key, InputState state = InputState::Held) const;
  bool GetKeyDown(KeyCode key) const {
    return GetKey(key, InputState::Pressed);
  }
  bool GetKeyUp(KeyCode key) const { return GetKey(key, InputState::Released); }
  void SetKeyState(KeyCode key, bool pressed);

  glm::vec2 GetMousePosition() const { return m_mousePosition; }
  glm::vec2 GetMouseDelta()    const { return m_mouseDelta; }

  bool GetMouseButton(MouseButton button, InputState state = InputState::Held) const;
  void SetMouseButtonState(MouseButton button, bool pressed);

  void SetMousePosition(float x, float y);

  float GetMouseScrollY() const { return m_mouseScrollY; }
  void  SetMouseScrollY(float yoffset) { m_mouseScrollY = yoffset; }
  void  ResetMouseScroll() { m_mouseScrollY = 0.0f; }
  void SetUICapture(bool mouseCaptured, bool keyboardCaptured) {
    m_uiMouseCaptured = mouseCaptured;
    m_uiKbdCaptured   = keyboardCaptured;
  }
  bool UIMouseCaptured()    const { return m_uiMouseCaptured; }
  bool UIKeyboardCaptured() const { return m_uiKbdCaptured; }

private:
  std::unordered_map<KeyCode, bool> m_currentKeyState;
  std::unordered_map<KeyCode, bool> m_previousKeyState;
  glm::vec2 m_mousePosition;
  glm::vec2 m_mouseDelta;
  
  std::unordered_map<MouseButton, bool>  m_mouseButtonState;
  std::unordered_map<MouseButton, bool>  m_prevMouseButtonState;
  float m_mouseScrollY;

  bool m_uiMouseCaptured{false};
  bool m_uiKbdCaptured{false};
  friend class Window; 
};

} // namespace nova


===== src/core/Application.cc =====
// Application.cc
#include "Application.h"
#include "app/IAppExtension.h"
#include "ui/EditorContext.h"

// clang-format off
// NOLINTBEGIN(llvm-include-order)
#ifndef GLFW_INCLUDE_NONE
#define GLFW_INCLUDE_NONE
#endif
#include <glad/glad.h>
#include <GLFW/glfw3.h>
// NOLINTEND(llvm-include-order)
// clang-format on

// ImGui core + backends

#include "data/SceneSerializer.h"
#include "graphics/TextureCache.h"
#include <core/Window.h>
#include <core/scene/Scene.h>
#include <iostream>
#include <stdexcept>
#include <thread>

namespace engine {
Application::Application(int width, int height, const char *title)
    : m_last_frame_time(static_cast<float>(glfwGetTime())), m_width(width),
      m_height(height) {
  m_window = std::make_unique<Window>(width, height, std::string(title));
  InitGL();

  // Сцена
  m_scene = std::make_unique<Scene>();
  m_scene->Init();
}

engine::Application::~Application() { m_scene->Shutdown(); }

void engine::Application::Shutdown() {
  if (m_scene) {
    m_scene->Shutdown(); // Должен очищать ВСЕ материалы, текстуры, VAO, VBO
  }

  // 2. Очищаем кэш текстур (после очистки сцены)
  TextureCache::Instance().Clear();

  // 3. Очищаем рендерер (шейдеры, etc.)

  // 4. ТОЛЬКО ПОТОМ уничтожаем окно и OpenGL контекст
  m_window.reset();
}

void Application::AddExtension(std::unique_ptr<IAppExtension> ext) {
  m_extensions.push_back(std::move(ext));
}

void Application::Run() {
  for (auto &e : m_extensions)
    e->OnInit(*this);

  while (!ShouldClose()) {
    TickCore();

    // Собираем контекст на кадр (размеры, матрицы и т.п.)
    ui::EditorContext ctx{};
    ctx.m_editor = &m_editor;
    ctx.m_app = this;
    ctx.m_window = GetNativeWindow();
    ctx.m_scene = m_scene.get();
    ctx.m_viewportW = GetViewportWidth();
    ctx.m_viewportH = GetViewportHeight();
    ctx.m_view = m_scene->GetViewMatrix();
    ctx.m_proj = m_scene->GetProjMatrix(ctx.m_viewportW, ctx.m_viewportH);

    // === ВАЖНО: сначала начинаем UI-кадр (NewFrame + построение окон) ===
    for (auto &e : m_extensions)
      e->OnBeginUI(*this, ctx);
    // Теперь ImGui::GetIO().WantCaptureMouse/Keyboard — АКТУАЛЬНЫЕ
    UpdateScene();
    RenderScene();

    // Завершаем UI (Render + RenderDrawData)
    for (auto &e : m_extensions)
      e->OnEndUI(*this, ctx);

    SwapBuffers();
  }

  for (auto &e : m_extensions)
    e->OnShutdown(*this);
}

void Application::InitGL() {
  // активировать GL-контекст окна (если нужно), инициализировать glad
  auto *win = static_cast<GLFWwindow *>(GetNativeWindow());
  glfwMakeContextCurrent(win);

  if (gladLoadGLLoader((GLADloadproc)glfwGetProcAddress) == 0) {
    throw std::runtime_error("Failed to initialize GLAD");
  }
  glViewport(0, 0, m_width, m_height);
}

void Application::TickCore() {
  // dt
  auto now = static_cast<float>(glfwGetTime());
  m_dt = now - m_last_frame_time;
  m_last_frame_time = now;

  // события окна/инпут
  m_window->PollEvents();
}

void Application::UpdateScene() { m_scene->Update(m_dt); }

void Application::RenderScene() { m_scene->Render(); }

void Application::SwapBuffers() { m_window->SwapBuffers(); }

bool Application::ShouldClose() const { return m_window->ShouldClose(); }

GLFWwindow *engine::Application::GetNativeWindow() const {
  return m_window->GetWindow();
}

int Application::GetViewportWidth() const { return m_width; }
int Application::GetViewportHeight() const { return m_height; }
float Application::GetDeltaTime() const { return m_dt; }
Scene &Application::GetScene() { return *m_scene; }

bool Application::SaveScene(const std::string &path) {
  if (!m_serializer) {
    m_serializer = std::make_unique<data::SceneSerializer>(m_scene);
  }
  return m_serializer->Serialize(path);
}

void Application::LoadScene(const std::string &path) {
  if (!m_serializer) {
    m_serializer = std::make_unique<data::SceneSerializer>(m_scene);
  }
  m_serializer->Deserialize(path);
}
} // namespace engine


===== src/core/Input.cc =====
#include "Input.h"
#include <iostream>
void engine::InputSystem::Update() {
  m_previousKeyState       = m_currentKeyState;
  m_prevMouseButtonState   = m_mouseButtonState;

  m_mouseDelta   = glm::vec2(0.0f, 0.0f);
}

bool engine::InputSystem::GetKey(KeyCode key, InputState state) const {
  const bool currentIt  = (m_currentKeyState.count(key) != 0U)  ? m_currentKeyState.at(key)  : false;
  const bool previous = (m_previousKeyState.count(key) != 0U) ? m_previousKeyState.at(key) : false;

  switch (state) {
    case InputState::Held:     return currentIt;
    case InputState::Pressed:  return  currentIt && !previous;
    case InputState::Released: return !currentIt &&  previous;
  }
  return false;
}
void engine::InputSystem::SetMousePosition(float x, float y) {
  glm::vec2 newPos(x, y);
  m_mouseDelta     += (newPos - m_mousePosition); // накапливаем за кадр
  m_mousePosition   = newPos;
}

void engine::InputSystem::SetMouseButtonState(MouseButton button, bool pressed) {
  m_mouseButtonState[button] = pressed;
}


bool engine::InputSystem::GetMouseButton(MouseButton button, InputState state) const {
  const bool currentIt  = m_mouseButtonState.count(button)   ? m_mouseButtonState.at(button)   : false;
  const bool previousIt = m_prevMouseButtonState.count(button)? m_prevMouseButtonState.at(button): false;

  switch (state) {
    case InputState::Held:     return currentIt;
    case InputState::Pressed:  return  currentIt && !previousIt;
    case InputState::Released: return !currentIt &&  previousIt;
  }
  return false;
}
void engine::InputSystem::SetKeyState(KeyCode key, bool pressed)
{
    m_currentKeyState[key] = pressed;
}
