===== src/math/ScreenRay.h =====
#pragma once
#include <cmath>
#include <glm/glm.hpp>
#include <glm/gtc/matrix_inverse.hpp>

namespace math {
inline void ScreenPointToRay(float px, float py, float viewportW,
                             float viewportH, const glm::mat4 &proj,
                             const glm::mat4 &view, glm::vec3 &outOrigin,
                             glm::vec3 &outDir) {
  float x = (2.0F * px / viewportW) - 1.0F;
  float y = 1.0F - (2.0F * py / viewportH);
  glm::vec4 ray_clip(x, y, -1.0F, 1.0F);
  glm::mat4 inv_proj = glm::inverse(proj);
  glm::vec4 ray_eye = inv_proj * ray_clip;
  ray_eye = glm::vec4(ray_eye.x, ray_eye.y, -1.0f, 0.0f);
  glm::mat4 inv_view = glm::inverse(view);
  glm::vec4 ray_world4 = inv_view * ray_eye;
  glm::vec3 ray_world = glm::normalize(glm::vec3(ray_world4));
  outOrigin = glm::vec3(inv_view[3]);
  outDir = ray_world;
}

inline bool IntersectRayPlaneY0(const glm::vec3 &ro, const glm::vec3 &rd,
                                glm::vec3 &hit) {
  if (std::fabs(rd.y) < 1e-6F) {
    return false;
}
  float t = -ro.y / rd.y;
  if (t < 0.0F) {
    return false;
}
  hit = ro + rd * t;
  return true;
}

inline bool IntersectRayPlaneAtDepth(const glm::vec3 &ro, const glm::vec3 &rd,
                                     const glm::vec3 &camDir, float depth,
                                     glm::vec3 &hit) {
  // Плоскость: нормаль = camDir, точка на плоскости = ro + depth * camDir
  glm::vec3 planePoint = ro + depth * camDir;
  float denom = glm::dot(rd, camDir);
  if (std::fabs(denom) < 1e-6f) {
    return false; // Луч параллелен плоскости
  }
  float t = glm::dot(planePoint - ro, camDir) / denom;
  if (t < 0.0f) {
    return false; // Пересечение позади камеры
  }
  hit = ro + rd * t;
  return true;
}
} // namespace math



===== src/data/SceneSerializer.h =====
#pragma once
#include "core/scene/Scene.h"
#include <chrono>
#include <yaml-cpp/emitterdef.h>
#include <yaml-cpp/emittermanip.h>
#include <yaml-cpp/yaml.h>
namespace engine::data {
class SceneSerializer {
public:
  SceneSerializer(std::shared_ptr<Scene> scene);

  bool Serialize(const std::string &path);

  void Deserialize(const std::string &path);

private:
  std::string getCurrentDateTime() {
    auto now = std::chrono::system_clock::now();
    auto in_time_t = std::chrono::system_clock::to_time_t(now);

    std::stringstream ss;
    ss << std::put_time(std::localtime(&in_time_t), "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S");
    return ss.str();
  }
  YAML::Emitter &SerializeAgent(YAML::Emitter &out, const Agent &agent, int id);
  YAML::Emitter &SerializeSceneObject(YAML::Emitter &out,
                                      const SceneObject &obj, int id);

  YAML::Emitter &SerializeFireCollider(YAML::Emitter &out,
                                       const FireCollider &collider);
  SceneObject DeserializeSceneObject(const YAML::Node &obj_node);
  Agent DeserializeAgent(const YAML::Node &agent_node);
  FireCollider DeserializeFireCollider(const YAML::Node &collider_node);
  std::shared_ptr<Scene> m_scene;
};
} // namespace engine::data


===== src/data/ModelData.h =====
#pragma once
#include <assimp/Importer.hpp>
#include <assimp/scene.h>
#include <assimp/postprocess.h>
#include <vector>
#include <glm/glm.hpp>
#include "graphics/Texture.h"
namespace engine::data {
    struct Vertex {
        glm::vec3 position;
        glm::vec3 normal;
        glm::vec2 texCoords;
        glm::vec3 tangent;
        glm::vec3 bitangent;
    };

    struct MeshData {
        std::vector<Vertex> vertices;
        std::vector<uint32_t> indices;
        std::string materialName;
        uint32_t materialIndex;
    };

    struct TextureData {
        std::shared_ptr<engine::Texture> texture;  
        std::string type;                          
        std::string path;                          
        
        bool operator==(const TextureData& other) const {
            return path == other.path;
        }
    };

    struct MaterialData {
        std::string name;
        glm::vec3 diffuseColor;
        glm::vec3 specularColor;
        float shininess;
        std::vector<TextureData> textures;
    };

    struct ModelData {
        std::vector<MeshData> meshes;
        std::vector<MaterialData> materials;
        std::string directory;
        
        // Assimp processing flags
        static const unsigned int IMPORT_FLAGS = 
            aiProcess_Triangulate | 
            aiProcess_GenNormals |
            aiProcess_CalcTangentSpace |
            aiProcess_FlipUVs |
            aiProcess_JoinIdenticalVertices;
    };

    class AssimpLoader {
    public:
        static ModelData LoadModel(const std::string& path);
        
    private:
        static void ProcessNode(aiNode* node, const aiScene* scene, ModelData& modelData);
        static MeshData ProcessMesh(aiMesh* mesh, const aiScene* scene);
        static MaterialData ProcessMaterial(aiMaterial* material, const std::string& directory);
        static std::vector<TextureData> LoadMaterialTextures(
            aiMaterial* mat, aiTextureType type, const std::string& typeName, const std::string& directory);
    };
}


===== src/data/SceneSerializer.cc =====
#include "SceneSerializer.h"
#include "core/scene/Scene.h"
#include <fstream>

#include <iostream>
#include <string>
namespace engine::data {
SceneSerializer::SceneSerializer(std::shared_ptr<Scene> scene)
    : m_scene(scene) {}

bool SceneSerializer::Serialize(const std::string &filepath) {
  try {
    YAML::Emitter out;
    out << YAML::BeginMap;

    // Метаданные
    out << YAML::Key << "metadata" << YAML::Value;
    out << YAML::BeginMap;
    out << YAML::Key << "version" << YAML::Value << "1.0";
    out << YAML::Key << "date" << YAML::Value << getCurrentDateTime();
    out << YAML::EndMap;

    // Агенты
    out << YAML::Key << "agents" << YAML::Value;
    out << YAML::BeginSeq;
    auto agents = m_scene->LoadAgentsFromGPU();
    for (int i = 0; i < agents.size(); i++) {
      SerializeAgent(out, agents[i], i);
    }
    out << YAML::EndSeq;

    // SceneObject'ы
    out << YAML::Key << "scene_objects" << YAML::Value << YAML::BeginSeq;
    auto view = m_scene->GetSceneObjectsView();
    for (auto [entity, transform, render, link] : view.each()) {
      SceneObject obj;
      obj.m_agent_id = link.gpu_index;
      obj.m_object_type = render.prefab_id;
      obj.m_position = transform.position;
      obj.m_scale = render.scale;
      obj.m_is_visible = render.is_visible;

      SerializeSceneObject(out, obj, link.gpu_index);
    }
    out << YAML::EndSeq;
    out << YAML::Key << "terrain" << YAML::Value;
    out << YAML::BeginMap;

    // Получаем настройки из текущего террейна
    auto terrain = m_scene->GetTerrain();
    if (terrain) {
      out << YAML::Key << "width" << YAML::Value << terrain->GetWidth();
      out << YAML::Key << "depth" << YAML::Value << terrain->GetDepth();
      out << YAML::Key << "scale" << YAML::Value << terrain->GetScale();
      out << YAML::Key << "maxHeight" << YAML::Value << terrain->GetMaxHeight();
      out << YAML::Key << "seed" << YAML::Value << terrain->GetSeed();
      out << YAML::Key << "frequency" << YAML::Value << terrain->GetFrequency();
      out << YAML::Key << "octaves" << YAML::Value << terrain->GetOctaves();
      out << YAML::Key << "persistence" << YAML::Value
          << terrain->GetPersistence();
      out << YAML::Key << "lacunarity" << YAML::Value
          << terrain->GetLacunarity();
      out << YAML::Key << "position" << YAML::Value;
      out << YAML::Flow << YAML::BeginSeq << terrain->GetPosition().x
          << terrain->GetPosition().y << terrain->GetPosition().z
          << YAML::EndSeq;
    }
    out << YAML::EndMap;
    out << YAML::EndMap;

    std::ofstream file(filepath);
    file << out.c_str();
    std::cout << "Scene saved to: " << filepath << std::endl;
    return true;

  } catch (const std::exception &e) {
    std::cerr << "Failed to serialize scene: " << e.what() << std::endl;
    return false;
  }
}

YAML::Emitter &
SceneSerializer::SerializeFireCollider(YAML::Emitter &out,
                                       const FireCollider &collider) {
  {
    out << YAML::BeginMap;

    // Bounds
    out << YAML::Key << "bounds_min" << YAML::Value;
    out << YAML::Flow << YAML::BeginSeq << collider.bounds_min.x
        << collider.bounds_min.y << collider.bounds_min.z << YAML::EndSeq;

    out << YAML::Key << "bounds_max" << YAML::Value;
    out << YAML::Flow << YAML::BeginSeq << collider.bounds_max.x
        << collider.bounds_max.y << collider.bounds_max.z << YAML::EndSeq;

    out << YAML::Key << "point_count" << YAML::Value << collider.point_count;

    // Heat points (только используемые)
    out << YAML::Key << "heat_points" << YAML::Value;
    out << YAML::BeginSeq;
    for (int i = 0; i < collider.point_count; i++) {
      out << YAML::Flow << YAML::BeginSeq << collider.heat_points[i].x
          << collider.heat_points[i].y << collider.heat_points[i].z
          << YAML::EndSeq;
    }
    out << YAML::EndSeq;

    // Intensities
    out << YAML::Key << "intensities" << YAML::Value;
    out << YAML::Flow << YAML::BeginSeq;
    for (int i = 0; i < collider.point_count; i++) {
      out << collider.intensities[i];
    }
    out << YAML::EndSeq;

    // Fuels
    out << YAML::Key << "fuels" << YAML::Value;
    out << YAML::Flow << YAML::BeginSeq;
    for (int i = 0; i < collider.point_count; i++) {
      out << collider.fuels[i];
    }
    out << YAML::EndSeq;

    out << YAML::EndMap;

    return out;
  }
}

YAML::Emitter &SceneSerializer::SerializeAgent(YAML::Emitter &out,
                                               const Agent &agent, int id) {
  out << YAML::BeginMap;
  out << YAML::Key << "id" << YAML::Value << id;
  out << YAML::Key << "type" << YAML::Value << static_cast<int>(agent.type);
  out << YAML::Key << "object_id" << YAML::Value << agent.object_id;
  out << YAML::Key << "intensity" << YAML::Value << agent.intensity;

  out << YAML::Key << "position" << YAML::Value;
  out << YAML::Flow << YAML::BeginSeq << agent.position.x << agent.position.y
      << agent.position.z << YAML::EndSeq;

  // FireCollider
  out << YAML::Key << "fire_collider" << YAML::Value;
  SerializeFireCollider(out, agent.fire_collider);

  out << YAML::EndMap;
  return out;
}

YAML::Emitter &SceneSerializer::SerializeSceneObject(YAML::Emitter &out,
                                                     const SceneObject &obj,
                                                     int id) {
  out << YAML::BeginMap;
  out << YAML::Key << "id" << YAML::Value << id;
  out << YAML::Key << "agent_id" << YAML::Value << obj.m_agent_id;
  out << YAML::Key << "object_type" << YAML::Value << obj.m_object_type;
  out << YAML::Key << "is_visible" << YAML::Value << obj.m_is_visible;

  // Позиция и масштаб
  out << YAML::Key << "position" << YAML::Value;
  out << YAML::Flow << YAML::BeginSeq << obj.m_position.x << obj.m_position.y
      << obj.m_position.z << YAML::EndSeq;

  out << YAML::Key << "scale" << YAML::Value;
  out << YAML::Flow << YAML::BeginSeq << obj.m_scale.x << obj.m_scale.y
      << obj.m_scale.z << YAML::EndSeq;

  out << YAML::EndMap;
  return out;
}

void SceneSerializer::Deserialize(const std::string &filename) {
  try {
    YAML::Node scene_data = YAML::LoadFile(filename);

    // Очищаем сцену
    m_scene->Clear();

    // 1. Загружаем агентов
    const YAML::Node &agents_node = scene_data["agents"];
    std::vector<Agent> agents;

    for (const YAML::Node &agent_node : agents_node) {
      agents.push_back(DeserializeAgent(agent_node));
    }

    // Загружаем агентов в GPU
    m_scene->GetAgentManager().SetAgents(agents);

    const YAML::Node &terrain_node = scene_data["terrain"];
    if (terrain_node) {
      TerrainGenerationSettings s;
      s.width = terrain_node["width"].as<int>();
      s.depth = terrain_node["depth"].as<int>();
      s.scale = terrain_node["scale"].as<float>();
      s.maxHeight = terrain_node["maxHeight"].as<float>();
      s.seed = terrain_node["seed"].as<uint32_t>();
      s.frequency = terrain_node["frequency"].as<float>();
      s.octaves = terrain_node["octaves"].as<int>();
      s.persistence = terrain_node["persistence"].as<float>();
      s.lacunarity = terrain_node["lacunarity"].as<float>();
      const YAML::Node &pos_node = terrain_node["position"];
      if (pos_node) {
        glm::vec3 pos(pos_node[0].as<float>(), pos_node[1].as<float>(),
                      pos_node[2].as<float>());
        m_scene->GetTerrain()->SetPosition(pos);
      }
      m_scene->GenerateTerrain(s);
    }
  for (const auto &node : scene_data["scene_objects"]) {
    SceneObject obj = DeserializeSceneObject(node);

    float loaded_scale = obj.m_scale.x; // если scale равномерный

    // здесь AddPrefab внутри создаст агент через PrefabSystem,
    // а в Scene::AddPrefab ты уже вызываешь:
    // m_fire.ApplyMaterialToAgent(agent, prefab.m_fireMaterialId);
    m_scene->AddPrefab(obj.m_object_type, obj.m_position.x,
                       obj.m_position.z, loaded_scale);
  }

  } catch (const std::exception &e) {
    std::cerr << "Failed to deserialize scene: " << e.what() << std::endl;
  }
}
SceneObject
SceneSerializer::DeserializeSceneObject(const YAML::Node &obj_node) {
  SceneObject obj;

  obj.m_agent_id = obj_node["agent_id"].as<int>();
  obj.m_object_type = obj_node["object_type"].as<std::string>();
  obj.m_is_visible = obj_node["is_visible"].as<bool>();

  // Позиция
  const YAML::Node &pos_node = obj_node["position"];
  obj.m_position = glm::vec3(pos_node[0].as<float>(), pos_node[1].as<float>(),
                             pos_node[2].as<float>());

  // Масштаб
  const YAML::Node &scale_node = obj_node["scale"];
  obj.m_scale = glm::vec3(scale_node[0].as<float>(), scale_node[1].as<float>(),
                          scale_node[2].as<float>());

  return obj;
}

FireCollider
SceneSerializer::DeserializeFireCollider(const YAML::Node &collider_node) {
  FireCollider collider;

  const YAML::Node &min_node = collider_node["bounds_min"];
  collider.bounds_min = Float3(min_node[0].as<float>(), min_node[1].as<float>(),
                               min_node[2].as<float>());

  const YAML::Node &max_node = collider_node["bounds_max"];
  collider.bounds_max = Float3(max_node[0].as<float>(), max_node[1].as<float>(),
                               max_node[2].as<float>());

  // Point count
  collider.point_count = collider_node["point_count"].as<int>();

  // Heat points
  const YAML::Node &points_node = collider_node["heat_points"];
  for (int i = 0; i < collider.point_count && i < points_node.size(); i++) {
    const YAML::Node &point_node = points_node[i];
    collider.heat_points[i] =
        Float3(point_node[0].as<float>(), point_node[1].as<float>(),
               point_node[2].as<float>());
  }

  // Intensities
  const YAML::Node &intensities_node = collider_node["intensities"];
  for (int i = 0; i < collider.point_count && i < intensities_node.size();
       i++) {
    collider.intensities[i] = intensities_node[i].as<float>();
  }

  // Fuels
  const YAML::Node &fuels_node = collider_node["fuels"];
  for (int i = 0; i < collider.point_count && i < fuels_node.size(); i++) {
    collider.fuels[i] = fuels_node[i].as<float>();
  }

  return collider;
}

Agent SceneSerializer::DeserializeAgent(const YAML::Node &agent_node) {
  Agent agent;

  agent.type = static_cast<AgentType>(agent_node["type"].as<int>());
  agent.object_id = agent_node["object_id"].as<int>();
  agent.intensity = agent_node["intensity"].as<float>();

  const YAML::Node &pos_node = agent_node["position"];
  agent.position = Float3(pos_node[0].as<float>(), pos_node[1].as<float>(),
                          pos_node[2].as<float>());

  if (agent_node["fire_collider"]) {
    agent.fire_collider = DeserializeFireCollider(agent_node["fire_collider"]);
  }

  return agent;
}
} // namespace engine::data



===== src/data/ModelData.cc =====
#include "ModelData.h"
#include <iostream>

namespace engine::data {
    ModelData AssimpLoader::LoadModel(const std::string& path) {
        Assimp::Importer importer;
        const aiScene* scene = importer.ReadFile(path, ModelData::IMPORT_FLAGS);
        
        if (!scene || scene->mFlags & AI_SCENE_FLAGS_INCOMPLETE || !scene->mRootNode) {
            throw std::runtime_error("Assimp error: " + std::string(importer.GetErrorString()));
        }
        
        ModelData modelData;
        modelData.directory = path.substr(0, path.find_last_of('/'));
        
        for (unsigned int i = 0; i < scene->mNumMaterials; i++) {
            modelData.materials.push_back(
                ProcessMaterial(scene->mMaterials[i], modelData.directory)
            );
        }
        
        ProcessNode(scene->mRootNode, scene, modelData);
        
        return modelData;
    }

    void AssimpLoader::ProcessNode(aiNode *node, const aiScene *scene, ModelData &modelData)
    {
        for(unsigned int i = 0; i < node->mNumMeshes; i++)
        {
            aiMesh* mesh = scene->mMeshes[node->mMeshes[i]];
            modelData.meshes.push_back(ProcessMesh(mesh, scene));
        }
        for(unsigned int i = 0; i < node->mNumChildren; i++)
        {
            ProcessNode(node->mChildren[i], scene, modelData);
        }
    }

    MeshData AssimpLoader::ProcessMesh(aiMesh *mesh, const aiScene *scene)
    {
        MeshData meshData;
        meshData.materialIndex = mesh->mMaterialIndex;
        
        for(unsigned int i = 0; i < mesh->mNumVertices; i++) {
            Vertex vertex;

            // Позиции
            vertex.position = glm::vec3(
                mesh->mVertices[i].x,
                mesh->mVertices[i].y, 
                mesh->mVertices[i].z
            );

            // Нормали
            if(mesh->HasNormals()) {
                vertex.normal = glm::vec3(
                    mesh->mNormals[i].x,
                    mesh->mNormals[i].y,
                    mesh->mNormals[i].z
                );
            }

            // Текстурные координаты (берем первый набор)
            if(mesh->mTextureCoords[0]) {
                vertex.texCoords = glm::vec2(
                    mesh->mTextureCoords[0][i].x,
                    mesh->mTextureCoords[0][i].y
                );
            } else {
                vertex.texCoords = glm::vec2(0.0f);
            }

            // Тангенты и битангенты (если есть)
            if(mesh->HasTangentsAndBitangents()) {
                vertex.tangent = glm::vec3(
                    mesh->mTangents[i].x,
                    mesh->mTangents[i].y,
                    mesh->mTangents[i].z
                );
                vertex.bitangent = glm::vec3(
                    mesh->mBitangents[i].x,
                    mesh->mBitangents[i].y, 
                    mesh->mBitangents[i].z
                );
            }

            meshData.vertices.push_back(vertex);
        }

        // Обрабатываем индексы
        for(unsigned int i = 0; i < mesh->mNumFaces; i++) {
            aiFace face = mesh->mFaces[i];
            for(unsigned int j = 0; j < face.mNumIndices; j++) {
                meshData.indices.push_back(face.mIndices[j]);
            }
        }

        return meshData;
    }
    MaterialData AssimpLoader::ProcessMaterial(aiMaterial* material, const std::string& directory) {
        MaterialData materialData;

        aiString name;
        if (material->Get(AI_MATKEY_NAME, name) == AI_SUCCESS) {
            materialData.name = name.C_Str();
        }

        // Диффузный цвет
        aiColor3D diffuse(0.f, 0.f, 0.f);
        if (material->Get(AI_MATKEY_COLOR_DIFFUSE, diffuse) == AI_SUCCESS) {
            materialData.diffuseColor = glm::vec3(diffuse.r, diffuse.g, diffuse.b);
        }

        // Спекулярный цвет
        aiColor3D specular(0.f, 0.f, 0.f);
        if (material->Get(AI_MATKEY_COLOR_SPECULAR, specular) == AI_SUCCESS) {
            materialData.specularColor = glm::vec3(specular.r, specular.g, specular.b);
        }

        // Блеск
        float shininess = 32.0f;
        if (material->Get(AI_MATKEY_SHININESS, shininess) == AI_SUCCESS) {
            materialData.shininess = shininess;
        }

        auto diffuseMaps = LoadMaterialTextures(material, aiTextureType_DIFFUSE, "texture_diffuse", directory);
        materialData.textures.insert(materialData.textures.end(), diffuseMaps.begin(), diffuseMaps.end());

        auto specularMaps = LoadMaterialTextures(material, aiTextureType_SPECULAR, "texture_specular", directory);
        materialData.textures.insert(materialData.textures.end(), specularMaps.begin(), specularMaps.end());

        auto normalMaps = LoadMaterialTextures(material, aiTextureType_NORMALS, "texture_normal", directory);
        materialData.textures.insert(materialData.textures.end(), normalMaps.begin(), normalMaps.end());
       
        return materialData;
    }

    std::vector<TextureData> AssimpLoader::LoadMaterialTextures(
        aiMaterial* mat, aiTextureType type, const std::string& typeName, const std::string& directory) {
        
        std::vector<TextureData> textures;
        
        for (unsigned int i = 0; i < mat->GetTextureCount(type); i++) {
            aiString str;
            mat->GetTexture(type, i, &str);

            TextureData textureData;
            textureData.type = typeName;
            textureData.path = directory + "/" + str.C_Str();

            textures.push_back(textureData);
        }

        return textures;
    }
}


===== src/data/PrefabSystem.cc =====
#include "PrefabSystem.h"
#include "graphics/TextureCache.h"
#include <algorithm>
#include <filesystem>
#include <fstream>
#include <iostream>
#include <stdexcept>
#include <cmath> // наверху файла, если ещё нет





namespace fs = std::filesystem;
namespace engine::data {

namespace {

// Генерация точек огня для "дерева" по bounds и scale
void GenerateTreeFirePointsFromBounds(const AgentPrefab& prefab,
                                      const glm::vec3& position,
                                      float scale,
                                      FireCollider& c) {
  const glm::vec3 scaled_min = prefab.m_bounds_min * scale;
  const glm::vec3 scaled_max = prefab.m_bounds_max * scale;

  const glm::vec3 world_min = position + scaled_min;
  const glm::vec3 world_max = position + scaled_max;

  const float height = scaled_max.y - scaled_min.y;
  if (height <= 0.0f) {
    // вырожденный объём – одна точка в центре
    const glm::vec3 center = 0.5f * (world_min + world_max);
    c.point_count    = 1;
    c.heat_points[0] = Float3::fromGlm(center);
    c.fuels[0]       = 100.0f;
    c.intensities[0] = 0.0f;
    return;
  }

  c.point_count = 0;
  constexpr int MAX_POINTS = 64;

  // --- ствол: несколько точек по высоте в центре
  const int trunkSegments = 6;
  for (int i = 0; i < trunkSegments && c.point_count < MAX_POINTS; ++i) {
    const float t      = (i + 0.5f) / trunkSegments; // 0..1
    const float yLocal = scaled_min.y + t * height;
    glm::vec3 world_pos = position + glm::vec3(0.0f, yLocal, 0.0f);

    c.heat_points[c.point_count] = Float3::fromGlm(world_pos);
    c.fuels[c.point_count]       = 30.0f;
    c.intensities[c.point_count] = 0.0f;
    ++c.point_count;
  }

  // --- крона: несколько колец по радиусу
  const float radiusX = 0.5f * (scaled_max.x - scaled_min.x);
  const float radiusZ = 0.5f * (scaled_max.z - scaled_min.z);
  const float baseRadius = std::max(radiusX, radiusZ);

  constexpr int   crownLevels      = 3;
  const float     levelHeights[crownLevels]   = {0.3f, 0.6f, 0.85f}; // доли высоты
  const float     levelRadiusMul[crownLevels] = {0.4f, 0.8f, 0.6f};
  constexpr float PI = 3.1415926535f;

  for (int li = 0; li < crownLevels && c.point_count < MAX_POINTS; ++li) {
    const float hLocal = scaled_min.y + levelHeights[li] * height;
    const float r      = baseRadius * levelRadiusMul[li];
    const int   numPts = 8 + li * 4; // 8, 12, 16

    for (int i = 0; i < numPts && c.point_count < MAX_POINTS; ++i) {
      const float a  = 2.0f * PI * (static_cast<float>(i) / numPts);
      const float cx = std::cos(a) * r;
      const float cz = std::sin(a) * r;

      glm::vec3 world_pos = position + glm::vec3(cx, hLocal, cz);

      c.heat_points[c.point_count] = Float3::fromGlm(world_pos);
      c.fuels[c.point_count]       = 20.0f;
      c.intensities[c.point_count] = 0.0f;
      ++c.point_count;
    }
  }

  // на всякий случай – если что-то пошло не так
  if (c.point_count == 0) {
    const glm::vec3 center = 0.5f * (world_min + world_max);
    c.point_count    = 1;
    c.heat_points[0] = Float3::fromGlm(center);
    c.fuels[0]       = 100.0f;
    c.intensities[0] = 0.0f;
  }
}

} // namespace


  
static void PushUnique(std::vector<std::string> &v, const std::string &id) {
  if (std::ranges::find(v, id) == v.end()) {
    v.push_back(id);
  }
}

void PrefabSystem::SavePrefab(const std::string &name,
                              const AgentPrefab &prefab) {
  m_prefabs[name] = prefab;
  PushUnique(m_idsInLoadOrder, name);
}

void PrefabSystem::SavePrefabToFile(const std::string &prefab_name,
                                    const std::string &file_path) {
  if (!PrefabExists(prefab_name)) {
    throw std::runtime_error("Prefab not found:3 " + prefab_name);
  }

  const AgentPrefab &prefab = GetPrefab(prefab_name);

  YAML::Node root;
  root["name"] = prefab_name;
  SerializePrefab(prefab, root);

  fs::path path(file_path);
  fs::create_directories(path.parent_path());

  std::ofstream file(file_path);
  file << root;
  PushUnique(m_idsInLoadOrder, prefab_name);
  std::cout << "Saved prefab '" << prefab_name << "' to " << file_path << '\n';
}
void PrefabSystem::LoadPrefabFromFile(const std::string &file_path) {
  if (!fs::exists(file_path)) {
    throw std::runtime_error("File not found: " + file_path);
  }

  YAML::Node root = YAML::LoadFile(file_path);

  // name из YAML, иначе — из имени файла
  std::string name;
  if (auto n = root["name"]; n && n.IsScalar()) {
    name = n.as<std::string>();
  } else {
    name = fs::path(file_path).stem().string();
  }

  AgentPrefab prefab = DeserializePrefab(root);
  prefab.m_name = name; // <<< ВАЖНО: заполняем поле структуры

  // защита от дубликатов (как минимум лог и замена/пропуск)
  auto it = m_prefabs.find(name);
  if (it != m_prefabs.end()) {
    std::cerr << "[PrefabSystem] duplicate name '" << name << "' from "
              << file_path << " — replacing previous.\n";
  }

  m_prefabs[name] = std::move(prefab);
  PushUnique(m_idsInLoadOrder, name);
  std::cout << "Loaded prefab '" << name << "' from " << file_path << '\n';
}

const AgentPrefab &PrefabSystem::GetPrefab(const std::string &name) const {
  auto it = m_prefabs.find(name);
  if (it == m_prefabs.end()) {
    throw std::runtime_error("Prefab not found:2 " + name);
  }
  return it->second;
}
bool PrefabSystem::PrefabExists(const std::string &name) const {
  return m_prefabs.contains(name);
}

Agent PrefabSystem::CreateAgentFromPrefab(const std::string &prefab_name,
                                          const glm::vec3 &position,
                                          float scale) {
  const AgentPrefab &prefab = GetPrefab(prefab_name);
  return CreateAgentFromPrefab(prefab, position, scale);
}

Agent PrefabSystem::CreateAgentFromPrefab(const AgentPrefab &prefab,
                                          const glm::vec3 &position,
                                          float scale) {
  Agent agent{};
  agent.position  = Float3::fromGlm(position);
  agent.type      = prefab.m_type;
  agent.intensity = prefab.m_physics.m_intencity;

  FireCollider &c = agent.fire_collider;

  // 1) Масштабируем bounds относительно позиции
  glm::vec3 scaled_min = prefab.m_bounds_min * scale;
  glm::vec3 scaled_max = prefab.m_bounds_max * scale;

  c.bounds_min = Float3::fromGlm(position + scaled_min);
  c.bounds_max = Float3::fromGlm(position + scaled_max);

  // 2) Если в префабе явно заданы fire_points – используем их
  if (!prefab.m_firePoints.empty()) {
    c.point_count = std::min<int>(prefab.m_firePoints.size(), 64);

    for (int i = 0; i < c.point_count; ++i) {
      const auto &fp = prefab.m_firePoints[i];

      glm::vec3 scaled_local_pos = fp.m_position * scale;
      glm::vec3 world_pos        = position + scaled_local_pos;

      c.heat_points[i]   = Float3::fromGlm(world_pos);
      c.fuels[i]         = fp.m_initialFuel;
      c.intensities[i]   = 0.0f;
    }

    return agent;
  }

  // 3) Иначе – автогенерация по типу

  // Тип 0 в UI – "Tree", в enum это твой NORMAL
  if (prefab.m_type == AgentType::NORMAL) {
    GenerateTreeFirePointsFromBounds(prefab, position, scale, c);
  } else {
    // простой fallback: одна точка в центре объёма
    const glm::vec3 world_min = position + scaled_min;
    const glm::vec3 world_max = position + scaled_max;
    const glm::vec3 center    = 0.5f * (world_min + world_max);

    c.point_count    = 1;
    c.heat_points[0] = Float3::fromGlm(center);
    c.fuels[0]       = 100.0f;
    c.intensities[0] = 0.0f;
  }

  return agent;
}


void PrefabSystem::CreatePrefabFromAgent(const std::string &name,
                                         const Agent &agent,
                                         const std::string &model_path,
                                         const glm::vec3 &scale) {
  AgentPrefab prefab;
  prefab.m_name = name;
  prefab.m_type = agent.type;
  prefab.m_modelPath = model_path;
  prefab.m_modelScale = scale;
  prefab.m_bounds_min = agent.fire_collider.bounds_min.toGlm();
  prefab.m_bounds_max = agent.fire_collider.bounds_max.toGlm();

  for (int i = 0; i < agent.fire_collider.point_count; i++) {
    AgentPrefab::FirePoint fp;
    fp.m_position =
        glm::vec3(agent.fire_collider.heat_points[i].x - agent.position.x,
                  agent.fire_collider.heat_points[i].y - agent.position.y,
                  agent.fire_collider.heat_points[i].z - agent.position.z);
    fp.m_initialFuel = agent.fire_collider.fuels[i];
    prefab.m_firePoints.push_back(fp);
  }

  prefab.m_physics.m_intencity = agent.intensity;

  m_prefabs[name] = prefab;
  PushUnique(m_idsInLoadOrder, name);
}

std::vector<std::string> PrefabSystem::GetPrefabNames() const {
  std::vector<std::string> result;
  result.reserve(m_prefabs.size());
  std::ranges::transform(m_prefabs, std::back_inserter(result),
                         [](const auto &pair) { return pair.first; });
  return result;
}
std::vector<std::string> PrefabSystem::GetPrefabsByType(AgentType type) const {
  std::vector<std::string> prefabs;

  for (auto [name, prefab] : m_prefabs) {
    if (prefab.m_type == type) {
      prefabs.push_back(name);
    }
  }

  return prefabs;
}
void PrefabSystem::SerializePrefab(const AgentPrefab &prefab,
                                   YAML::Node &node) const {
  node["type"] = static_cast<int>(prefab.m_type);
  node["model_path"] = prefab.m_modelPath;
  node["model_scale"] = std::vector<float>{
      prefab.m_modelScale.x, prefab.m_modelScale.y, prefab.m_modelScale.z};
  node["bounds_min"] = std::vector<float>{
      prefab.m_bounds_min.x, prefab.m_bounds_min.y, prefab.m_bounds_min.z};
  node["bounds_max"] = std::vector<float>{
      prefab.m_bounds_max.x, prefab.m_bounds_max.y, prefab.m_bounds_max.z};

  node["physics"]["intensity"] = prefab.m_physics.m_intencity;

  // 🔥 fire
  {
    YAML::Node fire;
    fire["material"]        = prefab.m_fireMaterialId;
    fire["ignition_mult"]   = prefab.m_fireIgnitionMult;
    fire["humidity_offset"] = prefab.m_fireMoistureOffset;
    node["fire"] = fire;
  }

  // fire_points
  for (const auto &fp : prefab.m_firePoints) {
    YAML::Node fp_node;
    fp_node["position"] =
        std::vector<float>{fp.m_position.x, fp.m_position.y, fp.m_position.z};
    fp_node["initial_fuel"] = fp.m_initialFuel;
    node["fire_points"].push_back(fp_node);
  }

  YAML::Node normal_node;
  YAML::Node burning_node;
  YAML::Node burned_node;

  SerializeMaterialInfo(prefab.normal_material, normal_node);
  SerializeMaterialInfo(prefab.burning_material, burning_node);
  SerializeMaterialInfo(prefab.burned_material, burned_node);

  node["materials"]["normal"]  = normal_node;
  node["materials"]["burning"] = burning_node;
  node["materials"]["burned"]  = burned_node;
}


// устойчивый к неполным файлам
AgentPrefab PrefabSystem::DeserializePrefab(const YAML::Node& node) const {
  AgentPrefab prefab;

  // type
  if (auto t = node["type"]) {
    prefab.m_type = static_cast<AgentType>(t.as<int>());
  } else {
    prefab.m_type = AgentType::NORMAL;
  }

  // model_path
  if (auto mp = node["model_path"]) {
    prefab.m_modelPath = mp.as<std::string>();
  } else {
    prefab.m_modelPath.clear();
  }

  // model_scale (опционален)
  if (auto ms = node["model_scale"]) {
    auto v = ms.as<std::vector<float>>();
    if (v.size() >= 3)
      prefab.m_modelScale = glm::vec3(v[0], v[1], v[2]);
    else
      prefab.m_modelScale = glm::vec3(1.0f);
  } else {
    prefab.m_modelScale = glm::vec3(1.0f);
  }

  // bounds_min / bounds_max
  if (auto bmin = node["bounds_min"]) {
    auto v = bmin.as<std::vector<float>>();
    if (v.size() >= 3)
      prefab.m_bounds_min = glm::vec3(v[0], v[1], v[2]);
  }
  if (auto bmax = node["bounds_max"]) {
    auto v = bmax.as<std::vector<float>>();
    if (v.size() >= 3)
      prefab.m_bounds_max = glm::vec3(v[0], v[1], v[2]);
  }

  // physics.intensity
  if (auto phys = node["physics"]) {
    if (auto inten = phys["intensity"]) {
      prefab.m_physics.m_intencity = inten.as<float>();
    } else {
      prefab.m_physics.m_intencity = 0.0f;
    }
  } else {
    prefab.m_physics.m_intencity = 0.0f;
  }

  // fire_points (опциональны)
  prefab.m_firePoints.clear();
  if (auto fps = node["fire_points"]) {
    for (const auto& fp_node : fps) {
      AgentPrefab::FirePoint fp{};
      if (auto pos = fp_node["position"]) {
        auto v = pos.as<std::vector<float>>();
        if (v.size() >= 3)
          fp.m_position = glm::vec3(v[0], v[1], v[2]);
      }
      if (auto fuel = fp_node["initial_fuel"]) {
        fp.m_initialFuel = fuel.as<float>();
      } else {
        fp.m_initialFuel = 100.0f;
      }
      prefab.m_firePoints.push_back(fp);
    }
  }

  // 🔥 fire: секция для физических параметров огня — максимально безопасно
 prefab.m_fireMaterialId     = "wood";
 prefab.m_fireIgnitionMult   = 1.0F;
 prefab.m_fireMoistureOffset = 0.0F;


  if (node["fire"] && node["fire"].IsMap()) {
    YAML::Node fire = node["fire"];

    if (auto mat = fire["material"]) {
      prefab.m_fireMaterialId = mat.as<std::string>();
    }
    if (auto ign_mult = fire["ignition_mult"]) {
      prefab.m_fireIgnitionMult = ign_mult.as<float>();
    }
    if (auto hum_off = fire["humidity_offset"]) {
      prefab.m_fireMoistureOffset = hum_off.as<float>();
    }
  }

  // materials
  if (node["materials"]["normal"]) {
    prefab.normal_material = ParseMaterialInfo(node["materials"]["normal"]);
  }
  if (node["materials"]["burning"]) {
    prefab.burning_material = ParseMaterialInfo(node["materials"]["burning"]);
  }
  if (node["materials"]["burned"]) {
    prefab.burned_material = ParseMaterialInfo(node["materials"]["burned"]);
  }

  prefab.LoadMaterials();

  return prefab;
}


void PrefabSystem::Clear() {
  m_prefabs.clear();
  m_idsInLoadOrder.clear();
}

void PrefabSystem::LoadAllFromDirectory(const std::string &dir) {
  namespace fs = std::filesystem;
  Clear();

  fs::path root(dir);
  if (!fs::exists(root))
    return;

  for (auto &e : fs::directory_iterator(root)) {
    if (!e.is_regular_file())
      continue;
    const auto ext = e.path().extension().string();
    if (ext != ".yaml" && ext != ".yml")
      continue;

    try {
      LoadPrefabFromFile(e.path().string());
    } catch (const std::exception &ex) {
      std::cerr << "[PrefabSystem] skip " << e.path() << " : " << ex.what()
                << "\n";
      continue;
    }
  }
}

} // namespace engine::data



===== src/data/PrefabSystem.h =====
#pragma once
#include "core/agent.h"
#include "graphics/Material.h"
#include <glm/glm.hpp>
#include <string>
#include <unordered_map>
#include <vector>
#include <yaml-cpp/yaml.h>
namespace engine::data {

struct AgentPrefab {
  std::string m_name;
  AgentType m_type = AgentType::NORMAL;

  glm::vec3 m_modelScale = glm::vec3(1.0F);

  std::string m_modelPath;

  glm::vec3 m_bounds_min = {-1.0F, 0.0F, -1.0F};
  glm::vec3 m_bounds_max = {1.0F, 2.0F, 1.0F};

  struct FirePoint {
    glm::vec3 m_position;
    float m_initialFuel = 100.0F;
  };
  std::vector<FirePoint> m_firePoints;

    // --- НОВОЕ: настройки огня для этого префаба ---
  std::string m_fireMaterialId   = "wood"; // id в FireConfig / FireMaterialLibrary
  float       m_fireIgnitionMult = 1.0F;   // множитель к ignition_temp
  float       m_fireMoistureOffset = 0.0F; // добавка к moisture[i]

  struct Phisics {
    float m_intencity;
  } m_physics;

  struct MaterialInfo {
    std::string diffuse_path;
    std::string specular_path;
    std::string normal_path;
    glm::vec3 diffuse_color = glm::vec3(1.0f, 0.0f, 1.0f);
    glm::vec3 specular_color = glm::vec3(0.5f);
    float shininess = 32.0f;

    // Создает материал из этих путей
    std::shared_ptr<Material> CreateMaterial() const {
      return Material::Create(diffuse_path, specular_path, normal_path,
                              diffuse_color, specular_color, shininess);
    }
  };

  MaterialInfo normal_material;
  MaterialInfo burning_material;
  MaterialInfo burned_material;

  std::shared_ptr<Material> normalMaterial;
  std::shared_ptr<Material> burningMaterial;
  std::shared_ptr<Material> burnedMaterial;

  void LoadMaterials() {
    normalMaterial = normal_material.CreateMaterial();
    burningMaterial = burning_material.CreateMaterial();
    burnedMaterial = burned_material.CreateMaterial();
  }
  std::shared_ptr<Material> GetMaterialForState(AgentType state) const {
    switch (state) {
    case AgentType::BURNED:
      return burnedMaterial;
    case AgentType::BURNING:
      return burningMaterial;
    default:
      return normalMaterial;
    }
  }
};

class PrefabSystem {
public:
  void SavePrefab(const std::string &name, const AgentPrefab &prefab);
  void SavePrefabToFile(const std::string &prefab_name,
                        const std::string &file_path);
  void LoadPrefabFromFile(const std::string &file_path);
  const AgentPrefab &GetPrefab(const std::string &name) const;
  bool PrefabExists(const std::string &name) const;

  Agent CreateAgentFromPrefab(const std::string &prefab_name,
                              const glm::vec3 &position, float scale);
  Agent CreateAgentFromPrefab(const AgentPrefab &prefab,
                              const glm::vec3 &position, float scale);
  void CreatePrefabFromAgent(const std::string &name, const Agent &agent,
                             const std::string &model_path,
                             const glm::vec3 &scale = glm::vec3(1.0F));

  std::vector<std::string> GetPrefabNames() const;
  std::vector<std::string> GetPrefabsByType(AgentType type) const;
  const std::vector<std::string> &ListIds() const { return m_idsInLoadOrder; }

  void LoadAllFromDirectory(const std::string &dir);
  void Clear();
  void ReleaseAllMaterials() {
    for (auto &[name, prefab] : m_prefabs) {
      // ЯВНО обнуляем материалы
      prefab.normalMaterial.reset();
      prefab.burningMaterial.reset();
      prefab.burnedMaterial.reset();
    }
    // И очищаем кэш если есть
    m_prefabs.clear();
  }
  AgentPrefab &GetPrefabMutable(const std::string &name) {
    auto it = m_prefabs.find(name);
    if (it == m_prefabs.end())
      throw std::runtime_error("Prefab not found:1 " + name);
    return it->second;
  }

private:
  AgentPrefab::MaterialInfo ParseMaterialInfo(const YAML::Node &node) const {
    AgentPrefab::MaterialInfo info;

    if (node["diffuse_path"]) {
      info.diffuse_path = node["diffuse_path"].as<std::string>();
    }
    if (node["specular_path"]) {
      info.specular_path = node["specular_path"].as<std::string>();
    }
    if (node["normal_path"]) {
      info.normal_path = node["normal_path"].as<std::string>();
    }
    if (node["diffuse_color"]) {
      info.diffuse_color = ParseVec3(node["diffuse_color"], glm::vec3(0.8f));
    }
    if (node["specular_color"]) {
      info.specular_color = ParseVec3(node["specular_color"], glm::vec3(0.5f));
    }
    if (node["shininess"]) {
      info.shininess = node["shininess"].as<float>(32.0f);
    }

    return info;
  }

  void SerializeMaterialInfo(const AgentPrefab::MaterialInfo &info,
                             YAML::Node &node) const {
    node["diffuse_path"] = info.diffuse_path;
    node["specular_path"] = info.specular_path;
    node["normal_path"] = info.normal_path;

    // inline массив для цвета
    YAML::Node diffuseNode(YAML::NodeType::Sequence);
    diffuseNode.push_back(info.diffuse_color.x);
    diffuseNode.push_back(info.diffuse_color.y);
    diffuseNode.push_back(info.diffuse_color.z);
    diffuseNode.SetStyle(YAML::EmitterStyle::Flow);
    node["diffuse_color"] = diffuseNode;

    YAML::Node specularNode(YAML::NodeType::Sequence);
    specularNode.push_back(info.specular_color.x);
    specularNode.push_back(info.specular_color.y);
    specularNode.push_back(info.specular_color.z);
    specularNode.SetStyle(YAML::EmitterStyle::Flow);
    node["specular_color"] = specularNode;

    node["shininess"] = info.shininess;
  }

  //   void SerializeMaterialInfo(const AgentPrefab::MaterialInfo& info,
  //   YAML::Node& node) const {
  //     node["diffuse_path"] = info.diffuse_path;
  //     node["specular_path"] = info.specular_path;
  //     node["normal_path"] = info.normal_path;

  //     node["diffuse_color"] = YAML::Node(YAML::NodeType::Sequence);
  //     node["diffuse_color"].push_back(info.diffuse_color.x);
  //     node["diffuse_color"].push_back(info.diffuse_color.y);
  //     node["diffuse_color"].push_back(info.diffuse_color.z);

  //     node["specular_color"] = YAML::Node(YAML::NodeType::Sequence);
  //     node["specular_color"].push_back(info.specular_color.x);
  //     node["specular_color"].push_back(info.specular_color.y);
  //     node["specular_color"].push_back(info.specular_color.z);

  //     node["shininess"] = info.shininess;
  //   }
  static glm::vec3 ParseVec3(const YAML::Node &node,
                             const glm::vec3 &default_value = glm::vec3(0.0f)) {
    if (!node || !node.IsSequence() || node.size() != 3) {
      return default_value;
    }

    try {
      return glm::vec3(node[0].as<float>(), node[1].as<float>(),
                       node[2].as<float>());
    } catch (...) {
      return default_value;
    }
  }
  void SerializePrefab(const AgentPrefab &prefab, YAML::Node &node) const;

  AgentPrefab DeserializePrefab(const YAML::Node &node) const;
  std::unordered_map<std::string, AgentPrefab> m_prefabs;

  std::vector<std::string> m_idsInLoadOrder;
};
} // namespace engine::data


===== src/ui/tools/PlaceOnGroundTool.cc =====
#include "../tools/PlaceOnGroundTool.h"

#include <GLFW/glfw3.h>
#include <cmath>
#include <glm/glm.hpp>
#include <glm/gtc/matrix_inverse.hpp>

#include "core/Input.h"
#include "core/scene/Scene.h"
#include "math/ScreenRay.h"
#include "ui/EditorContext.h"

namespace ui {

// ── ITool ─────────────────────────────────────────────
ToolType PlaceOnGroundTool::Type() const noexcept {
  return ToolType::PLACE_ON_GROUND;
}
const char *PlaceOnGroundTool::Name() const noexcept {
  return "Place on ground";
}
const char *PlaceOnGroundTool::Id() const noexcept {
  return "Tool.PlaceOnGround";
}

// ── Подметоды ─────────────────────────────────────────
glm::dvec2 PlaceOnGroundTool::ReadMouseWindow(GLFWwindow *win) {
  double mx = 0.0;
  double my = 0.0;
  glfwGetCursorPos(win, &mx, &my);
  return {mx, my};
}

bool PlaceOnGroundTool::WindowToViewportLogical(const EditorContext &ctx,
                                                const glm::dvec2 &mouseWin,
                                                glm::vec2 &outVpLogical) {
  const glm::vec2 VP_L = ctx.WindowToViewportLogical(
      static_cast<float>(mouseWin.x), static_cast<float>(mouseWin.y));
  const bool INSIDE =
      (VP_L.x >= 0.F && VP_L.y >= 0.F && VP_L.x < static_cast<float>(ctx.vpW) &&
       VP_L.y < static_cast<float>(ctx.vpH));
  if (INSIDE) {
    outVpLogical = VP_L;
  }
  return INSIDE;
}

glm::vec2 PlaceOnGroundTool::ToFramebufferPx(const EditorContext &ctx,
                                             const glm::vec2 &vpLogical) {
  return {vpLogical.x * ctx.m_vpScaleX, vpLogical.y * ctx.m_vpScaleY};
}

void PlaceOnGroundTool::BuildScreenRay(const EditorContext &ctx,
                                       const glm::vec2 &px,
                                       glm::vec3 &outOrigin,
                                       glm::vec3 &outDir) {
  const auto FB_W = static_cast<float>(ctx.m_viewportW);
  const auto FB_H = static_cast<float>(ctx.m_viewportH);
  math::ScreenPointToRay(px.x, px.y, FB_W, FB_H, ctx.m_proj, ctx.m_view,
                         outOrigin, outDir);
}

bool PlaceOnGroundTool::RaycastPlaneY0(const glm::vec3 &ro, const glm::vec3 &rd,
                                       glm::vec3 &outHit) {
  return math::IntersectRayPlaneY0(ro, rd, outHit);
}

bool PlaceOnGroundTool::RaycastTerrain(const engine::Terrain &T,
                                       const glm::vec3 &ro, const glm::vec3 &rd,
                                       float anchorY, glm::vec3 &outHit) {
  auto f = [&](float t) -> float {
    const glm::vec3 p = ro + rd * t;
    return p.y - (T.GetHeightAt(p.x, p.z) + anchorY);
  };

  const float T_MAX = 5000.F;
  const float STEP = 2.F;

  float t0 = 0.F;
  float v0 = f(t0);

  for (float t = STEP; t <= T_MAX; t += STEP) {
    float v1 = f(t);
    const bool SIGN_CHANGE = (v0 > 0.F && v1 <= 0.F) || (v0 < 0.F && v1 >= 0.F);
    if (SIGN_CHANGE) {
      float a = t - STEP;
      float b = t;
      for (int i = 0; i < 24; ++i) {
        float m = 0.5F * (a + b);
        float vm = f(m);
        if ((v0 > 0.F && vm > 0.F) || (v0 < 0.F && vm < 0.F)) {
          a = m;
          v0 = vm;
        } else {
          b = m;
        }
      }
      const float THIT = 0.5F * (a + b);
      outHit = ro + rd * THIT;
      return true;
    }
    t0 = t;
    v0 = v1;
  }
  return false;
}

// ── Основной тик ──────────────────────────────────────
void PlaceOnGroundTool::OnUpdate(EditorContext &ctx) {
  if (engine::InputSystem::Instance().UIMouseCaptured()) {
    return;
  }
  // игнорируем, если нажата клавиша Ctrl
  if (glfwGetKey(ctx.m_window, GLFW_KEY_LEFT_CONTROL) == GLFW_PRESS ||
      glfwGetKey(ctx.m_window, GLFW_KEY_RIGHT_CONTROL) == GLFW_PRESS) {
    return;
  }
  if (!engine::InputSystem::Instance().GetMouseButton(
          engine::MouseButton::Left, engine::InputState::Pressed)) {
    return;
  }

  // 1) курсор окна
  const glm::dvec2 MOUSE_WIN = ReadMouseWindow(ctx.m_window);

  // 2) окно → панель (логические px)
  glm::vec2 vpLogical{};
  const bool INSIDE = WindowToViewportLogical(ctx, MOUSE_WIN, vpLogical);

  if (!INSIDE) {
    return;
  }

  // 3) логические → framebuffer px
  const glm::vec2 PX = ToFramebufferPx(ctx, vpLogical);

  // 4) луч из пикселя FB
  glm::vec3 ro{};
  glm::vec3 rd{};
  BuildScreenRay(ctx, PX, ro, rd);

  // 5) сначала — попытка удаления (ПКМ или Ctrl+ЛКМ)
  if (HandleDeleteClick(ctx, ro, rd))
    return;

  // 6) далее — ИСХОДНАЯ логика: плейсмент только при ЛКМ
  if (!engine::InputSystem::Instance().GetMouseButton(
          engine::MouseButton::Left, engine::InputState::Pressed))
    return;

  // 6a) террейн (если есть) — ставим точно по земле
  glm::vec3 hitTerrain{};
  bool hitT = false;
  float anchorY = 0.0f;
  if (ctx.m_scene->GetTerrain() != nullptr) {
    if (!ctx.State().m_selectedPrefab.empty()) {
      const auto &prefab = ctx.m_scene->GetPrefabSystem().GetPrefab(
          ctx.State().m_selectedPrefab);
      anchorY = -prefab.m_bounds_min.y * prefab.m_modelScale.y;
    }
    hitT =
        RaycastTerrain(*ctx.m_scene->GetTerrain(), ro, rd, anchorY, hitTerrain);
  }
  if (hitT) {
    if (ctx.State().m_placeKind == EditorState::PlaceKind::FIRE) {
      ctx.m_scene->AddFireAt(hitTerrain, ctx.State().m_fireIntensity);
    } else {
      ctx.m_scene->AddPrefab(ctx.State().m_selectedPrefab, hitTerrain.x,
                             hitTerrain.z, 1);
    }
    return;
  }

  // 6b) фолбек: Y=0 (как у вас было)
  glm::vec3 hitPlane{};
  if (!RaycastPlaneY0(ro, rd, hitPlane)) {
    return;
  }

  if (ctx.State().m_placeKind == EditorState::PlaceKind::FIRE) {
    ctx.m_scene->AddFire(hitPlane.x, hitPlane.z, ctx.State().m_fireIntensity);
  } else {
    ctx.m_scene->AddPrefab(ctx.State().m_selectedPrefab, hitPlane.x, hitPlane.z,
                           1);
  }
}

bool PlaceOnGroundTool::IsDeleteGesture() noexcept {
  using engine::InputState;
  using engine::InputSystem;
  using engine::KeyCode;
  using engine::MouseButton;

  const bool RMB_PRESSED = InputSystem::Instance().GetMouseButton(
      MouseButton::Right, InputState::Pressed);
  const bool SPACE_LMB_PRESSED =
      InputSystem::Instance().GetKey(KeyCode::Space) &&
      InputSystem::Instance().GetMouseButton(MouseButton::Left,
                                             InputState::Pressed);
  return RMB_PRESSED || SPACE_LMB_PRESSED;
}

// ── Обработчик удаления ────────────────────────────
bool PlaceOnGroundTool::HandleDeleteClick(EditorContext &ctx,
                                          const glm::vec3 &ro,
                                          const glm::vec3 &rd) {
  if (!IsDeleteGesture()) {
    return false;
  }

  glm::vec3 hit{};
  bool got = false;

  // Предпочтительно — точка на террейне
  if (ctx.m_scene->GetTerrain() != nullptr) {
    got = RaycastTerrain(*ctx.m_scene->GetTerrain(), ro, rd, /*anchorY=*/0.0f,
                         hit);
  }
  // Фолбек — плоскость Y=0
  if (!got) {
    got = RaycastPlaneY0(ro, rd, hit);
  }
  if (!got)
    return true; // жест был, но по лучу ничего не попали — считать обработанным

  // Удаляем ближайший объект (или огонь) рядом с hit.xz
  // ВАЖНО: метод сцены должен существовать (см. нашу предыдущую заготовку
  // RemoveNearestAt)
  ctx.m_scene->RemoveNearestAt(hit, kPickRadiusXZ);
  return true;
}

} // namespace ui



===== src/ui/tools/PlaceOnGroundTool.h =====
#pragma once
#include "../ITool.h"
#include "ui/ToolType.h"
#include <string_view> 

struct GLFWwindow;
namespace engine { class Terrain; }

namespace ui {

class PlaceOnGroundTool final : public ITool {
public:
  explicit PlaceOnGroundTool(std::string_view /*primaryModelPath*/) noexcept {}

  // ITool (не меняем)
  [[nodiscard]] ToolType    Type() const noexcept override;
  [[nodiscard]] const char* Name() const noexcept override;
  [[nodiscard]] const char* Id()   const noexcept override;

  void OnActivate(EditorContext& /*ctx*/)   override {}
  void OnDeactivate(EditorContext& /*ctx*/) override {}
  void OnUpdate(EditorContext& ctx) override;

  // Переключатель: false — плоскость Y=0 (как сейчас), true — точный raycast по террейну
  void UseTerrainRaycast(bool v) noexcept { m_useTerrain = v; }

private:

  // 1) курсор окна (логические координаты окна)
  [[nodiscard]] static glm::dvec2 ReadMouseWindow(GLFWwindow* win) ;

  // 2) окно → панель (логические пиксели панели); false если курсор вне панели
  [[nodiscard]] static bool WindowToViewportLogical(const EditorContext& ctx,
                                             const glm::dvec2& mouseWin,
                                             glm::vec2& outVpLogical);

  // 3) логические пиксели панели → framebuffer-пиксели (учёт HiDPI)
  [[nodiscard]] static glm::vec2 ToFramebufferPx(const EditorContext& ctx,
                                          const glm::vec2& vpLogical);

  // 4) построить луч из пикселя фреймбуфера
  static void BuildScreenRay(const EditorContext& ctx, const glm::vec2& px,
                      glm::vec3& outOrigin, glm::vec3& outDir);

  // 5а) пересечение с плоскостью Y=0
  [[nodiscard]] static bool RaycastPlaneY0(const glm::vec3& ro, const glm::vec3& rd,
                                    glm::vec3& outHit);

  // 5б) пересечение с террейном (шаговый поиск + бинарка), anchorY = -bounds_min.y*scale.y
  [[nodiscard]] static bool RaycastTerrain(const engine::Terrain& T,
                                    const glm::vec3& ro, const glm::vec3& rd,
                                    float anchorY,
                                    glm::vec3& outHit);


     // Возвращает true, если удаление обработано (и дальнейшая логика не нужна)
  bool HandleDeleteClick(EditorContext& ctx, const glm::vec3& ro, const glm::vec3& rd);

  // Жест удаления: ПКМ или Ctrl+ЛКМ
  static bool IsDeleteGesture() noexcept;

  // Радиус подбора по XZ при удалении (можете подвинтить)
  static constexpr float kPickRadiusXZ = 3.0f;                           

  bool m_useTerrain{false};
};

} // namespace ui

===== src/ui/Editor.h =====
// ui/Editor.h
#pragma once
#include <memory>
#include <ranges>
#include <type_traits>
#include <vector>

#include "ui/EditorState.h"
#include "ui/IModule.h"
#include "ui/IPanel.h"
#include "ui/ITool.h"
// не инклюдим IModule тут — достаточно fwd-decl
namespace ui {
struct IModule;
} // namespace ui

namespace ui {
  struct EditorContext;

class Editor {
public:
  Editor() = default;

  // Запрет копирования
  Editor(const Editor &) = delete;
  Editor &operator=(const Editor &) = delete;

  // Перемещение: переносим владение, но сбрасываем стек (переактивация позже)
  Editor(Editor &&other) noexcept
      : m_panels(std::move(other.m_panels)), m_tools(std::move(other.m_tools)),
        m_modules(std::move(other.m_modules)) {
    m_activeStack.clear();
  }

  Editor &operator=(Editor &&other) noexcept {
    if (this != &other) {
      ShutdownModules(); // на всякий случай корректно выгружаем свои
      m_panels = std::move(other.m_panels);
      m_tools = std::move(other.m_tools);
      m_modules = std::move(other.m_modules);
      m_activeStack.clear();
    }
    return *this;
  }

  ~Editor() {
    // Гарантируем Shutdown модулей перед разрушением редактора
    for (auto &m_module : std::ranges::reverse_view(m_modules)) {
      m_module->Shutdown();
    }
  }

  // --- Регистрация панелей/инструментов (как было) ---
  void RegisterPanel(std::unique_ptr<IPanel> panel) {
    m_panels.emplace_back(std::move(panel));
  }
  void RegisterTool(std::unique_ptr<ITool> tool) {
    m_tools.emplace_back(std::move(tool));
  }

  template <class T> T *GetTool() {
    static_assert(std::is_base_of_v<ITool, T>, "T must be ITool");
    for (auto &up : m_tools) {
      if (auto *tool_pointer = dynamic_cast<T *>(up.get())) {
        return tool_pointer;
      }
    }
    return nullptr;
  }
  template <class T> const T *GetTool() const {
    static_assert(std::is_base_of_v<ITool, T>, "T must be ITool");
    for (const auto &up : m_tools) {
      if (auto *tool_pointer = dynamic_cast<const T *>(up.get())) {
        return tool_pointer;
      }
    }
    return nullptr;
  }

  // --- Модули ---
  void RegisterModule(std::unique_ptr<IModule> module) {
    m_modules.emplace_back(std::move(module));
    m_modules.back()->Startup(*this);
  }

  // При желании — явная остановка модулей до деструктора
  void ShutdownModules() {
    for (auto &m_module : std::ranges::reverse_view(m_modules)) {
      m_module->Shutdown();
    }
  }

  // --- Активные инструменты (как уже сделали) ---
  void SetActiveTool(ToolType type, EditorContext &ctx) {
    if (!m_activeStack.empty()) {
      m_activeStack.back()->OnDeactivate(ctx);
      m_activeStack.clear();
    }
    if (auto *tool = FindTool(type)) {
      m_activeStack.push_back(tool);
      tool->OnActivate(ctx);
    }
  }
  void PushTool(ToolType type, EditorContext &ctx) {
    if (auto *tool = FindTool(type)) {
      if (!m_activeStack.empty()) {
        m_activeStack.back()->OnDeactivate(ctx);
      }
      m_activeStack.push_back(tool);
      tool->OnActivate(ctx);
    }
  }
  void PopTool(EditorContext &ctx) {
    if (m_activeStack.empty()) {
      return;
    }
    m_activeStack.back()->OnDeactivate(ctx);
    m_activeStack.pop_back();
    if (!m_activeStack.empty()) {
      m_activeStack.back()->OnActivate(ctx);
    }
  }

  void DrawUI(EditorContext &ctx) {
    ctx.m_editor = this;
    for (auto &panel : m_panels) {
      panel->Draw(ctx);
    }
  }
  void TickTools(EditorContext &ctx) {
    if (!m_activeStack.empty()) {
      m_activeStack.back()->OnUpdate(ctx);
    }
  }

  [[nodiscard]] ToolType ActiveToolType() const {
    return m_activeStack.empty() ? ToolType::OTHER_TOOL
                                 : m_activeStack.back()->Type();
  }
  EditorState m_state;

private:
  ITool *FindTool(ToolType type) {
    for (auto &tool : m_tools) {
      if (tool->Type() == type) {
        return tool.get();
      }
    }
    return nullptr;
  }

  std::vector<std::unique_ptr<IPanel>> m_panels;
  std::vector<std::unique_ptr<ITool>> m_tools;
  std::vector<ITool *> m_activeStack;
  std::vector<std::unique_ptr<IModule>> m_modules; // <-- NEW
};

} // namespace ui

===== src/ui/EditorContext.h =====
#pragma once
#include "ui/EditorState.h"
#include "ui/ToolType.h"
#include <glm/glm.hpp>

struct GLFWwindow;
namespace engine {
class Scene;
class Camera;
class Application;
} // namespace engine

namespace ui {
class Editor; // <-- только форвард!

struct EditorContext {
  engine::Application *m_app = nullptr;
  GLFWwindow *m_window = nullptr;
  engine::Scene *m_scene = nullptr;
  const engine::Camera *m_camera = nullptr;
  bool m_sceneChanged = false;
  glm::mat4 m_view{}, m_proj{};
  int m_viewportW = 1920, m_viewportH = 1080;
  int vpX{0}, vpY{0}, vpW{1920}, vpH{1080};
  float m_vpScaleX{1.F}, m_vpScaleY{1.F};

  Editor *m_editor = nullptr; // не владеем

  // Только объявления — БЕЗ ТЕЛ в .h!
  EditorState &State();
  [[nodiscard]] const EditorState &State() const;

  [[nodiscard]] ToolType GetActiveToolType() const noexcept;

  [[nodiscard]] glm::vec2 WindowToViewportLogical(float mx, float my) const {
    return {mx - static_cast<float>(vpX), my - static_cast<float>(vpY)};
  }
  [[nodiscard]] glm::vec2 WindowToViewportFB(float mx, float my) const {
    return {(mx - static_cast<float>(vpX)) * m_vpScaleX,
            (my - static_cast<float>(vpY)) * m_vpScaleY};
  }
};
} // namespace ui



===== src/ui/ui_utils/SpatialGrid.h =====
// ui/utils/SpatialGrid.h
#pragma once
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdint>
#include <unordered_map>
#include <vector>

#include <glm/geometric.hpp>  // glm::dot
#include <glm/vec2.hpp>

namespace ui::util {

struct SpatialGrid {
  float cell = 1.0f;  // шаг сетки, обычно >= minSpacing
  std::unordered_map<std::int64_t, std::vector<glm::vec2>> buckets;

  static constexpr std::int64_t Key(int gx, int gy) noexcept {
    return (static_cast<std::int64_t>(gx) << 32) ^ static_cast<std::int64_t>(gy & 0xFFFFFFFF);
  }

  static constexpr std::pair<int, int> ToCell(const glm::vec2& p, float cell) noexcept {
    return {static_cast<int>(std::floor(p.x / cell)), static_cast<int>(std::floor(p.y / cell))};
  }

  void SetCell(float c) noexcept { cell = (c > 0.f ? c : 1.f); }
  void Clear() noexcept { buckets.clear(); }
  void Reserve(std::size_t nCells) { buckets.reserve(nCells); }  // опционально

  // Возвращает true, если точка принята (нет ближних соседей ближе minSpacing)
  bool Accept(const glm::vec2& p, float minSpacing) {
    const auto [cx, cy] = ToCell(p, cell);
    const float r2 = minSpacing * minSpacing;

    for (int dy = -1; dy <= 1; ++dy) {
      for (int dx = -1; dx <= 1; ++dx) {
        const auto it = buckets.find(Key(cx + dx, cy + dy));
        if (it == buckets.end())
          continue;
        for (const glm::vec2& q : it->second) {
          const glm::vec2 d = p - q;
          if (glm::dot(d, d) < r2)
            return false;
        }
      }
    }
    buckets[Key(cx, cy)].emplace_back(p);
    return true;
  }
};

}  // namespace ui::util
