===== src/ui/panels/FoliageBrushTool.cc =====
// ui/tools/FoliageBrushTool.cc
#include "FoliageBrushTool.h"
#include <GLFW/glfw3.h>
#include <imgui.h>
#include <cmath>
#include <numbers>
#include "core/Input.h"
#include "core/scene/Scene.h"
#include "math/ScreenRay.h"
#include "ui/EditorContext.h"
#include "ui/ui_utils/SpatialGrid.h"

using engine::InputState;
using engine::MouseButton;
using Grid = ui::util::SpatialGrid;

namespace ui {

static float ComputeAnchorY(const ui::EditorContext& ctx) {
  float anchorY = 0.f;
  if ((ctx.m_scene != nullptr) && !ctx.State().m_selectedPrefab.empty()) {
    const auto& id = ctx.State().m_selectedPrefab;
    const auto& P = ctx.m_scene->GetPrefabSystem().GetPrefab(id);
    anchorY = -P.m_bounds_min.y * P.m_modelScale.y;
  }
  return anchorY;
}

static bool RaycastTerrain(const engine::Terrain& T, const glm::vec3& ro, const glm::vec3& rd,
                           float anchorY, glm::vec3& outHit) {
  auto f = [&](float t) {
    glm::vec3 p = ro + rd * t;
    return p.y - (T.GetHeightAt(p.x, p.z) + anchorY);
  };
  const float T_MAX = 5000.F;
  const float STEP = 2.F;
  float t0 = 0.F;
  float v0 = f(t0);
  for (float t = STEP; t <= T_MAX; t += STEP) {
    float v1 = f(t);
    if ((v0 > 0.F && v1 <= 0.F) || (v0 < 0.F && v1 >= 0.F)) {
      float a = t - STEP;
      float b = t;
      for (int i = 0; i < 24; ++i) {
        float m = 0.5F * (a + b);
        float vm = f(m);
        if ((v0 > 0.f && vm > 0.f) || (v0 < 0.f && vm < 0.f)) {
          a = m;
          v0 = vm;
        } else {
          b = m;
        }
      }
      float thit = 0.5f * (a + b);
      outHit = ro + rd * thit;
      return true;
    }
    t0 = t;
    v0 = v1;
  }
  return false;
}

void FoliageBrushTool::OnActivate(EditorContext& /*ctx*/) {
  m_preview.clear();
  m_dragging = false;
  EnsureSeed();
}

void FoliageBrushTool::EnsureSeed() {
  m_rng.seed(m_settings.seed);
}

bool FoliageBrushTool::ScreenToGround(EditorContext& ctx, float mx, float my, glm::vec3& outHit) {
  // как в PlaceOnGroundTool: окно → viewport → луч → пересечение Y=0
  double dx = 0.0;
  double dy = 0.0;
  dx = mx;
  dy = my;

  const glm::vec2 vp = ctx.WindowToViewportFB(static_cast<float>(dx), static_cast<float>(dy));
  if (vp.x < 0.f || vp.y < 0.f || vp.x >= (float)ctx.vpW || vp.y >= (float)ctx.vpH) {
    return false;
  }

  const float x_ndc = ((vp.x / static_cast<float>(ctx.vpW)) * 2.F) - 1.F;
  const float y_ndc = 1.f - (vp.y / (float)ctx.vpH) * 2.f;

  const glm::mat4& V = ctx.m_view;
  const glm::mat4& P = ctx.m_proj;
  const glm::mat4 invVP = glm::inverse(P * V);

  glm::vec4 p0 = invVP * glm::vec4(x_ndc, y_ndc, -1.f, 1.f);
  glm::vec4 p1 = invVP * glm::vec4(x_ndc, y_ndc, 1.f, 1.f);
  p0 /= p0.w;
  p1 /= p1.w;

  const glm::vec3 ro = glm::vec3(p0);
  const glm::vec3 rd = glm::normalize(glm::vec3(p1) - ro);

  if (std::abs(rd.y) < 1e-6f)
    return false;
  const float t = -ro.y / rd.y;
  if (t < 0.f)
    return false;

  outHit = ro + rd * t;
  return true;
}

void FoliageBrushTool::OnUpdate(EditorContext& ctx) {
  const bool uiCaptured = engine::InputSystem::Instance().UIMouseCaptured();

  // --- обрабатываем ввод только когда мышь не захвачена UI ---
  // одиночный клик — назначаем центр
  if (!uiCaptured) {
    if (engine::InputSystem::Instance().GetMouseButton(MouseButton::Left, InputState::Pressed)) {
      double mx;
      double my;
      glfwGetCursorPos(ctx.m_window, &mx, &my);

      // строим луч так же, как сейчас
      const glm::vec2 fb = ctx.WindowToViewportFB((float)mx, (float)my);
      glm::vec3 ro;
      glm::vec3 rd;
      math::ScreenPointToRay(fb.x, fb.y, (float)ctx.m_viewportW, (float)ctx.m_viewportH, ctx.m_proj,
                             ctx.m_view, ro, rd);

      glm::vec3 hit{};
      bool ok = false;
      const auto* T = ctx.m_scene ? ctx.m_scene->GetTerrain() : nullptr;
      const float anchorY = ComputeAnchorY(ctx);

      if (T)
        ok = RaycastTerrain(*T, ro, rd, anchorY, hit);
      if (!ok)
        ok = math::IntersectRayPlaneY0(ro, rd, hit);  // фолбек

      if (ok) {
        m_center = hit;
        m_center.y = T ? T->GetHeightAt(hit.x, hit.z) + anchorY : 0.f;
        m_hasCenter = true;
        // пересобираем предпросмотр как и раньше
        if (m_settings.shape == Shape::Circle) {
          m_anchor = m_center;
          m_current = m_center + glm::vec3(m_settings.radius, 0, 0);
          RebuildPreviewCircle();
        } else {
          m_anchor = m_center - glm::vec3(m_settings.width * 0.5f, 0, m_settings.height * 0.5f);
          m_current = m_center + glm::vec3(m_settings.width * 0.5f, 0, m_settings.height * 0.5f);
          RebuildPreviewRect();
        }
      }
    }

    // хоткеи
    if (engine::InputSystem::Instance().GetKey(engine::KeyCode::Enter, InputState::Pressed)) {
      ApplyPlacement(*ctx.m_scene);
    }
    if (engine::InputSystem::Instance().GetKey(engine::KeyCode::Escape, InputState::Pressed)) {
      m_preview.clear();
      m_hasCenter = false;
    }
  }

  // --- оверлей (контур + точки) рисуем всегда ---
  DrawOverlay(ctx);
}

void FoliageBrushTool::DrawControls(EditorContext& ctx) {
  bool dirty = false;
  int shape = (m_settings.shape == Shape::Circle ? 0 : 1);
  ImGui::TextUnformatted("Shape");
  dirty |= ImGui::RadioButton("Circle", &shape, 0);
  ImGui::SameLine();
  dirty |= ImGui::RadioButton("Rect", &shape, 1);
  m_settings.shape = (shape == 0 ? Shape::Circle : Shape::Rect);
  if (m_settings.shape == Shape::Circle) {
    dirty |= ImGui::SliderFloat("Radius (m)", &m_settings.radius, 1.f, 200.f);
  } else {
    dirty |= ImGui::SliderFloat("Width (m)", &m_settings.width, 1.f, 400.f);
    dirty |= ImGui::SliderFloat("Height (m)", &m_settings.height, 1.f, 400.f);
  }
  ImGui::Separator();
  dirty |= ImGui::SliderFloat("Density (per m^2)", &m_settings.density, 0.0f, 1.0f);
  dirty |= ImGui::SliderFloat("Min Spacing (m)", &m_settings.minSpacing, 0.0f, 10.0f);
  dirty |= ImGui::SliderFloat("Jitter [0..1]", &m_settings.jitter, 0.0f, 1.0f);
  ImGui::Separator();
  dirty |= ImGui::InputInt("Seed", (int*)&m_settings.seed);
  dirty |= ImGui::SliderFloat("Scale min", &m_settings.scaleMin, 0.2f, 5.0f);
  dirty |= ImGui::SliderFloat("Scale max", &m_settings.scaleMax, 0.2f, 5.0f);
  dirty |= ImGui::SliderFloat("Yaw min", &m_settings.yawMinDeg, 0.f, 360.f);
  dirty |= ImGui::SliderFloat("Yaw max", &m_settings.yawMaxDeg, 0.f, 360.f);

  // ----- ВЫБОР ПРЕФАБА ИЗ СПИСКА -----
  ImGui::Separator();
  ImGui::TextUnformatted("Prefab");

  if (ctx.m_scene) {
    auto& prefabSystem = ctx.m_scene->GetPrefabSystem();
    auto prefabNames = prefabSystem.GetPrefabNames();  // все префабы

    if (!prefabNames.empty()) {
      // если текущий prefab пустой или не найден — берём первый
      int currentIndex = 0;
      if (!m_settings.prefab.empty()) {
        auto it = std::find(prefabNames.begin(), prefabNames.end(), m_settings.prefab);
        if (it != prefabNames.end()) {
          currentIndex = static_cast<int>(std::distance(prefabNames.begin(), it));
        }
      } else {
        m_settings.prefab = prefabNames[0];
        dirty = true;
      }

      // Комбо по имени префаба
      const char* preview = prefabNames[currentIndex].c_str();
      if (ImGui::BeginCombo("##PrefabCombo", preview)) {
        for (int i = 0; i < static_cast<int>(prefabNames.size()); ++i) {
          const bool isSelected = (i == currentIndex);
          const std::string& name = prefabNames[i];

          // Можно тут же скрывать "огненные" префабы, если не нужны:
          // if (IsFireName(name)) continue;

          if (ImGui::Selectable(name.c_str(), isSelected)) {
            currentIndex = i;
            m_settings.prefab = name;
            dirty = true;
          }
          if (isSelected)
            ImGui::SetItemDefaultFocus();
        }
        ImGui::EndCombo();
      }
    } else {
      ImGui::TextUnformatted("No prefabs loaded");
    }
  } else {
    ImGui::TextUnformatted("Scene not available");
  }

  if (ImGui::Button("Re-seed")) {
    EnsureSeed();
    dirty = true;
  }
  ImGui::SameLine();
  if (ImGui::Button("Apply")) {
    if (ctx.m_scene) {
      ApplyPlacement(*ctx.m_scene);
    }
  }
  ImGui::SameLine();
  if (ImGui::Button("Clear")) {
    m_preview.clear();
    m_hasCenter = false;
  }
  ImGui::Separator();
  ImGui::Text("Preview points: %zu", m_preview.size());

  if (dirty && m_hasCenter) {
    if (m_settings.shape == Shape::Circle) {
      m_anchor = m_center;
      m_current = m_center + glm::vec3(m_settings.radius, 0, 0);
      RebuildPreviewCircle();
    } else {
      m_anchor = m_center - glm::vec3(m_settings.width * 0.5f, 0, m_settings.height * 0.5f);
      m_current = m_center + glm::vec3(m_settings.width * 0.5f, 0, m_settings.height * 0.5f);
      RebuildPreviewRect();
    }
  }
}

// ——— генераторы предпросмотра ———

void FoliageBrushTool::RebuildPreviewCircle() {
  m_preview.clear();
  const glm::vec2 c = {m_anchor.x, m_anchor.z};
  const glm::vec2 q = {m_current.x, m_current.z};
  const float R = (m_dragging ? glm::length(q - c) : m_settings.radius);

  const float area = std::numbers::pi_v<float> * R * R;
  const float expectedN = area * m_settings.density;

  const float cell = std::max(m_settings.minSpacing, 0.5f);
  std::uniform_real_distribution<float> uni(-0.5f, 0.5f);

  Grid grid;
  grid.SetCell(cell);
  m_preview.reserve(static_cast<size_t>(std::max(0.f, expectedN)));

  for (float x = -R; x <= R; x += cell) {
    for (float y = -R; y <= R; y += cell) {
      if (x * x + y * y > R * R)
        continue;
      glm::vec2 p = {c.x + x, c.y + y};
      p += glm::vec2(uni(m_rng), uni(m_rng)) * (cell * m_settings.jitter);
      if (m_settings.minSpacing <= 0.0f || grid.Accept(p, m_settings.minSpacing)) {
        m_preview.emplace_back(p.x, 0.f, p.y);
      }
    }
  }

  if (expectedN > 1 && m_preview.size() > static_cast<size_t>(expectedN)) {
    m_preview.resize(static_cast<size_t>(expectedN));
  }

  // (опционально)
  constexpr size_t kMaxPreviewPoints = 120'000;
  if (m_preview.size() > kMaxPreviewPoints)
    m_preview.resize(kMaxPreviewPoints);
}

void FoliageBrushTool::RebuildPreviewRect() {
  m_preview.clear();
  const glm::vec2 a = {m_anchor.x, m_anchor.z};
  const glm::vec2 b = {m_current.x, m_current.z};
  const glm::vec2 minp = {std::min(a.x, b.x), std::min(a.y, b.y)};
  const glm::vec2 maxp = {std::max(a.x, b.x), std::max(a.y, b.y)};
  const glm::vec2 size = maxp - minp;

  const float area = size.x * size.y;
  const float expectedN = area * m_settings.density;

  const float cell = std::max(m_settings.minSpacing, 0.5f);
  std::uniform_real_distribution<float> uni(-0.5f, 0.5f);

  Grid grid;
  grid.SetCell(cell);
  m_preview.reserve(static_cast<size_t>(std::max(0.f, expectedN)));

  for (float x = minp.x; x <= maxp.x; x += cell) {
    for (float y = minp.y; y <= maxp.y; y += cell) {
      glm::vec2 p = {x, y};
      p += glm::vec2(uni(m_rng), uni(m_rng)) * (cell * m_settings.jitter);
      if (m_settings.minSpacing <= 0.0f || grid.Accept(p, m_settings.minSpacing)) {
        m_preview.emplace_back(p.x, 0.f, p.y);
      }
    }
  }

  if (expectedN > 1 && m_preview.size() > static_cast<size_t>(expectedN)) {
    m_preview.resize(static_cast<size_t>(expectedN));
  }

  // (опционально)
  constexpr size_t kMaxPreviewPoints = 120'000;
  if (m_preview.size() > kMaxPreviewPoints)
    m_preview.resize(kMaxPreviewPoints);
}

static bool IsFireName(std::string_view id) {
  // временная эвристика — до появления нормального category/flags в префабе
  return id.find("fire") != std::string_view::npos || id.find("flame") != std::string_view::npos;
}
// ——— размещение ———

void FoliageBrushTool::ApplyPlacement(engine::Scene& scene) {
  if (m_preview.empty())
    return;
  if (IsFireName(m_settings.prefab)) {
    m_preview.clear();
    return;  // тихо выходим (можно логнуть/мигнуть тултипом)
  }

  std::uniform_real_distribution<float> unif01(0.f, 1.f);
  const float smin = m_settings.scaleMin, smax = m_settings.scaleMax;
  const float yawMin = glm::radians(m_settings.yawMinDeg);
  const float yawMax = glm::radians(m_settings.yawMaxDeg);

  for (auto& p : m_preview) {
    // scale (для «разной высоты» используешь масштаб по Y, но я даю униформ по всем осям)
    const float t = unif01(m_rng);
    const float s = smin + (smax - smin) * t;

    // поворот по Y (если модели поддерживают, можно хранить как часть SceneObject при расширении)
    (void)yawMin;
    (void)yawMax;  // пока не сохраняем углы — при надобности расширь SceneObject

    scene.AddTree(p.x, p.z, m_settings.prefab, s);
  }

  m_preview.clear();
}

// FoliageBrushTool.cc
bool FoliageBrushTool::ProjectToScreen(const EditorContext& ctx, const glm::vec3& p,
                                       glm::vec2& out_px) {
  const glm::vec4 clip = ctx.m_proj * ctx.m_view * glm::vec4(p, 1.0f);
  if (clip.w <= 0.0f)
    return false;
  const glm::vec3 ndc = glm::vec3(clip) / clip.w;  // [-1..1]
  if (ndc.x < -1.f || ndc.x > 1.f || ndc.y < -1.f || ndc.y > 1.f)
    return false;
  // в пиксели framebuffer
  const float x = (ndc.x * 0.5f + 0.5f) * (float)ctx.vpW;
  const float y = (1.f - (ndc.y * 0.5f + 0.5f)) * (float)ctx.vpH;
  out_px = {x, y};
  return true;
}

void FoliageBrushTool::DrawOverlay(EditorContext& ctx) {
  auto* dl = ImGui::GetBackgroundDrawList();  // рисуем под UI (можно Foreground)
  const ImU32 COL_OUTLINE = IM_COL32(80, 200, 120, 200);
  const ImU32 COL_POINTS = IM_COL32(80, 200, 120, 180);

  const auto* T = ctx.m_scene ? ctx.m_scene->GetTerrain() : nullptr;
  const float ANCHOR_Y = ComputeAnchorY(ctx);
  auto lift = [&](glm::vec3 p) {
    if (T)
      p.y = T->GetHeightAt(p.x, p.z) + ANCHOR_Y;
    return p;
  };
  // контур области
  std::vector<ImVec2> poly;
  poly.reserve(128);

  if (m_hasCenter) {
    if (m_settings.shape == Shape::Circle) {
      const float R =
          (m_dragging ? glm::length(glm::vec2(m_current.x - m_anchor.x, m_current.z - m_anchor.z))
                      : m_settings.radius);
      const int SEG = 64;
      for (int i = 0; i < SEG; i++) {
        float a = (float)i / SEG * 2.f * std::numbers::pi_v<float>;
        glm::vec3 pw = m_center + glm::vec3(std::cos(a) * R, 0.f, std::sin(a) * R);
        pw = lift(pw);
        glm::vec2 ps;
        if (ProjectToScreen(ctx, pw, ps))
          poly.emplace_back(ps.x, ps.y);
      }
    } else {  // Rect — тесселированная рамка по рельефу
      const float w = m_settings.width;
      const float h = m_settings.height;
      const glm::vec3 c = m_center;  // y не важен — lift() выставит

      // сколько сегментов на метр (подбери; 0.5…2 — ок)
      const float segPerMeter = 1.0f;
      const int segX = std::max(4, (int)std::ceil(w * segPerMeter));
      const int segZ = std::max(4, (int)std::ceil(h * segPerMeter));

      auto push_proj = [&](const glm::vec3& pw) {
        glm::vec2 s;
        if (ProjectToScreen(ctx, pw, s))
          poly.emplace_back(s.x, s.y);
      };

      poly.clear();
      poly.reserve(2 * (segX + segZ) * 2);

      // Левая кромка: (-w/2, -h/2) → (-w/2, +h/2)
      for (int i = 0; i <= segZ; ++i) {
        float t = (float)i / segZ;
        glm::vec3 p = c + glm::vec3(-w * 0.5f, 0.f, -h * 0.5f + t * h);
        push_proj(lift(p));
      }
      // Верхняя: (-w/2, +h/2) → (+w/2, +h/2)
      for (int i = 1; i <= segX; ++i) {
        float t = (float)i / segX;
        glm::vec3 p = c + glm::vec3(-w * 0.5f + t * w, 0.f, +h * 0.5f);
        push_proj(lift(p));
      }
      // Правая: (+w/2, +h/2) → (+w/2, -h/2)
      for (int i = 1; i <= segZ; ++i) {
        float t = (float)i / segZ;
        glm::vec3 p = c + glm::vec3(+w * 0.5f, 0.f, +h * 0.5f - t * h);
        push_proj(lift(p));
      }
      // Нижняя: (+w/2, -h/2) → (-w/2, -h/2)
      for (int i = 1; i <= segX; ++i) {
        float t = (float)i / segX;
        glm::vec3 p = c + glm::vec3(+w * 0.5f - t * w, 0.f, -h * 0.5f);
        push_proj(lift(p));
      }
    }
  }

if (poly.size() >= 2) {
  const bool isFullCircle = (m_settings.shape == Shape::Circle && poly.size() == 64);
  dl->AddPolyline(poly.data(),
                  (int)poly.size(),
                  COL_OUTLINE,
                  isFullCircle ? ImDrawFlags_Closed : 0,
                  2.0f);
}

  // точки предпросмотра
  // при очень больших количествах точек можно прореживать stride'ом
  const size_t stride = (m_preview.size() > 20000 ? 3 : 1);
  for (size_t i = 0; i < m_preview.size(); i += stride) {
    const auto& p = m_preview[i];
    glm::vec3 pw = lift(p);
    glm::vec2 px;
    if (ProjectToScreen(ctx, pw, px)) {
      dl->AddCircleFilled(ImVec2(px.x, px.y), 2.0f, COL_POINTS, 8);
    }
  }
}

}  // namespace ui



===== src/ui/panels/SceneIOPanel.cc =====
#include "ui/panels/SceneIOPanel.h"
#include "core/Application.h"
#include "ui/EditorContext.h"
#include <imgui.h>

using namespace ui;

void SceneIOPanel::Draw(EditorContext &ctx) {
  bool open = true;
  if (!ImGui::Begin(Id(), &open)) {
    ImGui::End();
    return;
  }

  ImGui::TextUnformatted("Scene file");
  ImGui::InputText("##path", m_path.data(), m_path.size());

  ImGui::Separator();

  // Кнопки Load / Save
  if (ImGui::Button("Load")) {
    // Предполагаем, что в Scene добавлены методы: bool LoadScene(const char*
    // path);
    try {
      ctx.m_app->LoadScene(std::string(m_path.data()));
      ctx.m_sceneChanged = true;
      m_lastOk = true;
    } catch (...) {
      m_lastOk = false;
    }
    m_lastOp = "Load";
  }
  ImGui::SameLine();
  if (ImGui::Button("Save")) {
    try {
      ctx.m_app->SaveScene(std::string(m_path.data()));
      m_lastOk = true;
    } catch (...) {
      m_lastOk = false;
    }
    m_lastOp = "Save";
  }

  // Статус
  ImGui::SameLine();
  if ((m_lastOp != nullptr) && (*m_lastOp != 0)) {
    ImGui::Text("%s: %s", m_lastOp, (m_lastOk ? "OK" : "FAILED"));
  }

  // Быстрые пресеты (опционально)
  if (ImGui::CollapsingHeader("Quick slots", ImGuiTreeNodeFlags_DefaultOpen)) {
    if (ImGui::Button("data/scene1.yml")) {
      strncpy(m_path.data(), "data/scene1.yml", m_path.size());
    }
    ImGui::SameLine();
    if (ImGui::Button("data/scene2.yml")) {
      strncpy(m_path.data(), "data/scene2.yml", m_path.size());
    }
  }

  ImGui::End();
}



===== src/ui/panels/WindPanel.cc =====
#include "WindPanel.h"
#include "ui/EditorContext.h"
#include "core/scene/Scene.h"
#include <imgui.h>
#include <glm/gtc/type_ptr.hpp>

namespace ui {

void WindPanel::Draw(EditorContext& ctx) {
    if (!ctx.m_scene) return;
    
    auto& scene = *ctx.m_scene;
    auto& windConfig = scene.GetWindConfig();
    
    ImGui::Begin("Wind Configuration", nullptr, ImGuiWindowFlags_AlwaysAutoResize);
    
    // Направление ветра
    ImGui::Text("Wind Direction");
    ImGui::SameLine();
    ImGui::SetNextItemWidth(100);
    if (ImGui::DragFloat3("##Direction", m_direction, 0.1f, -1.0f, 1.0f)) {
        windConfig.direction = glm::vec3(m_direction[0], m_direction[1], m_direction[2]);
    }
    
    // Сила ветра
    ImGui::Text("Strength");
    ImGui::SameLine();
    ImGui::SetNextItemWidth(150);
    if (ImGui::SliderFloat("##Strength", &m_strength, 0.0f, 50.0f, "%.1f")) {
        windConfig.strength = m_strength;
    }
    
    // Порывистость
    ImGui::Text("Gustiness");
    ImGui::SameLine();
    ImGui::SetNextItemWidth(150);
    if (ImGui::SliderFloat("##Gustiness", &m_gustiness, 0.0f, 1.0f, "%.2f")) {
        windConfig.gustiness = m_gustiness;
    }
    
    // Скорость изменения
    ImGui::Text("Change Speed");
    ImGui::SameLine();
    ImGui::SetNextItemWidth(150);
    if (ImGui::SliderFloat("##ChangeSpeed", &m_changeSpeed, 0.0f, 1.0f, "%.2f")) {
        windConfig.change_speed = m_changeSpeed;
    }
    
    ImGui::Separator();
    
    // Визуализация
    ImGui::Checkbox("Show Wind Visualization", &m_showWindVisualization);
    
    // Текущий ветер
    auto currentWind = windConfig.getCurrentWind(scene.GetWindTime()); 
    ImGui::Text("Current Wind: (%.1f, %.1f, %.1f)", 
                currentWind.x, currentWind.y, currentWind.z);
    
    // Кнопка сброса
    if (ImGui::Button("Reset to Default")) {
        windConfig = WindConfig();
        m_direction[0] = 1.0f; m_direction[1] = 0.0f;
        m_strength = 10.0f;
        m_gustiness = 0.3f;
        m_changeSpeed = 0.1f;
    }
    
    ImGui::End();
}

} // namespace ui


===== src/ui/panels/FoliageBrushTool.h =====
// ui/tools/FoliageBrushTool.h
#pragma once
#include "ui/ITool.h"
#include <string>
#include <random>
#include <vector>
#include <glm/vec3.hpp>

namespace engine { class Scene; }
namespace ui {
class EditorContext;

class FoliageBrushTool final : public ITool {
public:
  enum class Shape { Circle, Rect };

  struct Settings {
    Shape shape = Shape::Circle;
    float radius = 10.f;        // для круга
    float width  = 20.f;        // для прямоугольника
    float height = 20.f;        // для прямоугольника
    float density = 0.1f;       // объектов на м² (например)
    float minSpacing = 1.0f;    // минимальная дистанция между точками
    float jitter = 0.5f;        // доля случайного смещения в ячейке
    unsigned seed = 12345;

    // визуальное разнообразие
    float scaleMin = 0.9f;
    float scaleMax = 1.3f;
    float yawMinDeg = 0.f;
    float yawMaxDeg = 360.f;

    // какой префаб/тип дерева
    std::string prefab = "default_tree";
  };

  explicit FoliageBrushTool(std::string_view /*unused*/) {}

  [[nodiscard]] ToolType Type() const noexcept override { return ToolType::OTHER_TOOL; }
  [[nodiscard]] const char* Name() const noexcept { return "Foliage Brush"; }
  [[nodiscard]] const char* Id()   const noexcept { return "Tool.FoliageBrush"; }

  void OnActivate(EditorContext& ctx) override;
  void OnDeactivate(EditorContext& /*ctx*/) override { m_preview.clear(); m_dragging = false; }
  void OnUpdate(EditorContext& ctx) override;

  // Контролы инструмента (без Begin/End)
  void DrawControls(EditorContext& ctx);

private:
  // выбор области
  bool m_dragging = false;
  glm::vec3 m_anchor{};     // первая точка (мир)
  glm::vec3 m_current{};    // текущая точка (мир)
  std::vector<glm::vec3> m_preview; // точки предпросмотра (мир)

  Settings m_settings{};
  std::mt19937 m_rng{m_settings.seed};

  // генераторы
  void RebuildPreviewCircle();
  void RebuildPreviewRect();
  void ApplyPlacement(engine::Scene& scene);

  // хелперы
  static bool ScreenToGround(EditorContext& ctx, float mx, float my, glm::vec3& outHit);
  void EnsureSeed();


  bool m_hasCenter = false;
    glm::vec3 m_center{0.0f};  // центр области по клику

    void DrawOverlay(EditorContext& ctx);           // рисование контура и точек
    static bool ProjectToScreen(const EditorContext& ctx,
                                const glm::vec3& p_world,
                                glm::vec2& out_px); // world -> screen px

};

} // namespace ui



===== src/ui/panels/TerrainConfigPanel.cc =====
// src/ui/panels/TerrainConfigPanel.cc
#include "TerrainConfigPanel.h"
#include "core/scene/Scene.h"
#include "core/scene/Terrain.h"
#include <filesystem>
#include <imgui.h>
#include <random>

namespace ui {

void TerrainConfigPanel::Draw(EditorContext &ctx) {
  ImGui::Begin("Terrain Configuration", nullptr,
               ImGuiWindowFlags_AlwaysAutoResize);

  ImGui::PushItemWidth(120);
  const auto *terrain = ctx.m_scene ? ctx.m_scene->GetTerrain() : nullptr;
  if (ImGui::IsWindowAppearing() || ctx.m_sceneChanged) {
    m_settings.width = terrain->GetWidth();
    m_settings.depth = terrain->GetDepth();
    m_settings.scale = terrain->GetScale();
    m_settings.maxHeight = terrain->GetMaxHeight();
    m_settings.seed = terrain->GetSeed();
    m_settings.frequency = terrain->GetFrequency();
    m_settings.octaves = terrain->GetOctaves();
    m_settings.persistence = terrain->GetPersistence();
    m_settings.lacunarity = terrain->GetLacunarity();
    m_settings.position = terrain->GetPosition();
    m_settings.textureSettings = terrain->GetTextureSettings();

    // Инициализируем буфер пути текстуры
    strncpy(m_texturePath, m_settings.textureSettings.diffuseMap.c_str(),
            sizeof(m_texturePath) - 1);
    m_texturePath[sizeof(m_texturePath) - 1] = '\0';

    // Загружаем превью если текстура уже есть
    if (!m_settings.textureSettings.diffuseMap.empty()) {
      LoadAndPreviewTexture(m_settings.textureSettings.diffuseMap);
    }

    ctx.m_sceneChanged = false;
  }
  bool changed = false;

  changed |= ImGui::SliderInt("Width", &m_settings.width, 32, 512, "%d",
                              ImGuiSliderFlags_AlwaysClamp);
  changed |= ImGui::SliderInt("Depth", &m_settings.depth, 32, 512, "%d",
                              ImGuiSliderFlags_AlwaysClamp);
  changed |=
      ImGui::SliderFloat("Scale", &m_settings.scale, 0.1f, 10.0f, "%.2f");
  changed |= ImGui::SliderFloat("Max Height", &m_settings.maxHeight, 1.0f,
                                300.0f, "%.1f");
  changed |= ImGui::SliderInt("Octaves", &m_settings.octaves, 1, 10);
  changed |= ImGui::SliderFloat("Frequency", &m_settings.frequency, 0.001f,
                                0.1f, "%.4f");
  changed |= ImGui::SliderFloat("Persistence", &m_settings.persistence, 0.1f,
                                1.0f, "%.2f");
  changed |= ImGui::SliderFloat("Lacunarity", &m_settings.lacunarity, 1.0f,
                                4.0f, "%.2f");
  ImGui::SeparatorText("Position");

  bool pos_changed = false;
  pos_changed |=
      ImGui::SliderFloat("X", &m_settings.position.x, -500.0f, 500.0f, "%.1f");
  pos_changed |=
      ImGui::SliderFloat("Y", &m_settings.position.y, -200.0f, 200.0f, "%.1f");
  pos_changed |=
      ImGui::SliderFloat("Z", &m_settings.position.z, -500.0f, 500.0f, "%.1f");

  if (pos_changed) {
    ctx.m_scene->GetTerrain()->SetPosition(m_settings.position);
  }

  DrawTextureSettings(ctx);

  ImGui::PopItemWidth();

  ImGui::Separator();

  if (ImGui::Button("Random Seed")) {
    std::random_device rd;
    m_settings.seed = rd();
    changed = true;
  }
  ImGui::SameLine();
  ImGui::Text("Seed: %u", m_settings.seed);
  changed |=
      ImGui::InputInt("##seed", reinterpret_cast<int *>(&m_settings.seed));

  ImGui::Separator();

  if (ImGui::Button("Regenerate Terrain", ImGui::GetContentRegionAvail())) {
    m_regenerate = true;
  }

  if (m_regenerate || changed || m_textureChanged) {
    engine::TerrainGenerationSettings s;
    s.width = m_settings.width;
    s.depth = m_settings.depth;
    s.scale = m_settings.scale;
    s.maxHeight = m_settings.maxHeight;
    s.seed = m_settings.seed;
    s.frequency = m_settings.frequency;
    s.octaves = m_settings.octaves;
    s.persistence = m_settings.persistence;
    s.lacunarity = m_settings.lacunarity;
    s.textureSettings = m_settings.textureSettings;

    ctx.m_scene->GenerateTerrain(s);
    m_regenerate = false;
    m_textureChanged = false;
  }
  ImGui::End();
}

void TerrainConfigPanel::LoadAndPreviewTexture(const std::string &path) {
  try {
    if (std::filesystem::exists(path)) {
      m_texturePreview = engine::Texture::Create(path);
      m_textureLoaded = true;
      strncpy(m_texturePath, path.c_str(), sizeof(m_texturePath) - 1);
      m_texturePath[sizeof(m_texturePath) - 1] = '\0';
    } else {
      m_textureLoaded = false;
      m_texturePreview.reset();
    }
  } catch (const std::exception &e) {
    m_textureLoaded = false;
    m_texturePreview.reset();
  }
}

void TerrainConfigPanel::DrawTexturePreview() {
  if (!m_textureLoaded || !m_texturePreview) {
    ImGui::TextColored(ImVec4(1.0f, 0.4f, 0.4f, 1.0f), "texture not loaded");
    return;
  }

  auto texture_id = m_texturePreview->GetId();

  if (texture_id) {
    float preview_size = 100.0f;

    ImGui::BeginChild((std::string("TexturePreview")).c_str(),
                      ImVec2(preview_size + 10, preview_size + 30), true);
    {
      ImGui::Image((ImTextureID)(intptr_t)texture_id,
                   ImVec2(preview_size, preview_size));
      ImGui::Text("Size: %dx%d", m_texturePreview->GetWidth(),
                  m_texturePreview->GetHeight());
    }
    ImGui::EndChild();
  }
}

void TerrainConfigPanel::DrawTextureSettings(EditorContext &ctx) {
  ImGui::SeparatorText("Texture");
  ImGui::PushItemWidth(300);
  // Поле для ввода пути к текстуре
  if (ImGui::InputText("Texture Path", m_texturePath, sizeof(m_texturePath))) {
    // Путь был изменен, но не применяем сразу
  }

  // Кнопка применения текстуры
  ImGui::SameLine();
  if (ImGui::Button("Apply")) {
    m_settings.textureSettings.diffuseMap = m_texturePath;
    LoadAndPreviewTexture(m_texturePath);
    m_textureChanged = true;
  }

  // Отображение текущего пути
  if (!m_settings.textureSettings.diffuseMap.empty()) {
    ImGui::Text("Current: %s", m_settings.textureSettings.diffuseMap.c_str());

    // Кнопка удаления текстуры
    ImGui::SameLine();
    if (ImGui::Button("Remove")) {
      m_settings.textureSettings.diffuseMap.clear();
      memset(m_texturePath, 0, sizeof(m_texturePath));
      m_textureLoaded = false;
      m_texturePreview.reset();
      m_textureChanged = true;
    }
  } else {
    ImGui::Text("No texture loaded");
  }
  DrawTexturePreview();
  // Настройки масштаба текстуры
  if (ImGui::SliderFloat("Texture Scale",
                         &m_settings.textureSettings.textureScale, 1.0f, 100.0f,
                         "%.1f")) {
    m_textureChanged = true;
  }

  // Настройки материала
  ImGui::SeparatorText("Material");
  if (ImGui::ColorEdit3("Specular", &m_settings.textureSettings.specular.x)) {
    m_textureChanged = true;
  }
  if (ImGui::SliderFloat("Shininess", &m_settings.textureSettings.shininess,
                         1.0f, 128.0f, "%.0f")) {
    m_textureChanged = true;
  }
}

} // namespace ui

===== src/ui/panels/FireParamsPanel.cc =====
#include "FireParamsPanel.h"
#include "graphics/Renderer.h"
#include "ui/EditorContext.h"
#include <imgui.h>
#include <iostream>
#include <fstream>
#include <yaml-cpp/yaml.h>

namespace ui {

void FireParamsPanel::Draw(EditorContext &ctx) {
  if (!ImGui::Begin(Id())) {
    ImGui::End();
    return;
  }

  DrawPresetsSection();
  
  ImGui::Separator();

  // Кнопки управления
  if (ImGui::Button("Reset to Default")) {
    m_params = FireParams();
    ApplyParamsToShader();
  }
  ImGui::SameLine();
  if (ImGui::Button("Enable All")) {
    m_params.fire.enabled = true;
    m_params.smoke.enabled = true;
    m_params.sparks.enabled = true;
    m_params.ash.enabled = true;
    m_params.heat.enabled = true;
    ApplyParamsToShader();
  }
  ImGui::SameLine();
  if (ImGui::Button("Disable All")) {
    m_params.fire.enabled = false;
    m_params.smoke.enabled = false;
    m_params.sparks.enabled = false;
    m_params.ash.enabled = false;
    m_params.heat.enabled = false;
    ApplyParamsToShader();
  }

  ImGui::Separator();

  // ВКЛАДКИ
  if (ImGui::BeginTabBar("EffectsTabs")) {
    // Глобальные настройки
    if (ImGui::BeginTabItem("Global")) {
      m_currentTab = 0;
      DrawGlobalTab();
      ImGui::EndTabItem();
    }
    
    // Настройки эффектов
    if (ImGui::BeginTabItem("Fire")) {
      m_currentTab = 1;
      DrawFireTab();
      ImGui::EndTabItem();
    }
    
    if (ImGui::BeginTabItem("Smoke")) {
      m_currentTab = 2;
      DrawSmokeTab();
      ImGui::EndTabItem();
    }
    
    if (ImGui::BeginTabItem("Sparks")) {
      m_currentTab = 3;
      DrawSparksTab();
      ImGui::EndTabItem();
    }
    
    if (ImGui::BeginTabItem("Ash")) {
      m_currentTab = 4;
      DrawAshTab();
      ImGui::EndTabItem();
    }
    
    if (ImGui::BeginTabItem("Heat")) {
      m_currentTab = 5;
      DrawHeatTab();
      ImGui::EndTabItem();
    }
    
    if (ImGui::BeginTabItem("Textures")) {
      m_currentTab = 6;
      DrawTexturesTab();
      ImGui::EndTabItem();
    }
    
    ImGui::EndTabBar();
  }

  // Дебаунсинг обновлений
  auto now = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
  if (m_needsUpdate && (now - m_lastUpdateTime) > UPDATE_DELAY) {
    ApplyParamsToShader();
    m_needsUpdate = false;
  }

  ImGui::End();
}

void FireParamsPanel::DrawGlobalTab() {
  ImGui::SeparatorText("Global Settings");
  
  bool changed = false;
  
  ImGui::SeparatorText("Quick Toggles");
  
  ImGui::BeginTable("QuickToggles", 5, ImGuiTableFlags_SizingFixedFit);
  ImGui::TableNextRow();
  
  ImGui::TableSetColumnIndex(0);
  changed |= ImGui::Checkbox("Fire", &m_params.fire.enabled);
  
  ImGui::TableSetColumnIndex(1);
  changed |= ImGui::Checkbox("Smoke", &m_params.smoke.enabled);
  
  ImGui::TableSetColumnIndex(2);
  changed |= ImGui::Checkbox("Sparks", &m_params.sparks.enabled);
  
  ImGui::TableSetColumnIndex(3);
  changed |= ImGui::Checkbox("Ash", &m_params.ash.enabled);
  
  ImGui::TableSetColumnIndex(4);
  changed |= ImGui::Checkbox("Heat", &m_params.heat.enabled);
  
  ImGui::EndTable();
  
  if (changed) {
    m_needsUpdate = true;
    m_lastUpdateTime = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
  }
}

void FireParamsPanel::DrawEffectParams(const char* name, FireParams::EffectParams& params, int effectType, bool showColor) {
  ImGui::SeparatorText(name);
  
  bool changed = false;
  
  changed |= ImGui::Checkbox("Enabled", &params.enabled);
  
  if (params.enabled) {
    ImGui::Indent();
    
    ImGui::Text("Size & Animation");
    changed |= ImGui::SliderFloat("Particle Size", &params.particleSize, 0.1f, 10.0f);
    changed |= ImGui::SliderFloat("Size Variation", &params.sizeVariation, 0.0f, 3.0f);
    changed |= ImGui::SliderFloat("Animation Speed", &params.animationSpeed, 0.1f, 5.0f);
    changed |= ImGui::SliderFloat("Lifetime", &params.lifetime, 0.5f, 15.0f);
    changed |= ImGui::SliderFloat("Spawn Rate", &params.spawnRate, 1.0f, 500.0f);
    
    ImGui::Text("Movement");
    changed |= ImGui::SliderFloat("Rise Speed", &params.riseSpeed, 0.0f, 10.0f);
    changed |= ImGui::SliderFloat("Turbulence", &params.turbulence, 0.0f, 3.0f);
    changed |= ImGui::SliderFloat("Wind Influence", &params.windInfluence, 0.0f, 3.0f);
    changed |= ImGui::SliderFloat("Spread", &params.spread, 0.1f, 5.0f);
    
    if (showColor) {
      ImGui::Text("Colors");
      changed |= ImGui::ColorEdit3("Base Color", &params.baseColor[0]);
      changed |= ImGui::ColorEdit3("End Color", &params.endColor[0]);
      changed |= ImGui::SliderFloat("Color Variation", &params.colorVariation, 0.0f, 1.0f);
    }
    ImGui::Text("Color Effects");
    changed |= ImGui::SliderFloat("Red Intensity", &params.redIntensity, 0.0f, 2.0f);
    changed |= ImGui::SliderFloat("Yellow Reduction", &params.yellowReduction, 0.0f, 1.0f);
    changed |= ImGui::SliderFloat("Alpha Multiplier", &params.alphaMultiplier, 0.1f, 3.0f);
    changed |= ImGui::SliderFloat("Texture Influence", &params.textureInfluence, 0.0f, 1.0f);
    
    // Информация о эффекте
    ImGui::TextDisabled("Effect appears at: ");
    if (effectType == 0) ImGui::TextDisabled("Always when burning");
    else if (effectType == 1) ImGui::TextDisabled("Medium fire intensity");
    else if (effectType == 2) ImGui::TextDisabled("High fire intensity"); 
    else if (effectType == 3) ImGui::TextDisabled("Any fire intensity");
    else if (effectType == 4) ImGui::TextDisabled("Creates heat distortion");
    
    ImGui::Unindent();
  }
  
  if (changed) {
    m_needsUpdate = true;
    m_lastUpdateTime = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
  }
}

void FireParamsPanel::DrawFireTab() {
  DrawEffectParams("Fire Parameters", m_params.fire, 0, true);
}

void FireParamsPanel::DrawSmokeTab() {
  DrawEffectParams("Smoke Parameters", m_params.smoke, 1, true);
}

void FireParamsPanel::DrawSparksTab() {
  DrawEffectParams("Sparks Parameters", m_params.sparks, 2, true);
}

void FireParamsPanel::DrawAshTab() {
  DrawEffectParams("Ash Parameters", m_params.ash, 3, false);
}

void FireParamsPanel::DrawHeatTab() {
  DrawEffectParams("Heat Parameters", m_params.heat, 4, false);
}

void FireParamsPanel::DrawTexturesTab() {
  ImGui::SeparatorText("Effect Textures");
  
  bool changed = false;
  
  // Огонь
  ImGui::Text("Fire Texture:");
  if (ImGui::InputText("##FireTex", m_texturePaths[0], sizeof(m_texturePaths[0]))) {
    m_params.fireTexturePath = m_texturePaths[0];
    LoadAndPreviewTexture(m_params.fireTexturePath, 0);
    changed = true;
  }
  ImGui::SameLine();
  if (ImGui::Button("Load##Fire")) {
    LoadAndPreviewTexture(m_params.fireTexturePath, 0);
    changed = true;
  }
  DrawTexturePreview(0, "Fire");
  
  // Дым
  ImGui::Text("Smoke Texture:");
  if (ImGui::InputText("##SmokeTex", m_texturePaths[1], sizeof(m_texturePaths[1]))) {
    m_params.smokeTexturePath = m_texturePaths[1];
    LoadAndPreviewTexture(m_params.smokeTexturePath, 1);
    changed = true;
  }
  ImGui::SameLine();
  if (ImGui::Button("Load##Smoke")) {
    LoadAndPreviewTexture(m_params.smokeTexturePath, 1);
    changed = true;
  }
  DrawTexturePreview(1, "Smoke");
  
  // Искры
  ImGui::Text("Sparks Texture:");
  if (ImGui::InputText("##SparksTex", m_texturePaths[2], sizeof(m_texturePaths[2]))) {
    m_params.sparksTexturePath = m_texturePaths[2];
    LoadAndPreviewTexture(m_params.sparksTexturePath, 2);
    changed = true;
  }
  ImGui::SameLine();
  if (ImGui::Button("Load##Sparks")) {
    LoadAndPreviewTexture(m_params.sparksTexturePath, 2);
    changed = true;
  }
  DrawTexturePreview(2, "Sparks");
  
  // Пепел
  ImGui::Text("Ash Texture:");
  if (ImGui::InputText("##AshTex", m_texturePaths[3], sizeof(m_texturePaths[3]))) {
    m_params.ashTexturePath = m_texturePaths[3];
    LoadAndPreviewTexture(m_params.ashTexturePath, 3);
    changed = true;
  }
  ImGui::SameLine();
  if (ImGui::Button("Load##Ash")) {
    LoadAndPreviewTexture(m_params.ashTexturePath, 3);
    changed = true;
  }
  DrawTexturePreview(3, "Ash");
  
  // Тепло
  ImGui::Text("Heat Texture:");
  if (ImGui::InputText("##HeatTex", m_texturePaths[4], sizeof(m_texturePaths[4]))) {
    m_params.heatTexturePath = m_texturePaths[4];
    LoadAndPreviewTexture(m_params.heatTexturePath, 4);
    changed = true;
  }
  ImGui::SameLine();
  if (ImGui::Button("Load##Heat")) {
    LoadAndPreviewTexture(m_params.heatTexturePath, 4);
    changed = true;
  }
  DrawTexturePreview(4, "Heat");
  
  if (changed) {
    m_needsUpdate = true;
    m_lastUpdateTime = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
  }
}

void FireParamsPanel::LoadAndPreviewTexture(const std::string& path, int textureIndex) {
  try {
    if (std::filesystem::exists(path)) {
      m_texturePreviews[textureIndex] = engine::Texture::Create(path);
      m_textureLoaded[textureIndex] = true;
      strncpy(m_texturePaths[textureIndex], path.c_str(), sizeof(m_texturePaths[textureIndex])-1);
      std::cout << "Texture preview loaded: " << path << std::endl;
    } else {
      m_textureLoaded[textureIndex] = false;
      std::cout << "Texture file not found: " << path << std::endl;
    }
  }
  catch (const std::exception& e) {
    m_textureLoaded[textureIndex] = false;
    std::cerr << "Failed to load texture: " << e.what() << std::endl;
  }
}

void FireParamsPanel::DrawTexturePreview(int textureIndex, const char* name) {
  if (!m_textureLoaded[textureIndex] || !m_texturePreviews[textureIndex]) {
    ImGui::TextColored(ImVec4(1.0f, 0.4f, 0.4f, 1.0f), "%s texture not loaded", name);
    return;
  }
  
  auto texture_id = m_texturePreviews[textureIndex]->GetId();
  
  if (texture_id) {
    float preview_size = 100.0f;
    
    ImGui::BeginChild((std::string("TexturePreview") + name).c_str(), 
                     ImVec2(preview_size + 10, preview_size + 30), true);
    {
      ImGui::Image((ImTextureID)(intptr_t)texture_id, ImVec2(preview_size, preview_size));
      ImGui::Text("Size: %dx%d", m_texturePreviews[textureIndex]->GetWidth(), 
                  m_texturePreviews[textureIndex]->GetHeight());
    }
    ImGui::EndChild();
  }
}


void FireParamsPanel::DrawPresetsSection() {
  ImGui::SeparatorText("Presets");
  
  // Создание директории для пресетов, если её нет
  if (!std::filesystem::exists(m_presetsDirectory)) {
    std::filesystem::create_directory(m_presetsDirectory);
  }
  
  // Кнопка обновления списка пресетов
  if (ImGui::Button("Refresh Presets")) {
    RefreshPresetsList();
  }
  ImGui::SameLine();
  
  // Сохранение пресета
  ImGui::InputText("##NewPresetName", m_newPresetName, sizeof(m_newPresetName));
  ImGui::SameLine();
  if (ImGui::Button("Save Preset") && strlen(m_newPresetName) > 0) {
    SavePreset(m_newPresetName);
    m_newPresetName[0] = '\0';
    RefreshPresetsList();
  }
  
  // Список пресетов
  if (!m_presets.empty()) {
    ImGui::Text("Available Presets:");
    ImGui::BeginChild("PresetsList", ImVec2(0, 150), true);
    
    for (const auto& [name, preset] : m_presets) {
      ImGui::PushID(name.c_str());
      
      bool isCurrent = (m_currentPresetName == name);
      if (ImGui::Button("Load")) {
        LoadPreset(name);
      }
      ImGui::SameLine();
      
      if (ImGui::Button("Delete")) {
        DeletePreset(name);
        ImGui::PopID();
        break;
      }
      ImGui::SameLine();
      
      if (isCurrent) {
        ImGui::TextColored(ImVec4(0.0f, 1.0f, 0.0f, 1.0f), "%s", name.c_str());
      } else {
        ImGui::Text("%s", name.c_str());
      }
      
      ImGui::PopID();
    }
    
    ImGui::EndChild();
  } else {
    ImGui::TextDisabled("No presets found. Create one or refresh the list.");
  }
}

void FireParamsPanel::SavePreset(const std::string& name) {
  Preset preset;
  preset.name = name;
  preset.params = m_params;
  
  SavePresetToYAML(preset);
  m_currentPresetName = name;
  
  std::cout << "Preset saved: " << name << std::endl;
}

void FireParamsPanel::LoadPreset(const std::string& name) {
  std::string filename = m_presetsDirectory + "/" + name + ".yaml";
  Preset preset;
  
  if (LoadPresetFromYAML(filename, preset)) {
    m_params = preset.params;
    m_currentPresetName = name;
    
    // Обновляем пути текстур в UI
    strncpy(m_texturePaths[0], m_params.fireTexturePath.c_str(), sizeof(m_texturePaths[0])-1);
    strncpy(m_texturePaths[1], m_params.smokeTexturePath.c_str(), sizeof(m_texturePaths[1])-1);
    strncpy(m_texturePaths[2], m_params.sparksTexturePath.c_str(), sizeof(m_texturePaths[2])-1);
    strncpy(m_texturePaths[3], m_params.ashTexturePath.c_str(), sizeof(m_texturePaths[3])-1);
    strncpy(m_texturePaths[4], m_params.heatTexturePath.c_str(), sizeof(m_texturePaths[4])-1);
    
    // Перезагружаем preview текстур
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
      LoadAndPreviewTexture(m_texturePaths[i], i);
    }
    
    ApplyParamsToShader();
    
    std::cout << "Preset loaded: " << name << std::endl;
  }
}

void FireParamsPanel::DeletePreset(const std::string& name) {
  std::string filename = m_presetsDirectory + "/" + name + ".yaml";
  if (std::filesystem::exists(filename)) {
    std::filesystem::remove(filename);
    m_presets.erase(name);
    if (m_currentPresetName == name) {
      m_currentPresetName.clear();
    }
    std::cout << "Preset deleted: " << name << std::endl;
  }
}

void FireParamsPanel::RefreshPresetsList() {
  m_presets.clear();
  
  if (!std::filesystem::exists(m_presetsDirectory)) {
    return;
  }
  
  for (const auto& entry : std::filesystem::directory_iterator(m_presetsDirectory)) {
    if (entry.path().extension() == ".yaml") {
      std::string filename = entry.path().stem().string();
      Preset preset;
      
      if (LoadPresetFromYAML(entry.path().string(), preset)) {
        m_presets[filename] = preset;
      }
    }
  }
  
  std::cout << "Refreshed presets list. Found: " << m_presets.size() << " presets" << std::endl;
}

void FireParamsPanel::SavePresetToYAML(const Preset& preset) {
  YAML::Emitter out;
  
  out << YAML::BeginMap;
  out << YAML::Key << "name" << YAML::Value << preset.name;
  
  // Global settings
  // Texture paths
  out << YAML::Key << "fireTexturePath" << YAML::Value << preset.params.fireTexturePath;
  out << YAML::Key << "smokeTexturePath" << YAML::Value << preset.params.smokeTexturePath;
  out << YAML::Key << "sparksTexturePath" << YAML::Value << preset.params.sparksTexturePath;
  out << YAML::Key << "ashTexturePath" << YAML::Value << preset.params.ashTexturePath;
  out << YAML::Key << "heatTexturePath" << YAML::Value << preset.params.heatTexturePath;
  
  // Effect parameters
  auto saveEffectParams = [&](const std::string& effectName, const FireParams::EffectParams& params) {
    out << YAML::Key << effectName + "_enabled" << YAML::Value << params.enabled;
    out << YAML::Key << effectName + "_particleSize" << YAML::Value << params.particleSize;
    out << YAML::Key << effectName + "_sizeVariation" << YAML::Value << params.sizeVariation;
    out << YAML::Key << effectName + "_animationSpeed" << YAML::Value << params.animationSpeed;
    out << YAML::Key << effectName + "_lifetime" << YAML::Value << params.lifetime;
    out << YAML::Key << effectName + "_spawnRate" << YAML::Value << params.spawnRate;
    out << YAML::Key << effectName + "_riseSpeed" << YAML::Value << params.riseSpeed;
    out << YAML::Key << effectName + "_turbulence" << YAML::Value << params.turbulence;
    out << YAML::Key << effectName + "_windInfluence" << YAML::Value << params.windInfluence;
    out << YAML::Key << effectName + "_spread" << YAML::Value << params.spread;
    out << YAML::Key << effectName + "_colorVariation" << YAML::Value << params.colorVariation;

    out << YAML::Key << effectName + "_redIntensity" << YAML::Value << params.redIntensity;
    out << YAML::Key << effectName + "_yellowReduction" << YAML::Value << params.yellowReduction;
    out << YAML::Key << effectName + "_alphaMultiplier" << YAML::Value << params.alphaMultiplier;
    out << YAML::Key << effectName + "_textureInfluence" << YAML::Value << params.textureInfluence;
    
    out << YAML::Key << effectName + "_baseColor" << YAML::Value << YAML::Flow << YAML::BeginSeq
        << params.baseColor[0] << params.baseColor[1] << params.baseColor[2] << YAML::EndSeq;
    out << YAML::Key << effectName + "_endColor" << YAML::Value << YAML::Flow << YAML::BeginSeq
        << params.endColor[0] << params.endColor[1] << params.endColor[2] << YAML::EndSeq;
  };
  
  saveEffectParams("fire", preset.params.fire);
  saveEffectParams("smoke", preset.params.smoke);
  saveEffectParams("sparks", preset.params.sparks);
  saveEffectParams("ash", preset.params.ash);
  saveEffectParams("heat", preset.params.heat);
  
  out << YAML::EndMap;
  
  std::string filename = m_presetsDirectory + "/" + preset.name + ".yaml";
  std::ofstream file(filename);
  file << out.c_str();
}

bool FireParamsPanel::LoadPresetFromYAML(const std::string& filename, Preset& preset) {
  try {
    YAML::Node config = YAML::LoadFile(filename);
    
    preset.name = config["name"].as<std::string>();
    
    // Global settings
    
    // Texture paths
    if (config["fireTexturePath"]) preset.params.fireTexturePath = config["fireTexturePath"].as<std::string>();
    if (config["smokeTexturePath"]) preset.params.smokeTexturePath = config["smokeTexturePath"].as<std::string>();
    if (config["sparksTexturePath"]) preset.params.sparksTexturePath = config["sparksTexturePath"].as<std::string>();
    if (config["ashTexturePath"]) preset.params.ashTexturePath = config["ashTexturePath"].as<std::string>();
    if (config["heatTexturePath"]) preset.params.heatTexturePath = config["heatTexturePath"].as<std::string>();
    
    // Effect parameters loader
    auto loadEffectParams = [&](const std::string& effectName, FireParams::EffectParams& params) {
      if (config[effectName + "_enabled"]) params.enabled = config[effectName + "_enabled"].as<bool>();
      if (config[effectName + "_particleSize"]) params.particleSize = config[effectName + "_particleSize"].as<float>();
      if (config[effectName + "_sizeVariation"]) params.sizeVariation = config[effectName + "_sizeVariation"].as<float>();
      if (config[effectName + "_animationSpeed"]) params.animationSpeed = config[effectName + "_animationSpeed"].as<float>();
      if (config[effectName + "_lifetime"]) params.lifetime = config[effectName + "_lifetime"].as<float>();
      if (config[effectName + "_spawnRate"]) params.spawnRate = config[effectName + "_spawnRate"].as<float>();
      if (config[effectName + "_riseSpeed"]) params.riseSpeed = config[effectName + "_riseSpeed"].as<float>();
      if (config[effectName + "_turbulence"]) params.turbulence = config[effectName + "_turbulence"].as<float>();
      if (config[effectName + "_windInfluence"]) params.windInfluence = config[effectName + "_windInfluence"].as<float>();
      if (config[effectName + "_spread"]) params.spread = config[effectName + "_spread"].as<float>();
      if (config[effectName + "_colorVariation"]) params.colorVariation = config[effectName + "_colorVariation"].as<float>();
           
      if (config[effectName + "_redIntensity"]) params.redIntensity = config[effectName + "_redIntensity"].as<float>();
      if (config[effectName + "_yellowReduction"]) params.yellowReduction = config[effectName + "_yellowReduction"].as<float>();
      if (config[effectName + "_alphaMultiplier"]) params.alphaMultiplier = config[effectName + "_alphaMultiplier"].as<float>();
      if (config[effectName + "_textureInfluence"]) params.textureInfluence = config[effectName + "_textureInfluence"].as<float>();
      if (config[effectName + "_baseColor"]) {
        auto baseColor = config[effectName + "_baseColor"].as<std::vector<float>>();
        for (int i = 0; i < 3 && i < baseColor.size(); i++) {
          params.baseColor[i] = baseColor[i];
        }
      }
      
      if (config[effectName + "_endColor"]) {
        auto endColor = config[effectName + "_endColor"].as<std::vector<float>>();
        for (int i = 0; i < 3 && i < endColor.size(); i++) {
          params.endColor[i] = endColor[i];
        }
      }
    };
    
    loadEffectParams("fire", preset.params.fire);
    loadEffectParams("smoke", preset.params.smoke);
    loadEffectParams("sparks", preset.params.sparks);
    loadEffectParams("ash", preset.params.ash);
    loadEffectParams("heat", preset.params.heat);
    
    return true;
  }
  catch (const std::exception& e) {
    std::cerr << "Error loading preset from " << filename << ": " << e.what() << std::endl;
    return false;
  }
}

void FireParamsPanel::ApplyParamsToShader() {
  auto &renderer = engine::Renderer::Instance();
  
  // Обновляем пути текстур из UI в параметры
  m_params.fireTexturePath = m_texturePaths[0];
  m_params.smokeTexturePath = m_texturePaths[1];
  m_params.sparksTexturePath = m_texturePaths[2];
  m_params.ashTexturePath = m_texturePaths[3];
  m_params.heatTexturePath = m_texturePaths[4];
  
  renderer.SetFireParams(m_params);
}

// Остальные методы (DrawPresetsSection, SavePreset, LoadPreset, DeletePreset, 
// RefreshPresetsList, SavePresetToYAML, LoadPresetFromYAML) 
// нужно адаптировать под новую структуру FireParams

} // namespace ui
