void ToolbarPanel::Draw(EditorContext& ctx) {
  // 1) режимы (Prefab / Fire)
  DrawModeSwitch(ctx);

  ImGui::Separator();

  // 2) селектор инструментов (PlaceOnGround / FoliageBrush)
  DrawToolSelector(ctx);

  ImGui::Separator();

  // 3) панель активного инструмента (например, настройки кисти)
  DrawActiveToolUI(ctx);
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// private

void ToolbarPanel::DrawModeSwitch(EditorContext& ctx) {
  auto& st = ctx.State();

  bool isPrefab = (st.m_placeKind == EditorState::PlaceKind::PREFAB);
  if (ImGui::RadioButton("Prefab##mode", isPrefab)) {
    st.m_placeKind = EditorState::PlaceKind::PREFAB;
  }
  ImGui::SameLine();

  bool isFire = (st.m_placeKind == EditorState::PlaceKind::FIRE);
  if (ImGui::RadioButton("Fire##mode", isFire)) {
    st.m_placeKind = EditorState::PlaceKind::FIRE;
    if (ctx.m_editor != nullptr) { ctx.m_editor->SetActiveTool(ui::ToolType::PLACE_ON_GROUND, ctx);
}
  }

  // огонь — его параметры сразу тут
  if (st.m_placeKind == EditorState::PlaceKind::FIRE) {
    ImGui::SliderFloat("Fire intensity", &st.m_fireIntensity, 0.f, 10.f);
    if (ctx.m_scene) ctx.m_scene->SetFireIntensity(st.m_fireIntensity);
    // при необходимости: кнопки запуска/остановки/сброса огня
  }

  // префабы — выбор актуального префаба
  if (st.m_placeKind == EditorState::PlaceKind::PREFAB) {
    if (ctx.m_scene) {
      const auto& ids = ctx.m_scene->ListPrefabIds();
      if (!ids.empty()) {
        if (std::ranges::find(ids, st.m_selectedPrefab) == ids.end()) {
          st.m_selectedPrefab = ids.front();
        }
        if (ImGui::BeginCombo("Prefab##combo", st.m_selectedPrefab.c_str())) {
          for (const auto& id : ids) {
            bool sel = (id == st.m_selectedPrefab);
            if (ImGui::Selectable(id.c_str(), sel)) st.m_selectedPrefab = id;
            if (sel) ImGui::SetItemDefaultFocus();
          }
          ImGui::EndCombo();
        }
      } else {
        ImGui::TextDisabled("no prefabs loaded");
      }
    }
  }
}

void ToolbarPanel::DrawToolSelector(EditorContext& ctx) {
  // тут просто переключаем активный тул
  const auto active = ctx.GetActiveToolType();

  if (ImGui::Selectable("Place on ground", active == ToolType::PLACE_ON_GROUND)) {
    if (ctx.m_editor) ctx.m_editor->SetActiveTool(ToolType::PLACE_ON_GROUND, ctx);
  }

  if (ImGui::Selectable("Foliage Brush", active == ToolType::OTHER_TOOL)) { // пока OTHER_TOOL
    if (ctx.m_editor) ctx.m_editor->SetActiveTool(ToolType::OTHER_TOOL, ctx);
  }
}

// ui/panels/ToolbarPanel.cpp


void ToolbarPanel::DrawActiveToolUI(EditorContext& ctx) {
  const auto active = ctx.GetActiveToolType();
  constexpr ImGuiWindowFlags kWinFlags =
      ImGuiWindowFlags_NoCollapse | ImGuiWindowFlags_NoSavedSettings;

  switch (active) {
    case ToolType::PLACE_ON_GROUND: {
      bool open = true; // без крестика можно передать nullptr
      if (ImGui::Begin("Place on ground", &open, kWinFlags)) {
        if (auto* t = ctx.m_editor ? ctx.m_editor->GetTool<ui::PlaceOnGroundTool>() : nullptr) {
          // если нет контролов — можно текст “no settings”
          // t->DrawControls(ctx);
          ImGui::TextDisabled("No settings.");
        }
      }
      ImGui::End();
      break;
    }
    case ToolType::OTHER_TOOL: { // Foliage Brush
      bool open = true;
      if (ImGui::Begin("Foliage Brush", &open, kWinFlags)) {
        if (auto* t = ctx.m_editor ? ctx.m_editor->GetTool<FoliageBrushTool>() : nullptr) {
          t->DrawControls(ctx); // ВАЖНО: без Begin/End внутри
        }
      }
      ImGui::End();
      break;
    }
    default: break;
  }
}





}  // namespace ui



===== src/ui/panels/PrefabPreviewRenderer.h =====
#pragma once
#include "data/PrefabSystem.h"
#include "graphics/Model.h"
#include <glm/glm.hpp>

namespace ui {

class PrefabPreviewRenderer {
public:
  PrefabPreviewRenderer(int width = 300, int height = 300);
  ~PrefabPreviewRenderer();

  void Render(const engine::data::AgentPrefab &prefab);

  GLuint GetTexture() const { return m_texture; }
  void Resize(int w, int h);

  void Rotate(float yaw, float pitch);
  void Zoom(float delta);
  void ResetView();

private:
  void UpdateCamera();
  void SetupFBO();

  GLuint m_fbo = 0, m_texture = 0, m_rbo = 0;
  int m_width, m_height;

  float m_yaw = -90.0f, m_pitch = 0.0f;
  float m_distance = 10.0f;
  const float m_minDist = 2.0f, m_maxDist = 50.0f;

  glm::vec3 m_target = glm::vec3(0);
  glm::mat4 m_view, m_proj;

  // Кэш
  std::shared_ptr<engine::Model> m_model;
  std::shared_ptr<engine::Material> m_material;
};

} // namespace ui


===== src/ui/panels/PrefabBrowserPanel.cc =====
#include "ui/panels/PrefabBrowserPanel.h"
#include "core/scene/Scene.h"
#include "data/PrefabSystem.h"
#include "graphics/Material.h"
#include "ui/EditorContext.h"
#include "ui/panels/PrefabPreviewRenderer.h"
#include <filesystem>
#include <imgui.h>
#include <memory>
using engine::data::AgentPrefab;

namespace ui {

inline bool DragVec3(const char *label, glm::vec3 &v, float speed = 0.1F,
                     float v_min = -FLT_MAX, float v_max = FLT_MAX,
                     const char *fmt = "%.3f") {
  float a[3] = {v.x, v.y, v.z};
  const bool CHANGED = ImGui::DragFloat3(label, a, speed, v_min, v_max, fmt);
  if (CHANGED) {
    v.x = a[0];
    v.y = a[1];
    v.z = a[2];
  }
  return CHANGED;
}

void PrefabBrowserPanel::OnShow(EditorContext &ctx) {

  glBindFramebuffer(GL_FRAMEBUFFER, 0);
}

PrefabBrowserPanel::PrefabBrowserPanel()
    : m_preview(std::make_unique<PrefabPreviewRenderer>(300, 300)) {}

PrefabBrowserPanel::~PrefabBrowserPanel() {}
void PrefabBrowserPanel::OnHide(EditorContext &ctx) {}
void PrefabBrowserPanel::Draw(EditorContext &ctx) {
  bool open = true;
  if (!ImGui::Begin(Id(), &open)) {
    ImGui::End();
    return;
  }

  const auto &ids = ctx.m_scene->ListPrefabIds();
  if (ids.empty()) {
    ImGui::TextDisabled("No prefabs loaded");
    ImGui::End();
    return;
  }

  const std::string &id = EnsureSelection(ctx, ids);
  AgentPrefab &P = *m_edit;

  ImGui::Columns(3, "PrefabBrowserColumns", true);

  ImGui::BeginChild("LeftColumn", ImVec2(0, 0), true);
  DrawLeftColumn(ctx, ids);
  ImGui::EndChild();

  ImGui::NextColumn();

  ImGui::BeginChild("CenterColumn", ImVec2(0, 0), true);

  ImGui::SeparatorText("Model Preview");

  ImGui::BeginChild("PreviewArea", ImVec2(0, 300 + 40), true,
                    ImGuiWindowFlags_NoScrollbar |
                        ImGuiWindowFlags_NoScrollWithMouse);
  DrawModelPreview(ctx);
  ImGui::Separator();
  ImGui::TextDisabled("LMB + drag: rotate");
  ImGui::TextDisabled("Mouse wheel: zoom");
  ImGui::TextDisabled("Yaw: %.1f, Pitch: %.1f", m_previewYaw, m_previewPitch);

  ImGui::EndChild();
  ImGui::EndChild();
  ImGui::NextColumn();

  ImGui::BeginChild("RightColumn", ImVec2(0, 0), true);

  DrawHeader(id);
  DrawType(P);
  DrawModelSection(P);
  DrawBounds(P);
  DrawPhysics(P);
  DrawFireSection(P);
  DrawFirePoints(P);
  DrawVisuals(P);

  ImGui::Separator();
  DrawSaveButtons(ctx, id, P);
  DrawSavePopup(ctx, id, P);

  ImGui::EndChild();

  ImGui::Columns(1);

  ImGui::End();
}

void PrefabBrowserPanel::DrawModelPreview(EditorContext &ctx) {
  AgentPrefab &P = *m_edit;

  if (ImGui::IsWindowHovered() && ImGui::IsMouseDown(ImGuiMouseButton_Left)) {
    ImVec2 delta = ImGui::GetMouseDragDelta(0, 0.0f);
    if (!m_dragging) {
      m_lastMouse = ImGui::GetMousePos();
      m_dragging = true;
    }
    ImVec2 d = ImVec2(ImGui::GetMousePos().x - m_lastMouse.x,
                      ImGui::GetMousePos().y - m_lastMouse.y);
    m_preview->Rotate(d.x * 0.2f, d.y * 0.2f);
    m_lastMouse = ImGui::GetMousePos();
  } else {
    m_dragging = false;
  }

  if (ImGui::IsWindowHovered()) {
    float wheel = ImGui::GetIO().MouseWheel;
    if (wheel != 0) {
      m_preview->Zoom(wheel * 2.0f);
      ImGui::GetIO().MouseWheel = 0;
    }
  }

  m_preview->Render(P);

  ImGui::Image((ImTextureID)(intptr_t)m_preview->GetTexture(), ImVec2(300, 300),
               ImVec2(0, 1), ImVec2(1, 0));
}

// ------------------ helpers ------------------

const std::string &
PrefabBrowserPanel::EnsureSelection(EditorContext &ctx,
                                    const std::vector<std::string> &ids) {
  if (m_selectedIndex < 0 || m_selectedIndex >= (int)ids.size()) {
    m_selectedIndex = 0;
    m_edit.reset();
  }
  const std::string &id = ids[m_selectedIndex];
  if (!m_edit.has_value()) {
    m_edit = ctx.m_scene->GetPrefabSystem().GetPrefab(id);
    // Синхронизируем текстовые буферы один раз при загрузке
    std::snprintf(m_modelBuf, sizeof(m_modelBuf), "%s",
                  m_edit->m_modelPath.c_str());
    std::snprintf(m_matNormalBuf, sizeof(m_matNormalBuf), "%s",
                  m_edit->normal_material.normal_path.c_str());
    std::snprintf(m_matBurningBuf, sizeof(m_matBurningBuf), "%s",
                  m_edit->normal_material.specular_path.c_str());
    std::snprintf(m_matBurnedBuf, sizeof(m_matBurnedBuf), "%s",
                  m_edit->normal_material.diffuse_path.c_str());
    std::snprintf(m_fireMaterialBuf, sizeof(m_fireMaterialBuf), "%s",
                 m_edit->m_fireMaterialId.c_str());
  }
  return id;
}

void PrefabBrowserPanel::DrawLeftColumn(EditorContext &ctx,
                                        const std::vector<std::string> &ids) {
  for (int i = 0; i < (int)ids.size(); ++i) {
    const bool sel = (i == m_selectedIndex);
    if (ImGui::Selectable(ids[i].c_str(), sel)) {
      m_selectedIndex = i;
      // Перезагружаем редактируемую копию и буферы
      m_edit = ctx.m_scene->GetPrefabSystem().GetPrefab(ids[i]);
      std::snprintf(m_modelBuf, sizeof(m_modelBuf), "%s",
                    m_edit->m_modelPath.c_str());
      std::snprintf(m_matNormalBuf, sizeof(m_matNormalBuf), "%s",
                    m_edit->normal_material.normal_path.c_str());
      std::snprintf(m_matBurningBuf, sizeof(m_matBurningBuf), "%s",
                    m_edit->normal_material.specular_path.c_str());
      std::snprintf(m_matBurnedBuf, sizeof(m_matBurnedBuf), "%s",
                    m_edit->normal_material.diffuse_path.c_str());
    }
    if (sel)
      ImGui::SetItemDefaultFocus();
  }
}

void PrefabBrowserPanel::DrawHeader(const std::string &id) {
  ImGui::Text("Prefab: %s", id.c_str());
  ImGui::Separator();
}

void PrefabBrowserPanel::DrawType(AgentPrefab &P) {
  int t = (int)P.m_type;
  const char *types[] = {"Tree", "Burned", "Fire", "House"};
  if (ImGui::Combo("Type", &t, types, IM_ARRAYSIZE(types))) {
    P.m_type = static_cast<AgentType>(t);
  }
}

void PrefabBrowserPanel::DrawModelSection(AgentPrefab &P) {
  if (ImGui::InputText("Model path", m_modelBuf, IM_ARRAYSIZE(m_modelBuf))) {
    P.m_modelPath = m_modelBuf;
  }
  DragVec3("Model scale", P.m_modelScale, 0.01F);
}

void PrefabBrowserPanel::DrawBounds(AgentPrefab &P) {
  if (ImGui::CollapsingHeader("Bounds", ImGuiTreeNodeFlags_DefaultOpen)) {
    DragVec3("Min", P.m_bounds_min, 0.01f);
    DragVec3("Max", P.m_bounds_max, 0.01f);
  }
}

void PrefabBrowserPanel::DrawPhysics(AgentPrefab &P) {
  if (ImGui::CollapsingHeader("Physics", ImGuiTreeNodeFlags_DefaultOpen)) {
    ImGui::DragFloat("Intensity", &P.m_physics.m_intencity, 0.05f, 0.0f,
                     1000.0f);
  }
}


void PrefabBrowserPanel::DrawFireSection(AgentPrefab &P) {
  if (!ImGui::CollapsingHeader("Fire", ImGuiTreeNodeFlags_DefaultOpen))
    return;

  // ID материала (строка из FireConfig, пока редактируем руками)
  if (ImGui::InputText("Material id", m_fireMaterialBuf,
                       IM_ARRAYSIZE(m_fireMaterialBuf))) {
    P.m_fireMaterialId = m_fireMaterialBuf;
  }

  // Мультипликатор температуры воспламенения
  ImGui::DragFloat("Ignition mult", &P.m_fireIgnitionMult,
                   0.01f, 0.1f, 10.0f);

  // Смещение влажности (от -1 до 1)
  ImGui::DragFloat("Moisture offset", &P.m_fireMoistureOffset,
                   0.01f, -1.0f, 1.0f);
}


void PrefabBrowserPanel::DrawFirePoints(AgentPrefab &P) {
  if (!ImGui::CollapsingHeader("Fire points", ImGuiTreeNodeFlags_DefaultOpen))
    return;

  if (ImGui::Button("Add point")) {
    P.m_firePoints.push_back({glm::vec3(0), 100.0f});
  }
  for (int i = 0; i < (int)P.m_firePoints.size(); ++i) {
    ImGui::PushID(i);
    ImGui::Separator();
    DragVec3("Pos", P.m_firePoints[i].m_position, 0.01f);
    ImGui::DragFloat("Fuel", &P.m_firePoints[i].m_initialFuel, 0.5f, 0.0f,
                     10000.0f);
    ImGui::SameLine();
    if (ImGui::SmallButton("Remove")) {
      P.m_firePoints.erase(P.m_firePoints.begin() + i);
      ImGui::PopID();
      break;
    }
    ImGui::PopID();
  }
}

void PrefabBrowserPanel::DrawVisuals(AgentPrefab &P) {
  if (!ImGui::CollapsingHeader("Visuals", ImGuiTreeNodeFlags_DefaultOpen))
    return;

  bool changed = false;

  changed |=
      ImGui::InputText("Normal", m_matNormalBuf, IM_ARRAYSIZE(m_matNormalBuf));
  changed |= ImGui::InputText("Burning", m_matBurningBuf,
                              IM_ARRAYSIZE(m_matBurningBuf));
  changed |=
      ImGui::InputText("Burned", m_matBurnedBuf, IM_ARRAYSIZE(m_matBurnedBuf));

  // Цвета
  changed |=
      ImGui::ColorEdit3("Normal diffuse", &P.normal_material.diffuse_color[0]);
  changed |= ImGui::ColorEdit3("Normal specular",
                               &P.normal_material.specular_color[0]);
  changed |= ImGui::ColorEdit3("Burning diffuse",
                               &P.burning_material.diffuse_color[0]);
  changed |= ImGui::ColorEdit3("Burning specular",
                               &P.burning_material.specular_color[0]);
  changed |=
      ImGui::ColorEdit3("Burned diffuse", &P.burned_material.diffuse_color[0]);
  changed |= ImGui::ColorEdit3("Burned specular",
                               &P.burned_material.specular_color[0]);

  // Шайнинесс
  changed |= ImGui::DragFloat("Normal shininess", &P.normal_material.shininess,
                              1.0f, 0.0f, 256.0f);
  changed |= ImGui::DragFloat(
      "Burning shininess", &P.burning_material.shininess, 1.0f, 0.0f, 256.0f);
  changed |= ImGui::DragFloat("Burned shininess", &P.burned_material.shininess,
                              1.0f, 0.0f, 256.0f);

  if (changed) {
    P.normal_material.diffuse_path = m_matNormalBuf;
    P.normal_material.specular_path = m_matNormalBuf;
    P.normal_material.normal_path = m_matNormalBuf;

    P.burning_material.diffuse_path = m_matBurningBuf;
    P.burning_material.specular_path = m_matBurningBuf;
    P.burning_material.normal_path = m_matBurningBuf;

    P.burned_material.diffuse_path = m_matBurnedBuf;
    P.burned_material.specular_path = m_matBurnedBuf;
    P.burned_material.normal_path = m_matBurnedBuf;
  }
}

void PrefabBrowserPanel::DrawSaveButtons(EditorContext &ctx,
                                         const std::string &id,
                                         AgentPrefab &P) {
  static float tConfirm = -1000.f;

  if (ImGui::Button("Apply (runtime)")) {
    ctx.m_scene->GetPrefabSystem().SavePrefab(id, P);
    P.LoadMaterials();
    ctx.m_scene->ReloadModelForPrefab(id, P);
    tConfirm = (float)ImGui::GetTime();
  }
  if ((float)ImGui::GetTime() - tConfirm < 1.0f) {
    ImGui::SameLine();
    ImGui::TextColored(ImVec4(0, 1, 0, 1), "Saved");
  }

  ImGui::SameLine();

  if (ImGui::Button("Save to file")) {
    if (m_savePath.empty())
      m_savePath = "res/prefabs/" + id + ".yaml";
    std::snprintf(m_saveBuf, sizeof(m_saveBuf), "%s", m_savePath.c_str());
    m_savePopupOpen = true;
    ImGui::OpenPopup("Save Prefab");
    P.LoadMaterials();
    ctx.m_scene->ReloadModelForPrefab(id, P);
  }
}

void PrefabBrowserPanel::DrawSavePopup(EditorContext &ctx,
                                       const std::string &id, AgentPrefab &P) {
  if (!ImGui::BeginPopupModal("Save Prefab", &m_savePopupOpen,
                              ImGuiWindowFlags_AlwaysAutoResize))
    return;

  ImGui::InputText("Path", m_saveBuf, IM_ARRAYSIZE(m_saveBuf));

  if (ImGui::Button("Save")) {
    m_savePath = m_saveBuf;

    auto &sys = ctx.m_scene->GetPrefabSystem();

    std::string newId;
    {
      std::filesystem::path out(m_savePath);
      newId = out.stem().string();
      if (newId.empty())
        newId = id; // fallback
    }
    sys.SavePrefab(newId, P);

    sys.SavePrefabToFile(newId, m_savePath);

    m_selectedIndex = 0;
    const auto &ids = ctx.m_scene->ListPrefabIds();
    for (int i = 0; i < (int)ids.size(); ++i) {
      if (ids[i] == newId) {
        m_selectedIndex = i;
        break;
      }
    }
    m_edit.reset();
    ImGui::CloseCurrentPopup();
    m_savePopupOpen = false;
  }

  ImGui::SameLine();
  if (ImGui::Button("Cancel")) {
    ImGui::CloseCurrentPopup();
    m_savePopupOpen = false;
  }
  ImGui::EndPopup();
}

} // namespace ui



===== src/ui/panels/ParticleSystemPanel.cc =====
#include "ParticleSystemPanel.h"
#include "core/scene/Scene.h"
#include <imgui.h>

namespace ui {

ParticleSystemPanel::ParticleSystemPanel() {
  m_currentEffect = engine::ParticleEffect::CreateDefault();
}

void ParticleSystemPanel::Draw(EditorContext &ctx) {
  ImGui::Begin("Particle Systems");

  // Кнопка добавления системы частиц
  if (ImGui::Button("Add Particle System")) {
    ImGui::OpenPopup("AddParticleSystemPopup");
  }

  if (ImGui::BeginPopup("AddParticleSystemPopup")) {
    DrawAddParticleSystemMenu(ctx);
    ImGui::EndPopup();
  }

  ImGui::SameLine();
  ImGui::Text("(%zu systems)",
              ctx.m_scene ? ctx.m_scene->GetParticleSystems().size() : 0);

  ImGui::Separator();

  DrawParticleSystemList(ctx);

  if (m_selectedSystem) {
    DrawParticleSystemControls(ctx);
    ImGui::Separator();
    DrawEffectEditor();
  }

  ImGui::End();
}

void ParticleSystemPanel::DrawEffectEditor() {
  if (!m_selectedSystem || !m_selectedSystem->GetEffect())
    return;

  auto effect = m_selectedSystem->GetEffect();

  if (ImGui::CollapsingHeader("Main Module", ImGuiTreeNodeFlags_DefaultOpen)) {
    ImGui::DragFloat("Duration", &effect->main.duration, 0.1f, 0.1f, 100.0f);
    ImGui::Checkbox("Looping", &effect->main.looping);
    ImGui::DragFloat("Start Delay", &effect->main.startDelay, 0.1f, 0.0f,
                     10.0f);

    ImGui::Separator();
    ImGui::Text("Start Values:");
    ImGui::DragFloat("Start Lifetime", &effect->main.startLifetime, 0.1f, 0.1f,
                     100.0f);
    ImGui::DragFloat3("Start Speed", &effect->main.startSpeed[0], 0.1f, -50.0f,
                      50.0f);
    ImGui::DragFloat3("Start Size", &effect->main.startSize[0], 0.1f, 0.01f,
                      10.0f);
    ImGui::Checkbox("3D Size", &effect->main.use3DSize);
    ImGui::DragFloat3("Start Rotation", &effect->main.startRotation[0], 0.1f,
                      -180.0f, 180.0f);
    ImGui::Checkbox("3D Rotation", &effect->main.use3DRotation);
    ImGui::ColorEdit4("Start Color", &effect->main.startColor[0]);
    ImGui::DragFloat("Gravity Modifier", &effect->main.gravityModifier, 0.1f,
                     -10.0f, 10.0f);

    ImGui::Separator();
    ImGui::Text("Simulation:");
    const char *simulationSpaces[] = {"Local", "World", "Custom"};
    ImGui::Combo("Simulation Space", &effect->main.simulationSpace,
                 simulationSpaces, IM_ARRAYSIZE(simulationSpaces));
    ImGui::DragFloat("Simulation Speed", &effect->main.simulationSpeed, 0.1f,
                     0.0f, 10.0f);

    ImGui::DragInt("Max Particles", (int *)&effect->main.maxParticles, 100, 100,
                   100000);
  }

  if (ImGui::CollapsingHeader("Emission Module")) {
    ImGui::Checkbox("Enabled", &effect->emission.enabled);
    ImGui::DragFloat("Rate Over Time", &effect->emission.rateOverTime, 1.0f,
                     0.0f, 1000.0f);
    ImGui::DragFloat("Rate Over Distance", &effect->emission.rateOverDistance,
                     0.1f, 0.0f, 10.0f);

    // Bursts
    if (ImGui::TreeNode("Bursts")) {
      for (size_t i = 0; i < effect->emission.bursts.size(); ++i) {
        auto &burst = effect->emission.bursts[i];
        ImGui::PushID(static_cast<int>(i));
        ImGui::DragFloat("Time", &burst.time, 0.1f, 0.0f,
                         effect->main.duration);
        ImGui::DragInt("Count", (int *)&burst.count, 1, 1, 1000);
        ImGui::DragInt("Cycles", (int *)&burst.cycles, 1, 1, 100);
        ImGui::DragFloat("Interval", &burst.interval, 0.1f, 0.1f, 10.0f);
        ImGui::DragFloat("Probability", &burst.probability, 0.1f, 0.0f, 1.0f);

        if (ImGui::Button("Remove")) {
          effect->emission.bursts.erase(effect->emission.bursts.begin() + i);
          ImGui::PopID();
          break;
        }
        ImGui::PopID();
      }

      if (ImGui::Button("Add Burst")) {
        effect->emission.bursts.push_back({});
      }
      ImGui::TreePop();
    }
  }

  if (ImGui::CollapsingHeader("Shape Module")) {
    ImGui::Checkbox("Enabled", &effect->shape.enabled);

    const char *shapeTypes[] = {"Sphere", "Cone", "Box", "Mesh"};
    ImGui::Combo("Shape Type", &effect->shape.shapeType, shapeTypes,
                 IM_ARRAYSIZE(shapeTypes));

    if (effect->shape.shapeType == 0) { // Sphere
      ImGui::DragFloat("Radius", &effect->shape.radius, 0.1f, 0.1f, 100.0f);
    } else if (effect->shape.shapeType == 1) { // Cone
      ImGui::DragFloat("Angle", &effect->shape.angle, 1.0f, 0.0f, 90.0f);
      ImGui::DragFloat("Radius", &effect->shape.radius, 0.1f, 0.1f, 100.0f);
    } else if (effect->shape.shapeType == 2) { // Box
      ImGui::DragFloat3("Box Size", &effect->shape.boxSize[0], 0.1f, 0.1f,
                        100.0f);
    }

    ImGui::DragFloat("Arc", &effect->shape.arc, 1.0f, 0.0f, 360.0f);
    ImGui::DragFloat("Random Direction", &effect->shape.randomDirection, 0.1f,
                     0.0f, 1.0f);
    ImGui::Checkbox("Align To Direction", &effect->shape.alignToDirection);
  }

  if (ImGui::CollapsingHeader("Velocity Over Lifetime")) {
    ImGui::Checkbox("Enabled##Velocity", &effect->velocityOverLifetime.enabled);
    ImGui::DragFloat3("Linear Velocity",
                      &effect->velocityOverLifetime.linear[0], 0.1f, -10.0f,
                      10.0f);
    ImGui::DragFloat3("Orbital Velocity",
                      &effect->velocityOverLifetime.orbital[0], 0.1f, -10.0f,
                      10.0f);
    ImGui::DragFloat("Speed Modifier",
                     &effect->velocityOverLifetime.speedModifier, 0.1f, 0.1f,
                     10.0f);
  }

  if (ImGui::CollapsingHeader("Force Over Lifetime")) {
    ImGui::Checkbox("Enabled##Force", &effect->forceOverLifetime.enabled);
    ImGui::DragFloat3("Force", &effect->forceOverLifetime.force[0], 0.1f,
                      -10.0f, 10.0f);
    ImGui::Checkbox("Randomize", &effect->forceOverLifetime.randomize);
    if (effect->forceOverLifetime.randomize) {
      ImGui::DragFloat3("Random Force",
                        &effect->forceOverLifetime.randomForce[0], 0.1f, 0.0f,
                        10.0f);
    }
  }

  if (ImGui::CollapsingHeader("Limit Velocity Over Lifetime")) {
    ImGui::Checkbox("Enabled##LimitVelocity",
                    &effect->limitVelocityOverLifetime.enabled);
    ImGui::DragFloat("Speed Limit", &effect->limitVelocityOverLifetime.limit,
                     0.1f, 0.1f, 100.0f);
    ImGui::DragFloat("Dampen", &effect->limitVelocityOverLifetime.dampen, 0.1f,
                     0.0f, 1.0f);
    ImGui::Checkbox("Separate Axes",
                    &effect->limitVelocityOverLifetime.separateAxes);
    if (effect->limitVelocityOverLifetime.separateAxes) {
      ImGui::DragFloat3("Limit XYZ",
                        &effect->limitVelocityOverLifetime.limitXYZ[0], 0.1f,
                        0.1f, 100.0f);
    }
  }

  if (ImGui::CollapsingHeader("Color Over Lifetime")) {
    ImGui::Checkbox("Enabled##Color", &effect->colorOverLifetime.enabled);

    // Gradient editor (упрощенный)
    if (ImGui::TreeNode("Gradient")) {
      for (size_t i = 0; i < effect->colorOverLifetime.gradient.size(); ++i) {
        auto &key = effect->colorOverLifetime.gradient[i];
        ImGui::PushID(static_cast<int>(i));
        ImGui::DragFloat("Time", &key.time, 0.01f, 0.0f, 1.0f);
        ImGui::ColorEdit4("Color", &key.color[0]);

        if (ImGui::Button("Remove")) {
          effect->colorOverLifetime.gradient.erase(
              effect->colorOverLifetime.gradient.begin() + i);
          ImGui::PopID();
          break;
        }
        ImGui::PopID();
      }

      if (ImGui::Button("Add Key")) {
        effect->colorOverLifetime.gradient.push_back({0.0f, glm::vec4(1.0f)});
      }
      ImGui::TreePop();
    }
  }

  if (ImGui::CollapsingHeader("Size Over Lifetime")) {
    ImGui::Checkbox("Enabled##Size", &effect->sizeOverLifetime.enabled);

    if (ImGui::TreeNode("Size Curve")) {
      for (size_t i = 0; i < effect->sizeOverLifetime.curve.size(); ++i) {
        auto &key = effect->sizeOverLifetime.curve[i];
        ImGui::PushID(static_cast<int>(i));
        ImGui::DragFloat("Time", &key.time, 0.01f, 0.0f, 1.0f);
        ImGui::DragFloat3("Size", &key.size[0], 0.1f, 0.0f, 10.0f);

        if (ImGui::Button("Remove")) {
          effect->sizeOverLifetime.curve.erase(
              effect->sizeOverLifetime.curve.begin() + i);
          ImGui::PopID();
          break;
        }
        ImGui::PopID();
      }

      if (ImGui::Button("Add Key")) {
        effect->sizeOverLifetime.curve.push_back({0.0f, glm::vec3(1.0f)});
      }
      ImGui::TreePop();
    }
  }

  if (ImGui::CollapsingHeader("Rotation Over Lifetime")) {
    ImGui::Checkbox("Enabled##Rotation", &effect->rotationOverLifetime.enabled);
    ImGui::DragFloat3("Angular Velocity",
                      &effect->rotationOverLifetime.angularVelocity[0], 1.0f,
                      -360.0f, 360.0f);
    ImGui::Checkbox("Separate Axes##Rotation",
                    &effect->rotationOverLifetime.separateAxes);
  }

  if (ImGui::CollapsingHeader("Renderer Module")) {
    // Texture path
    static char texturePath[256] = "";
    ImGui::InputText("Texture Path", texturePath, sizeof(texturePath));
    if (ImGui::Button("Load Texture")) {
      effect->renderer.texturePath = texturePath;
      m_selectedSystem->LoadTexture(texturePath);
    }

    const char *renderModes[] = {"Billboard", "Stretched Billboard",
                                 "Horizontal Billboard", "Vertical Billboard",
                                 "Mesh"};
    ImGui::Combo("Render Mode", &effect->renderer.renderMode, renderModes,
                 IM_ARRAYSIZE(renderModes));

    ImGui::DragFloat3("Normal Direction", &effect->renderer.normalDirection[0],
                      0.1f, -1.0f, 1.0f);
    ImGui::ColorEdit4("Material Color", &effect->renderer.materialColor[0]);
    ImGui::Checkbox("Cast Shadows", &effect->renderer.castShadows);
    ImGui::Checkbox("Receive Shadows", &effect->renderer.receiveShadows);
  }

  // Кнопки для применения изменений
  ImGui::Separator();
  if (ImGui::Button("Apply Changes")) {
    m_selectedSystem->SetEffect(effect);
  }
  ImGui::SameLine();
  if (ImGui::Button("Reset")) {
    effect = m_selectedSystem->GetEffect();
  }

  ImGui::Separator();
  ImGui::Text("Serialization:");

  static char savePath[256] = "res/effects/effect.yaml";
  ImGui::InputText("Save Path", savePath, sizeof(savePath));

  if (ImGui::Button("Save Effect")) {
    effect->SaveToFile(savePath);
  }

  ImGui::SameLine();

  static char loadPath[256] = "res/effects/effect.yaml";
  ImGui::InputText("Load Path", loadPath, sizeof(loadPath));

  if (ImGui::Button("Load Effect")) {
    effect->LoadFromFile(loadPath);
    m_selectedSystem->SetEffect(effect); // Применяем загруженный эффект
  }

  // Кнопки для применения изменений
  ImGui::Separator();
  if (ImGui::Button("Apply Changes")) {
    m_selectedSystem->SetEffect(effect);
  }
  ImGui::SameLine();
  if (ImGui::Button("Reset")) {
    effect = m_selectedSystem->GetEffect();
  }
}

void ParticleSystemPanel::DrawParticleSystemList(EditorContext &ctx) {
  auto *scene = ctx.m_scene;
  if (!scene) {
    ImGui::Text("No scene loaded");
    return;
  }

  const auto &systems = scene->GetParticleSystems();

  if (systems.empty()) {
    ImGui::Text("No particle systems");
    return;
  }

  ImGui::Text("Particle Systems:");
  ImGui::Separator();

  for (size_t i = 0; i < systems.size(); ++i) {
    auto &system = systems[i];
    bool isSelected = (system == m_selectedSystem);

    std::string label = "ParticleSystem " + std::to_string(i);
    if (system->IsPlaying()) {
      label += " ▶";
    } else {
      label += " ■";
    }

    if (ImGui::Selectable(label.c_str(), isSelected)) {
      m_selectedSystem = system;
    }

    // Кнопка удаления
    ImGui::SameLine();
    ImGui::PushID(static_cast<int>(i));
    if (ImGui::SmallButton("X")) {
      if (m_selectedSystem == system) {
        m_selectedSystem.reset();
      }
      scene->RemoveParticleSystem(system);
      ImGui::PopID();
      break;
    }
    ImGui::PopID();
  }
}

void ParticleSystemPanel::DrawParticleSystemControls(EditorContext &ctx) {
  ImGui::Separator();
  ImGui::Text("Selected Particle System");
  ImGui::Separator();

  // Кнопки управления
  if (ImGui::Button("Play")) {
    m_selectedSystem->Play();
  }
  ImGui::SameLine();
  if (ImGui::Button("Stop")) {
    m_selectedSystem->Stop();
  }
  ImGui::SameLine();
  if (ImGui::Button("Restart")) {
    m_selectedSystem->Stop();
    m_selectedSystem->Play();
  }

  // Статус
  ImGui::Text("Status: %s",
              m_selectedSystem->IsPlaying() ? "Playing" : "Stopped");
  ImGui::Text("Particles: %u", m_selectedSystem->GetParticleCount());

  // Быстрые пресеты
  ImGui::Separator();
  ImGui::Text("Quick Presets:");

  if (ImGui::Button("Fire Preset")) {
    auto effect = engine::ParticleEffect::CreateFire();
    m_selectedSystem->SetEffect(effect);
  }
  ImGui::SameLine();
  if (ImGui::Button("Smoke Preset")) {
    auto effect = engine::ParticleEffect::CreateSmoke();
    m_selectedSystem->SetEffect(effect);
  }
  ImGui::SameLine();
  if (ImGui::Button("Magic Preset")) {
    auto effect = engine::ParticleEffect::CreateMagic();
    m_selectedSystem->SetEffect(effect);
  }
  ImGui::SameLine();
}

void ParticleSystemPanel::DrawAddParticleSystemMenu(EditorContext &ctx) {
  ImGui::Text("Add Particle System:");
  ImGui::Separator();

  if (ImGui::MenuItem("Fire")) {
    auto effect = engine::ParticleEffect::CreateFire();
    auto system = std::make_shared<engine::ParticleSystem>();
    system->SetFireEffect();
    system->Play();

    if (ctx.m_scene) {
      ctx.m_scene->AddParticleSystem(system);
      m_selectedSystem = system; // Автоматически выбираем новую систему
    }
  }

  if (ImGui::MenuItem("Smoke")) {
    auto effect = engine::ParticleEffect::CreateSmoke();
    auto system = std::make_shared<engine::ParticleSystem>();
    system->SetEffect(effect);
    system->Play();

    if (ctx.m_scene) {
      ctx.m_scene->AddParticleSystem(system);
      m_selectedSystem = system;
    }
  }

  if (ImGui::MenuItem("Magic")) {
    auto effect = engine::ParticleEffect::CreateMagic();
    auto system = std::make_shared<engine::ParticleSystem>();
    system->SetEffect(effect);
    system->Play();

    if (ctx.m_scene) {
      ctx.m_scene->AddParticleSystem(system);
      m_selectedSystem = system;
    }
  }

  if (ImGui::MenuItem("Custom (Empty)")) {
    auto effect = engine::ParticleEffect::CreateDefault();
    auto system = std::make_shared<engine::ParticleSystem>();
    system->SetEffect(effect);

    if (ctx.m_scene) {
      ctx.m_scene->AddParticleSystem(system);
      m_selectedSystem = system;
    }
  }
}

} // namespace ui

===== src/ui/panels/PrefabPreviewRenderer.cc =====
#include "PrefabPreviewRenderer.h"
#include "graphics/Renderer.h"
#include <glm/gtc/matrix_transform.hpp>
#include <iostream>
namespace ui {

PrefabPreviewRenderer::PrefabPreviewRenderer(int w, int h)
    : m_width(w), m_height(h) {
  SetupFBO();
  UpdateCamera();
}

PrefabPreviewRenderer::~PrefabPreviewRenderer() {
  glDeleteFramebuffers(1, &m_fbo);
  glDeleteTextures(1, &m_texture);
  glDeleteRenderbuffers(1, &m_rbo);
}

void PrefabPreviewRenderer::SetupFBO() {
  glGenFramebuffers(1, &m_fbo);
  glBindFramebuffer(GL_FRAMEBUFFER, m_fbo);

  glGenTextures(1, &m_texture);
  glBindTexture(GL_TEXTURE_2D, m_texture);
  glTexImage2D(GL_TEXTURE_2D, 0, GL_RGB, m_width, m_height, 0, GL_RGB,
               GL_UNSIGNED_BYTE, nullptr);
  glTexParameteri(GL_TEXTURE_2D, GL_TEXTURE_MIN_FILTER, GL_LINEAR);
  glTexParameteri(GL_TEXTURE_2D, GL_TEXTURE_MAG_FILTER, GL_LINEAR);
  glFramebufferTexture2D(GL_FRAMEBUFFER, GL_COLOR_ATTACHMENT0, GL_TEXTURE_2D,
                         m_texture, 0);

  glGenRenderbuffers(1, &m_rbo);
  glBindRenderbuffer(GL_RENDERBUFFER, m_rbo);
  glRenderbufferStorage(GL_RENDERBUFFER, GL_DEPTH24_STENCIL8, m_width,
                        m_height);
  glFramebufferRenderbuffer(GL_FRAMEBUFFER, GL_DEPTH_STENCIL_ATTACHMENT,
                            GL_RENDERBUFFER, m_rbo);

  if (glCheckFramebufferStatus(GL_FRAMEBUFFER) != GL_FRAMEBUFFER_COMPLETE)
    std::cerr << "FBO incomplete!\n";

  glBindFramebuffer(GL_FRAMEBUFFER, 0);
}

void PrefabPreviewRenderer::Resize(int w, int h) {
  if (w == m_width && h == m_height)
    return;
  m_width = w;
  m_height = h;
  glDeleteTextures(1, &m_texture);
  glDeleteRenderbuffers(1, &m_rbo);
  SetupFBO();
}

void PrefabPreviewRenderer::UpdateCamera() {
  glm::vec3 front(cos(glm::radians(m_yaw)) * cos(glm::radians(m_pitch)),
                  sin(glm::radians(m_pitch)),
                  sin(glm::radians(m_yaw)) * cos(glm::radians(m_pitch)));
  front = glm::normalize(front);
  glm::vec3 pos = m_target - front * m_distance;

  m_view = glm::lookAt(pos, m_target, glm::vec3(0, 1, 0));
  m_proj = glm::perspective(glm::radians(45.0f), (float)m_width / m_height,
                            0.1f, 100.0f);
}

void PrefabPreviewRenderer::Rotate(float dyaw, float dpitch) {
  m_yaw += dyaw;
  m_pitch = glm::clamp(m_pitch + dpitch, -89.0f, 89.0f);
  UpdateCamera();
}

void PrefabPreviewRenderer::Zoom(float delta) {
  m_distance = glm::clamp(m_distance - delta, m_minDist, m_maxDist);
  UpdateCamera();
}

void PrefabPreviewRenderer::ResetView() {
  m_yaw = -90.0f;
  m_pitch = 0.0f;
  m_distance = 10.0f;
  UpdateCamera();
}

void PrefabPreviewRenderer::Render(const engine::data::AgentPrefab &P) {
  if (!m_model || m_model->GetPath() != P.m_modelPath) {
    m_model = std::make_shared<engine::Model>(P.m_modelPath);
    m_material = engine::Material::Create();
  }

  glBindFramebuffer(GL_FRAMEBUFFER, m_fbo);
  glViewport(0, 0, m_width, m_height);
  glClearColor(0.2f, 0.2f, 0.2f, 1.0f);
  glClear(GL_COLOR_BUFFER_BIT | GL_DEPTH_BUFFER_BIT);
  glEnable(GL_DEPTH_TEST);

  engine::Renderer::Instance().BeginScene(m_view, m_proj);
  engine::Renderer::Instance().DrawModelWithMaterial(
      *m_model, glm::vec3(0, 0, -10), P.m_modelScale, m_target, m_material);

  // Bounds wireframe
  glPolygonMode(GL_FRONT_AND_BACK, GL_LINE);
  glm::vec3 center = (P.m_bounds_min + P.m_bounds_max) * 0.5f;
  center.z -= 10;
  glm::vec3 size = P.m_bounds_max - P.m_bounds_min;
  engine::Renderer::Instance().DrawCube(center, glm::vec3(1, 0, 0), size);
  glPolygonMode(GL_FRONT_AND_BACK, GL_FILL);

  engine::Renderer::Instance().EndScene();
  glBindFramebuffer(GL_FRAMEBUFFER, 0);
}

} // namespace ui

===== src/ui/panels/FireParamsPanel.h =====

    // Загружаем preview для всех текстур

===== src/ui/panels/FireDebugPanel.cc =====
// ui/panels/FireDebugPanel.cc
#include "FireDebugPanel.h"

#include <GLFW/glfw3.h>
#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>
#include <imgui.h>

#include "core/Input.h"
#include "core/scene/Scene.h"
#include "math/ScreenRay.h"

namespace ui {

void FireDebugPanel::Draw(EditorContext &ctx) {
  // 0) Всегда слушаем жест Ctrl+ЛКМ — он может открыть панель
  HandlePick(ctx);

  // 1) Если панель скрыта — даже не создаём ImGui-окно
  if (!m_visible) {
    return;
  }

  // 2) Окно с крестиком (ImGui меняет m_visible при нажатии X)
  if (!ImGui::Begin(Id(), &m_visible)) {
    ImGui::End();
    if (!m_visible) {
      m_hasSelection = false;
      m_selectedGpuIndex = -1;
    }
    return;
  }

  if (!ctx.m_scene) {
    ImGui::TextUnformatted("No scene.");
    ImGui::End();
    return;
  }

  // Пока окно открыто, можно пере-выбирать другого агента тем же жестом
  HandlePick(ctx);

  // Подтягиваем актуальное состояние с GPU для выбранного агента
  if (m_hasSelection) {
    auto agents = ctx.m_scene->LoadAgentsFromGPU();
    if (m_selectedGpuIndex >= 0 &&
        m_selectedGpuIndex < static_cast<int>(agents.size())) {
      m_agentSnapshot = agents[m_selectedGpuIndex];
    }
  }

  if (!m_hasSelection) {
    ImGui::TextUnformatted("Наведитесь курсором на объект в сцене\n"
                           "и нажмите Ctrl+ЛКМ для инспекции огня.");
  } else {
    DrawAgentInfo();
  }

  ImGui::End();

  // Если пользователь закрыл окно крестиком — очищаем выбор
  if (!m_visible) {
    m_hasSelection = false;
    m_selectedGpuIndex = -1;
  }
}

void FireDebugPanel::HandlePick(EditorContext &ctx) {
  using engine::InputSystem;
  using engine::KeyCode;

  // 0) если мышь захвачена UI — не трогаем
  if (InputSystem::Instance().UIMouseCaptured()) {
    return;
  }
  if (!ctx.m_window || !ctx.m_scene) {
    return;
  }

  const bool CTRL_DOWN =
      glfwGetKey(ctx.m_window, GLFW_KEY_LEFT_CONTROL) == GLFW_PRESS ||
      glfwGetKey(ctx.m_window, GLFW_KEY_RIGHT_CONTROL) == GLFW_PRESS;

  const bool LMB_PRESSED = InputSystem::Instance().GetMouseButton(
      engine::MouseButton::Left, engine::InputState::Pressed);

  if (!CTRL_DOWN || !LMB_PRESSED)
    return;

  // 2) Позиция курсора в окне (как в PlaceOnGroundTool)
  double mx = 0.0;
  double my = 0.0;
  glfwGetCursorPos(ctx.m_window, &mx, &my);

  // 3) окно -> логические координаты вьюпорта
  const glm::vec2 VP_LOGICAL = ctx.WindowToViewportLogical(
      static_cast<float>(mx), static_cast<float>(my));

  const bool INSIDE = (VP_LOGICAL.x >= 0.F && VP_LOGICAL.y >= 0.F &&
                       VP_LOGICAL.x < static_cast<float>(ctx.vpW) &&
                       VP_LOGICAL.y < static_cast<float>(ctx.vpH));

  if (!INSIDE)
    return;

  // 4) логические -> framebuffer px (ровно как в
  // PlaceOnGroundTool::ToFramebufferPx)
  const glm::vec2 px = {
      VP_LOGICAL.x * ctx.m_vpScaleX,
      VP_LOGICAL.y * ctx.m_vpScaleY,
  };

  // 5) луч из пикселя FB
  glm::vec3 ro{};
  glm::vec3 rd{};
  const auto FB_W = static_cast<float>(ctx.m_viewportW);
  const auto FB_H = static_cast<float>(ctx.m_viewportH);
  math::ScreenPointToRay(px.x, px.y, FB_W, FB_H, ctx.m_proj, ctx.m_view, ro,
                         rd);

  // 6) пересечение с плоскостью Y=0
  glm::vec3 hit{};
  if (!math::IntersectRayPlaneY0(ro, rd, hit)) {
    return;
  }

  // 7) если есть террейн — подтягиваем Y по высоте
  if (auto *T = ctx.m_scene->GetTerrain()) {
    hit.y = T->GetHeightAt(hit.x, hit.z);
  }

  // 8) ищем ближайший объект по XZ (радиус ~2 метра)
  float bestDist2 = 4.0f; // 2^2
  int bestGpuIndex = -1;

  auto view = ctx.m_scene->GetSceneObjectsView();
  for (auto [entity, transform, render, link] : view.each()) {
    const float DX = transform.position.x - hit.x;
    const float DZ = transform.position.z - hit.z;
    const float DIST2 = (DX * DX) + (DZ * DZ);
    if (DIST2 < bestDist2) {
      bestDist2 = DIST2;
      bestGpuIndex = link.gpu_index;
    }
  }

  if (bestGpuIndex < 0)
    return;

  // 9) Загружаем агентов с GPU и берём нужного
  auto agents = ctx.m_scene->LoadAgentsFromGPU();
  if (bestGpuIndex >= 0 && bestGpuIndex < static_cast<int>(agents.size())) {
    m_agentSnapshot = agents[bestGpuIndex];
    m_selectedGpuIndex = bestGpuIndex;
    m_hasSelection = true;
    m_pointIndex = 0;

    m_visible = true;
  }
}

void FireDebugPanel::DrawAgentInfo() {
  const Agent &a = m_agentSnapshot;
  const FireCollider &c = a.fire_collider;

  ImGui::SeparatorText("Selected Agent");

  ImGui::Text("GPU index: %d", m_selectedGpuIndex);
  ImGui::Text("Type: %d", static_cast<int>(a.type));
  ImGui::Text("Object id: %d", a.object_id);
  ImGui::Text("Overall intensity: %.3f", a.getOverallIntensity());
  ImGui::Text("Collider points: %d", c.point_count);
  ImGui::Separator();

  ImGui::Text("Material:");
  ImGui::Text("  Conductivity: %.3f", c.conductivity);
  ImGui::Text("  Ignition temp: %.1f", c.ignition_temp);

  // Соберём агрегатные значения по температурам и т.п.
  float minT = std::numeric_limits<float>::max();
  float maxT = -std::numeric_limits<float>::max();
  float sumT = 0.f;

  float minI = std::numeric_limits<float>::max();
  float maxI = -std::numeric_limits<float>::max();
  float sumI = 0.f;

  float minFuel = std::numeric_limits<float>::max();
  float maxFuel = -std::numeric_limits<float>::max();
  float sumFuel = 0.f;

  float minM = std::numeric_limits<float>::max();
  float maxM = -std::numeric_limits<float>::max();
  float sumM = 0.f;

  const int N = c.point_count;
  for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
    const float T = c.temperatures[i];
    const float I = c.intensities[i];
    const float F = c.fuels[i];
    const float M = c.moisture[i];

    minT = std::min(minT, T);
    maxT = std::max(maxT, T);
    sumT += T;

    minI = std::min(minI, I);
    maxI = std::max(maxI, I);
    sumI += I;

    minFuel = std::min(minFuel, F);
    maxFuel = std::max(maxFuel, F);
    sumFuel += F;

    minM = std::min(minM, M);
    maxM = std::max(maxM, M);
    sumM += M;
  }

  const float avgT = (N > 0) ? sumT / N : 0.f;
  const float avgI = (N > 0) ? sumI / N : 0.f;
  const float avgFuel = (N > 0) ? sumFuel / N : 0.f;
  const float avgM = (N > 0) ? sumM / N : 0.f;

  ImGui::SeparatorText("Aggregate");
  ImGui::Text("Temperature:   min %.1f  avg %.1f  max %.1f  (threshold: %.1f)",
              minT, avgT, maxT, c.ignition_temp);
  ImGui::Text("Intensity:     min %.3f  avg %.3f  max %.3f", minI, avgI, maxI);
  ImGui::Text("Fuel:          min %.1f  avg %.1f  max %.1f", minFuel, avgFuel,
              maxFuel);
  ImGui::Text("Moisture:      min %.3f  avg %.3f  max %.3f", minM, avgM, maxM);

  if (maxT < c.ignition_temp) {
    ImGui::TextColored(ImVec4(1.f, 0.6f, 0.2f, 1.f),
                       "Not enough temperature for ignition.\n"
                       "Max T = %.1f, need >= %.1f (delta %.1f).",
                       maxT, c.ignition_temp, c.ignition_temp - maxT);
  } else {
    ImGui::TextColored(ImVec4(0.3f, 1.f, 0.3f, 1.f),
                       "Ignition threshold reached (max T = %.1f, thr = %.1f).",
                       maxT, c.ignition_temp);
  }

  ImGui::SeparatorText("Point details");

  if (N <= 0) {
    ImGui::TextUnformatted("No fire points.");
    return;
  }

  if (m_pointIndex >= N) {
    m_pointIndex = N - 1;
  }
  m_pointIndex = std::max(m_pointIndex, 0);

  ImGui::SliderInt("Point", &m_pointIndex, 0, N - 1);

  const int i = m_pointIndex;
  ImGui::Text("Point %d:", i);
  ImGui::Text("  Pos:      (%.2f, %.2f, %.2f)", c.heat_points[i].x,
              c.heat_points[i].y, c.heat_points[i].z);
  ImGui::Text("  Temp:     %.2f", c.temperatures[i]);
  ImGui::Text("  Intensity %.3f", c.intensities[i]);
  ImGui::Text("  Fuel:     %.1f", c.fuels[i]);
  ImGui::Text("  Moisture: %.3f", c.moisture[i]);
}

} // namespace ui



===== src/ui/backends/ImGuiBackend.cc =====
#include "ui/backends/ImGuiBackend.h"
#include <imgui.h>

// clang-format off
// NOLINTBEGIN(llvm-include-order)
#ifndef GLFW_INCLUDE_NONE
#define GLFW_INCLUDE_NONE
#endif
#include <glad/glad.h>
#include <GLFW/glfw3.h>
// NOLINTEND(llvm-include-order)
// clang-format on

#include <backends/imgui_impl_glfw.h>
#include <backends/imgui_impl_opengl3.h>

namespace {
constexpr ImGuiConfigFlags Or(ImGuiConfigFlags first, ImGuiConfigFlags second) {
  return static_cast<ImGuiConfigFlags>(static_cast<unsigned>(first) |
                                       static_cast<unsigned>(second));
}
} // namespace

void ui::ImGuiBackend::Init(GLFWwindow *ui_window, const char *glsl) {
  IMGUI_CHECKVERSION();
  ImGui::CreateContext();

  ImGuiIO &io = ImGui::GetIO();
  io.ConfigFlags = Or(io.ConfigFlags, ImGuiConfigFlags_DockingEnable);
  io.ConfigFlags = Or(io.ConfigFlags, ImGuiConfigFlags_NavEnableKeyboard);

  ImGui::StyleColorsDark();

  ImGui_ImplGlfw_InitForOpenGL(ui_window, /*install_callbacks=*/true);
  ImGui_ImplOpenGL3_Init(glsl);
}

void ui::ImGuiBackend::Shutdown() {
  ImGui_ImplOpenGL3_Shutdown();
  ImGui_ImplGlfw_Shutdown();
  ImGui::DestroyContext();
}

void ui::ImGuiBackend::BeginFrame() {
  ImGui_ImplOpenGL3_NewFrame();
  ImGui_ImplGlfw_NewFrame();
  ImGui::NewFrame();
}

void ui::ImGuiBackend::EndFrame() {
  ImGui::Render();
  ImGui_ImplOpenGL3_RenderDrawData(ImGui::GetDrawData());
}

void ui::ImGuiBackend::DrawDockspace()
{
    ImGuiIO& io = ImGui::GetIO();
    ImGuiViewport* viewport = ImGui::GetMainViewport();

    // Хост-окно на весь экран без фона
    ImGui::SetNextWindowPos(viewport->Pos);
    ImGui::SetNextWindowSize(viewport->Size);
    ImGui::SetNextWindowViewport(viewport->ID);

    ImGuiWindowFlags host_flags =
        ImGuiWindowFlags_NoDocking |
        ImGuiWindowFlags_NoTitleBar |
        ImGuiWindowFlags_NoCollapse |
        ImGuiWindowFlags_NoResize |
        ImGuiWindowFlags_NoMove |
        ImGuiWindowFlags_NoBringToFrontOnFocus |
        ImGuiWindowFlags_NoNavFocus |
        ImGuiWindowFlags_NoBackground; // ← важное: нет фона, сцена видна

    ImGui::PushStyleVar(ImGuiStyleVar_WindowRounding, 0.0f);
    ImGui::PushStyleVar(ImGuiStyleVar_WindowBorderSize, 0.0f);

    ImGui::Begin("##DockSpaceHost", nullptr, host_flags);
    ImGui::PopStyleVar(2);

    // Сам докспейс — с Passthru в центре
    ImGuiID dockspace_id = ImGui::GetID("MainDockSpace");
    ImGuiDockNodeFlags dock_flags = ImGuiDockNodeFlags_PassthruCentralNode; // ← оставляем
    ImGui::DockSpace(dockspace_id, ImVec2(0.0f, 0.0f), dock_flags);

    ImGui::End();
}


namespace ui {
void ImGuiBackend::SaveLayout(const char* path) {
  ImGui::SaveIniSettingsToDisk(path);
}
void ImGuiBackend::LoadLayout(const char* path) {
  ImGui::LoadIniSettingsFromDisk(path);
}
} // namespace ui

class ImGuiBackend final : public IGuiBackend {

  void Shutdown() override;

  void EndFrame() override;

  void LoadLayout(const char* path) override;

===== src/app/EditorExtension.cc =====
// src/editor/EditorExtension.cpp
#include "app/EditorExtension.h"
#include <GLFW/glfw3.h>
#include "core/Application.h"
#include "core/Input.h"
#include "core/scene/Scene.h"
#include "imgui.h"
#include "ui/Editor.h"
#include "ui/EditorContext.h"
#include "ui/backends/ImGuiBackend.h"
#include "ui/modules/BasicToolsModule.h"

EditorExtension::~EditorExtension() noexcept = default;

void EditorExtension::OnInit(engine::Application& app) {
  m_imgui = std::make_unique<ui::ImGuiBackend>();
  m_imgui->Init(static_cast<GLFWwindow*>(app.GetNativeWindow()), "#version 330");

  m_editor = std::make_unique<ui::Editor>();
  m_editor->RegisterModule(std::make_unique<ui::BasicToolsModule>());
    ui::EditorContext ec{};
  ec.m_app    = &app;
  ec.m_window = app.GetNativeWindow();
  ec.m_scene  = &app.GetScene();
  m_editor->SetActiveTool(ui::ToolType::PLACE_ON_GROUND, ec);
}

void EditorExtension::OnBeginUI(engine::Application& /*app*/, ui::EditorContext& ctx) {
  // 1) начинаем кадр ImGui
  m_imgui->BeginFrame();
  m_imgui->DrawDockspace();

  // 2) Получаем актуальный viewport из ImGui
  ImGuiViewport* vp = ImGui::GetMainViewport();
  ctx.vpX = static_cast<int>(vp->WorkPos.x);
  ctx.vpY = static_cast<int>(vp->WorkPos.y);
  ctx.vpW = static_cast<int>(vp->WorkSize.x);
  ctx.vpH = static_cast<int>(vp->WorkSize.y);

  // 3) HiDPI scale
  int fbw = 0, fbh = 0, ww = 0, wh = 0;
  glfwGetFramebufferSize(ctx.m_window, &fbw, &fbh);
  glfwGetWindowSize(ctx.m_window, &ww, &wh);
  ctx.m_vpScaleX = (ww > 0) ? static_cast<float>(fbw) / static_cast<float>(ww) : 1.f;
  ctx.m_vpScaleY = (wh > 0) ? static_cast<float>(fbh) / static_cast<float>(wh) : 1.f;

  // 4) Размеры вьюпорта для проекций (в framebuffer-пикселях)
  ctx.m_viewportW = static_cast<int>(ctx.vpW * ctx.m_vpScaleX);
  ctx.m_viewportH = static_cast<int>(ctx.vpH * ctx.m_vpScaleY);

  // 5) Обновляем матрицы из сцены с ПРАВИЛЬНЫМИ размерами
  ctx.m_view = ctx.m_scene->GetViewMatrix();
  ctx.m_proj = ctx.m_scene->GetProjMatrix(ctx.m_viewportW, ctx.m_viewportH);

  // 6) Сообщаем сцене актуальные параметры viewport
  ctx.m_scene->SetViewportRect(ctx.vpX, ctx.vpY, ctx.vpW, ctx.vpH);
  ctx.m_scene->SetViewportScale(ctx.m_vpScaleX, ctx.m_vpScaleY);

  // 7) Рендер UI редактора
  m_editor->DrawUI(ctx);
  
  // 8) Обновляем состояние захвата ввода
  const ImGuiIO& io = ImGui::GetIO();
  engine::InputSystem::Instance().SetUICapture(io.WantCaptureMouse, io.WantCaptureKeyboard);
  
  // 9) Обновляем активные инструменты
  m_editor->TickTools(ctx);
}


void EditorExtension::OnEndUI(engine::Application&, ui::EditorContext&) {
  // 3) завершаем кадр ImGui (Render + RenderDrawData + платформенные окна)
  m_imgui->EndFrame();
}

void EditorExtension::OnShutdown(engine::Application&) {
  m_editor.reset();  // сначала редактор
  if (m_imgui) {
    m_imgui->Shutdown();
  }
  m_imgui.reset();
}



===== src/app/EditorExtension.h =====
// src/editor/EditorExtension.h
#pragma once
#include "app/IAppExtension.h"
#include "ui/Editor.h" 
#include "ui/EditorContext.h"
#include "ui/backends/ImGuiBackend.h" 
#include <memory>

class EditorExtension final : public IAppExtension {
public:

  void OnInit(engine::Application& app) override;
  void OnBeginUI(engine::Application& app, ui::EditorContext& ctx) override; 
  void OnEndUI(engine::Application& app, ui::EditorContext& ctx) override;   
  void OnShutdown(engine::Application& app) override;
  ~EditorExtension();

private:
  std::unique_ptr<ui::ImGuiBackend> m_imgui;
  std::unique_ptr<ui::Editor> m_editor;
};



===== src/app/IAppExtension.h =====
// src/app/IAppExtension.h
#pragma once
#include <memory>

namespace engine { class Application; }
namespace ui { struct EditorContext; }

struct IAppExtension {

  // вызываться один раз, когда окно и GL уже готовы
  virtual void OnInit(engine::Application& app) = 0;

  virtual void OnBeginUI(engine::Application& app, ui::EditorContext& ctx) = 0;
  virtual void OnEndUI(engine::Application& app, ui::EditorContext& ctx) = 0;

  virtual void OnShutdown(engine::Application& app) = 0;
};
